# Candidate Thought-Chains, Working Notes

## Purpose

People treat these messages as scripture. A chain layer that traces a single
idea across the archive, in the order the angels gave it, with each message
read in full, is both a study aid and a safeguard against the teachings being
quoted in fragments out of context.

This document is where we NOTICE candidate chains as we process each message.
We are not building chains yet. We are gathering evidence so that when the
archive is complete (messages uploaded through 2026), the eventual chains can
be built once, against the full set, with the entire arc visible.

## Design decisions already made (for future reference)

- **Unit of linking:** the whole message (message-level anchoring), never a
  sentence or fragment. This forces whole-context reading and discourages
  proof-texting.
- **Structure within a chain (revised 2026-07-06):** a chain is organized as
  role SECTIONS, in the order the argument requires (the registry's
  "argument it traces" column is the spine), with each section holding ALL
  its qualifying messages in chronological order by `date` received.
  Rationale: the angels rephrase important teachings because they are not
  always absorbed the first time, so restatements are part of the revelation
  pattern, not noise, strong witnesses are included, not discarded, and
  multiple messages may share a role. Within each section, one message is
  designated the ANCHOR (the developed statement); the others stand beneath
  it as witnesses, chronologically. Chronology thus still carries the
  angels' intended ordering, one level down: argument order lives in the
  sections, timing lives within each section.
- **A chain is a PATH, not a BAG.** A collection is unordered membership
  ("all the Two Paths messages"). A chain is an ordered argument that builds
  ("Hell is not eternal -> the dark spheres are a condition not a sentence ->
  any soul there can still turn to God -> therefore no soul is beyond
  redemption"). If a proposed chain is just "every message tagged X," it is a
  collection, not a chain. The anchor-per-section rule is the guard that
  inclusion never collapses a path back into a bag: a section may hold many
  witnesses, but the path is walked anchor to anchor.
- **One message belongs to many chains.** Chains will be defined in their own
  files (a future `chains/` directory, one file per chain, membership only).
  Chains are NEVER stored in a message's own front matter. In-message
  "next/previous in this chain" navigation will be GENERATED by a script that
  inverts the chain index, so bidirectional navigation costs nothing extra to
  maintain once the chain files exist.
- **Terminology:** prefer "paths" or "doors" over "chain" in any
  reader-facing language, to avoid collision with the existing "Chain of
  Light / Lattice of Light" terminology. ("Chain" is fine internally.)
- **Build timing:** construct the real chains AFTER the archive is caught up
  through 2026. Until then, only notice and log here.

## Link-role vocabulary (controlled, use these exact terms)

When logging a message against a candidate chain, tag what role it plays in
that chain's argument:

- **Foundation**, establishes the premise the rest of the thread depends on.
- **Elaboration**, deepens or extends a claim already made.
- **Objection-removed**, clears a contrary belief that blocks the next step.
- **Reframe**, recasts an idea in a new light or corrects a misunderstanding.
- **Testimony**, a spirit's first-person experience offered as evidence.
- **Chrysalis**, the transformational turn: once an objection is cleared or a
  premise laid, the chain opens a glimpse of what God's Divine Plan holds for
  the soul who walks this path. Forward-looking and revelatory. Distinct from
  Reframe (which changes how an idea is *seen*) and Testimony (which offers
  *evidence*); the Chrysalis shows the transformed vista the cleared ground
  now makes visible, the brick wall turned to glass.
- **Capstone**, the conclusion the thread builds toward. Distinct from
  Chrysalis: a Capstone *closes the argument*; a Chrysalis *opens a vista*. A
  chain may end on either, or carry both.

A message may carry different roles in different chains.

---

## How to read this file

This is the **Per-Message Log**. Each entry records which candidate chain(s) a
message joined and the role it played. Entries are in chronological order by
message date. Messages are not catalogued in date order, so a new entry is
inserted at its date's position rather than added at the end.

For the thread registry (the scannable index of every candidate chain), the
holding pen (candidates not yet minted), and open questions, see the companion
file **chains-threads.md**. Check the registry there before minting a new
thread, to avoid duplicates.

Link-role vocabulary (Foundation, Elaboration, Objection-removed, Reframe,
Testimony, Chrysalis, Capstone) is defined in chains-threads.md.

Every record carries a tier. A member is a role line. Everything below member
level is a NOTE whose parenthetical names its kind and, where it concerns a
thread, that thread's slug. There are six kinds:

> - NOTE (witness, `slug`): ...
> - NOTE (sighting, `slug`): ...
> - NOTE (considered and set aside, `slug`): ...
> - NOTE (build, `slug`): ...
> - NOTE (pattern, described and not penned): ...
> - NOTE (context): ...

Witness, sighting, and considered and set aside grade the message as evidence
for a thread. Build is addressed to whoever builds that thread: a tension, a
fence, a caveat about the text, an open anchor question, a pairing to read
together. A pattern is a recurring observation that is not yet a candidate
thread. Context is anything about the message that bears on no thread: medium
or spirit biography, family relationships, paired receptions, unidentified
spirits, practice files, collection questions, and subject-level connections.
A note that touches several threads is split into one NOTE per thread. A NOTE
with no parenthetical is invisible to any search that keys on its kind, so it
does not count as logged.

A record says what the message contains and why it earns its role. It records
nothing about this file's own history: no mint dates, no pen dates, no session
or reading dates, no curator attributions. This is the history of the messages,
not the history of the log. Where a standing ruling governs a decision, name
the ruling descriptively rather than by the date it was made.

Entries marked [back-search] were logged from a message's description rather
than from a full reading of the message. They can be any kind of message. Their
roles are PROVISIONAL and should be confirmed against the full text when chains
are built.

---

# Per-Message Log

> Chronological by message date; insert each new entry at its date's position.
> Format per entry:
> message_id, title, date
> - `slug` **[Role]** : note on why this message belongs and what it contributes
>
> One record per line. Records are never wrapped onto continuation lines, so a
> search for a slug returns the whole record and a diff shows which records
> changed.
>
> A message may carry more than one line for the same thread and role when
> each line covers a different passage. They count as one role for that
> message; when the chain is built, the passages are read in the order the
> lines give.
>
> [back-search] marks entries logged from a description rather than a full
> reading; their roles are provisional pending full-text confirmation.

---

### 1963-12-26-ds-mary | Jesus' Birth and Youth | 1963-12-26
- `who-jesus-was` **[Foundation]** : Mary corrects the record of her son's birth and nature as the one person present for it: "Most of what the New Testament says about me is untrue." There was no virgin birth. Jesus was "born exactly like other babies." Joseph was a young husband, not the aged figure of tradition. There were eight children in the house. From this she gives the thread its central claim: Jesus became the Messiah rather than being born one, because God's Love "permeated his soul... made him, in time, His real, only begotten son." She also reports that his own family thought him insane, corrects the idea that his death was a blood sacrifice, and mentions that his natural love absorbed the ordinary turn toward marriage, which the complete-soul teaching takes up (2019-01-27-af-jesus). -- Foundation-section member; the anchor is a build-time choice between this message, 2018-11-26-af-jesus, and 2019-01-27-af-jesus.
- `two-paths` **[Testimony]** : Two lives showing the difference between the loves, and showing that one can become the other. Of her son: "Jeshu's love for his family was a natural love, purified. It later became Divine through prayer." Of herself: her love for him "deepened into the Divine Love" only after his death, giving her what her natural love could not give her at the cross. The thread's argument, lived rather than taught.
- `religious-error` **[Elaboration]** : Mary corrects doctrine as someone who was there, evidence of a different kind from a reformer correcting doctrine he studied. The virgin birth narrative is untrue. The portrait of an elderly Joseph was invented. The crucifixion was "not as a blood sacrifice as most Gentiles believe" but the sacrifice of a life in carrying out a mission of preaching God's Love. That last correction sits directly against the thread's central claim about inherited error.
- NOTE (sighting, `divine-love-mediumship`): Mary communicates only with the approval of Jesus, and the message continues a line of instruction running from Padgett to Samuels. -- adjacency only; noted so the continuity is findable.
- NOTE (sighting, `prayer-soul-longing`): the Love came to Jesus as he was turned "to think of God and long for God's Presence; to pray."

### 2014-07-26-af-augustine | Free Will Makes for a Free Wheeling World, Nothing Is Guaranteed | 2014-07-26
- `free-will-and-gods-plan` **[Foundation]** : the unpredictability step argued from the spirit side. Augustine begins by refusing the premise that events express God's intention: "the events of the world do not unfold to the Will of God but to the will of man," so that "great good and great negativity can be in the world at the same time, because of this choice." Then the claim that gives the thread its shape: "the flow of the events in this world are not altogether predictable, are not altogether under the control of God. There is an element of chaos and changeability." The constraint is described as binding the angels too: "we cannot control the events of the world in the way that you would like us to... we are limited in our efforts as well because of the power of free will and the grave conditions that mankind has established in this world." He stops short of powerlessness, "This is not to say that we are powerless to influence man." Where the thread traces God orchestrating through circuitous routes, this says why the routes are circuitous, and that those working them are bound by the same law.
- `free-will-and-self-responsibility` **[Elaboration]** : the purpose behind the law of non-interference. Augustine gives the refusal and its reason in one breath: "We cannot interfere with your free will. We will not, for this is your precious life and it is for you to grow, to learn, to strengthen, to love, to understand the nature of life, the nature of Creation, the being that is your Creator and the being that is you, the created." The non-interference is pedagogical rather than merely legal, and responsibility follows from it: "it is the power of your free will, your choice, your efforts which will determine how you deal with these conditions, what is manifest, what unfolds." -- this rests on two sentences, so witness would be the modest reading. It is held as an Elaboration because it gives the purpose behind the law, and a thread built on a law is stronger for holding its reason. Demote if the roster prefers members at argument length.
- NOTE (witness, `earth-conditions-and-prayer`): the field described, with what meets it given as a standing relation rather than a daily practice. The conditions are man-made and pressing, "the conditions in the world bring strife, the conditions in the world are far from the conditions in the Celestial Heavens," and what turns them is the alliance itself: "in this alliance that you have with Heaven, with your Creator, with the Love flowing within your lives much of that darkness, much of that chaos, much of those conditions set about by humans, is turned to light and harmony within your lives." Augustine also bounds the field to mortal life: "When you pass into Spirit, these situations, this impinging darkness will not beset you and cause you strife. But until that day, you are mortal." -- the mortality boundary is worth weighing at build: if the darkness that presses on us stops at death, that changes what the daily prayer is defending against.
- NOTE (witness, `judgment-to-love`): the cost of judging falling on the one who judges, with self and others named in a single move, "The judgments of yourselves and of others are not required for this will bring darkness to you and disharmony within." -- the self-and-others pairing is also `self-love-foundation` territory, asserted here rather than argued.
- NOTE (witness, `provision-for-service`): provision stated plainly and twice, "You have all that you require to set forth upon your path," and at the close, "you will be given all that you require. Do not doubt this but rejoice in this," with "God will take care of the rest." Augustine is assuring individuals on a spiritual path rather than describing the supply of an undertaking. -- the fence: no mission, no work and nothing material is named, so this carries the provision language without the claim the thread is built on. Stronger than the opportunity-not-provision case fenced at 2016-09-13-af-goldie, and still short of it.
- NOTE (sighting, `angelic-accompaniment`): the accompaniment given in the attending spirit's own voice, "we will always be with you no matter the difficulties," and at the close, "Your teacher Augustine is always by your side." -- unambiguously the standing form rather than an angel dispatched on a request, which bears on the candidate's scope question in favour of the default reading. Asserted and not developed.
- NOTE (sighting, `worthiness-as-gate`): the belief named and contradicted, "at times you feel inadequate and uncared for but this is not true... you are truly loved and God has claimed your souls as His children." -- pastoral correction only, with nothing said about the doubt limiting what can be received, which is the candidate's actual claim.
- NOTE (sighting, `prayer-changes-world-conditions`): the requirement stated without a mechanism, "your prayers are required, your influence is required," with the object being that God's Will manifest in the world rather than any named person or place.
- NOTE (sighting, `incremental-journey`): "Your eyes are slowly opening, the scales peel away, and with each day comes a little more vision and understanding," with the same shape at the close, to "learn each day a little more truth."
- NOTE (witness, `preparation-to-be-a-rock`): the readying working through the soul's own perception, "as you grow in the love and your soul grows in wisdom, in insight and perception with the love, you will see your way through more clearly. You will understand to some extent the events to come and will adjust accordingly." -- also a `soul-faculties` touch.
- NOTE (considered and set aside, `two-currents-cosmology`): "life does have this mixture of light and dark, harmony and disharmony. It has been like this since the beginning," set against "God is making a great effort. We are making a greater effort to bring harmony and light to this world." -- set aside: light and darkness at world scale, but given as permanent coexistence and a divine effort rather than as two currents in contest, and without the coalescing movement that candidate turns on.

### 2014-10-11-af-judas | Every Soul Can Receive the Divine Love and Be Transformed by It | 2014-10-11
- `redemption-universal` **[Foundation]** : the premise stated as a rule, with both grounds for exclusion denied in one sentence. "It can lift up any soul who is willing to receive this blessing, to walk this path... Every soul has this choice no matter where they reside and who they are, and what they have done in their lives." The location clause covers the dark spheres and the deed clause covers the worst case, so neither where a soul is nor what it did removes the choice. Restated positively, "Every soul has the opportunity to receive this gift for it is meant for every soul."
- `redemption-universal` **[Testimony]** : the claim proved by the speaker, and the transit reported as accomplished rather than promised. "Yes, I am the Judas that betrayed our beloved Master and in this betrayal I suffered greatly. But it was the power of God's Love which indeed was the message that our beloved Master gave to the world, it was the power of God's Love that redeemed me and brought me from Hell to Heaven, from darkness to light, from deep remorse and sadness to joy and liberation." The hardest case the thread could be asked to carry, carried by the man history names for betrayal.
- `conduit-not-scripture` **[Elaboration]** : the rapport with a published text stated plainly, "I am close to him because he reads my messages, the book that is named with my name, and I impress upon him the truths which I intended to convey through this effort." The spirit attends the reading and impresses the intended meaning, so the text is a conduit whose author is still working through it. -- the same claim as the 2016-03-31-af-judas Elaboration, and earlier; weigh which carries the facet at build.
- NOTE (witness, `prayer-soul-longing`): the longing intensified by being met rather than satisfied, "Seek the Love, ask for this gift and you will feel it burning deep within, eliciting you to seek in greater depths, in greater yearning."
- NOTE (sighting, `soul-faculties`): the dormant potential named as what the gift releases, "that being which has the potential within but cannot be expressed without this gift," with the seed and germination image, "Every soul is like a seed waiting for the warmth of Love."
- NOTE (sighting, `bell-toll-instrumentality`): "a channel of love opening up within you to touch many souls in this world"; asserted.

### 2014-12-01-af-john-the-beloved | Express Your Yearnings to God | 2014-12-01
- NOTE (witness, `prayer-soul-longing`): the circle's yearning as what draws God's answer, "in this yearning, you all express yourselves to God and create a light amongst you that is powerful and beautiful as God responds to your prayers, your soul longings and bring you fully into His presence." Stated in blessing form rather than developed.
- NOTE (witness, `self-love-foundation`): "Indeed it is your challenge to love yourselves and in so doing to truly love your fellow man."

### 2015-03-23-af-john-the-beloved | Your Purpose in This Gathering Is to Teach the Prayer Practice | 2015-03-23
- NOTE (witness, `incremental-journey`): the gradual pace named and set against eternity, "This gradual awakening, this gradual enlightenment is the sure road to at-onement with God, and may feel at times tedious or slow, but you grow with each step, each breath, and that awakening, my beloveds, is swift in terms of the measure of eternity - it is very swift." In the Foundation section, beneath 2015-05-03-af-confucius.

### 2015-03-24-af-augustine | There Is Fate and Yet There Is No Fate | 2015-03-24
- `free-will-and-gods-plan` **[Foundation]** : Given in New York to a small circle beginning something new. Augustine sets out the premise the thread rests on: "there is fate and yet there is no fate." God holds an intended outcome and sees the whole of it, "God does see all and anticipates all." But free will pushes against that outcome constantly: "the power of free will buffets the enactment of God's will, turns and twists this flow." God does not answer this by overriding it, and Augustine is explicit that the constraint is legal rather than practical: "the laws of cause and effect, of free will, of creation, must be obeyed." What follows is a delay rather than a defeat. Outcomes are "not altogether predictable nor can they manifest in a predictable order," and "there is an end result which may take many, many years and generations and lifetimes to bring about." Instead of overriding, God works through willing agents, inspiring "His angels and the spirits and the many who wish to serve Him" to arrange events, which "may take circuitous routes and more time than anticipated and different players." What shortens the route is a willing soul that has been lit: the enactment "is not obscured by a lack of understanding of the truth, it is not obscured by a soul that is not ignited by God's Love," and "together the enlivened soul and the willing spirit may bring about miracles."
- `free-will-and-gods-plan` **[Foundation]** : The delay is chosen rather than imposed. Augustine states the greater power without qualification, "the power of God's Love is greater than all the power of man's will," and in the next breath reaffirms that the laws must be obeyed anyway. God is not held back by free will; God declines to use a power He has. -- the difference between a limit and a restraint, and worth stating that way when the chain is built.
- `free-will-and-gods-plan` **[Foundation]** : What the argument costs, and what it is for. Augustine names "the injustices and the extreme perversions of life, and the innocence of those who are the victims of the great power of mankind's free will, and the sin and error mounted one upon the other for generations," and then locates the remedy in souls who are "unencumbered by these conditions" and "strong enough because they possess the greatest gift possible." The delay is what allows the suffering to continue; the willing soul is what shortens it. -- the practical stake of the whole thread, stated directly. Merge with the lines above at build, or keep separate if it carries better alone.
- NOTE (witness, `provision-for-service`): circumstances arranged to put people where they are needed, "many acts and actions had to take place for you to arrive at this very moment, and the angels indeed are kept very busy to orchestrate many, many things in this world." Spoken as gratitude for the medium's own arrival rather than as teaching.
- NOTE (sighting, `soul-purpose-emergence`): "within your souls know that you have a part to play, a role, a journey, and an unfolding, manifestation of your true soul's gifts."
- NOTE (sighting, `challenges-as-blessings`): "Every day brings its challenges and its opportunities and every day that you overcome that which would hold you back into the human condition, we rejoice."

### 2015-03-27-af-andrew | Every Human Soul Is Damaged by the Earth Condition | 2015-03-27
- `two-paths` **[Foundation]** : A teaching given in New York to a circle preparing to hold a gathering there. Andrew argues from a single starting point, that everyone is wounded, to the necessity of inner healing and the choice of road by which it happens. The starting point: "Every soul has deep and abiding pain and wounds within. Every soul we meet, every individual who walks this Earth is injured by the conditions created by mankind over millennia of error and abuse and suppression. Whether that soul be in poverty and deprivation or in great wealth, there is need for healing." Then the two roads, and the requirement both share: "every soul, whether they walk the natural path or the Divine are obligated to release conditions which are not in harmony with the higher spheres of life. This process must be completed, whether it is done through the long and tedious road of the natural path or the powerful and dynamic road of God's Love, the Divine Path." The choice belongs to the hearer, and he urges one of them: "What process you choose, or route you take will determine how quickly and effectively this healing is accomplished. And of course we urge you to take the Divine Path." -- lays the premise and the fork but not the Law of Compensation machinery or the Celestial-reach claim, both of which arrive with the Capstone (2016-05-02-af-andrew, same spirit); the Capstone gives the fuller statement.
- `soul-expiation` **[Foundation]** : The starting point again, with the hiddenness of the wound named: "even those with smiles on their faces carry within them deep hurt and the unfulfilled longing for love. For some, this is quite apparent; for others it is hidden very deep." Why it has to be cleared: "in order to be that clear channel of Love those elements within which block the passage, which inhibit the Love, must be cleansed." Then a further point, that the wound can be hidden from the person carrying it: "We see within your hearts and souls that which you hide away even from yourselves," and "It is not easy to look deep within yourself to pull back the hurt where that pain exists hiding in the shadows, but it must be done and God's Love will wedge this dark and heavy thing from its hiding place and which may bring about your tears and your anguish." He also explains why the experience of healing goes up and down: "some days you feel the heaviness of this pain, while other days you're in bliss. And this remains a mystery to you, my beloveds, why this is so. But the conditions within your soul flare up into your consciousness and cause you distress at times, a reminder that there is more healing to be done." And what finally does the work is the Love rather than the effort, in his closing prayer: "such a mighty inflowing of Love within your souls that all of that which remains there which is not in harmony with this Love shall be forced out by it." -- carries three steps of the registry spine, not only the premise. Nearest members: 2016-05-09-af-augustine (three origins of the conditions), 2018-04-03-af-luke (the inherited strand), 2018-07-14-af-seretta-kem (Reframe).
- `healing-channel` **[Elaboration]** : Joins two things: you cannot carry Love to others until you have let it heal you. "In order to be a clear channel of Love one must be healed within to allow the love of God to do its work, and to do so in a conscious way, to make those choices that do not further injure yourself or others," restated at the close as the cleansing of "those elements within which block the passage, which inhibit the Love." He also states the thread's small-numbers claim: "it does not require great numbers to bring love and light to this world, it requires a yearning soul in prayer in harmony with one another." -- open question for build: the nearest member is 2016-02-10-af-confucius (method and precondition); if, read in full, its precondition is already the self-healing one, demote this to witness.
- NOTE (witness, `challenges-as-blessings`): the daily-test claim, "Every day brings a new test and a new reward and a new opportunity to grow and expand in this Love," and "With each test you will let go of your fears and your deep pain." Stated rather than argued, so witness level.
- NOTE (sighting, `incremental-journey`): the incremental release in a clause, "with each step comes and with each step something is released." -- keep distinct at build from 2019-04-04-af-matthew: Matthew explains why progress oscillates, Andrew explains why the felt experience oscillates during cleansing, and Andrew's account is logged as a `soul-expiation` member above.
- NOTE (witness, `fear-of-full-immersion`): the same subject from the other side. Where the thread diagnoses what people fear to lose, Andrew asks for the thing directly: "Will you open yourselves to this in a complete and vulnerable way? Be with God, as you never have been before," with "desire to be completely in God's Love" earlier. An invitation rather than an account of the obstacle, and a witness rather than a sighting because the complete-and-vulnerable opening it asks for is this thread's own subject named from the requesting end. -- 2016-08-15-af-alec-gaunt names vulnerability among the losses people imagine.
- NOTE (witness, `prayer-changes-world-conditions`): "it does not require great numbers to bring love and light to this world, it requires a yearning soul in prayer in harmony with one another, and there will be many in spirit with you." The condition is harmony, not numbers, which 2016-09-01-af-seretta-kem later develops at length; here it is one clause about a small gathering. -- same passage does the work in the `healing-channel` role above; recorded once so the earlier date is findable, not counted twice.
- NOTE (considered and set aside, `divine-love-healing`): "there is no freedom without the complete and beautiful healing within that comes with God's Love" claims sufficiency but makes no comparison with other kinds of healing and no argument for primacy, so it sits below the thread's bar. -- recorded so the checkpoint is not re-litigated.
- NOTE (pattern, described and not penned): the message turns the diagnosis into the assignment. Because everyone is wounded, every encounter is an opening: "no matter where you turn there is an opportunity to be a channel of Love." Distinct from `channel-of-love-in-the-material`, which is about the sanctity of ordinary work, and from `bell-toll-instrumentality`, which is about radiating without speaking. Here the state of the world is itself the mission field.

### 2015-03-28-af-augustine | The Journey of Divine Love Takes Time but Brings Great Benefits | 2015-03-28
- `religious-error` **[Testimony]** : Augustine names himself, names what he did, and says what he now does about it. He opens: "I am Augustine, once the bishop of Hippo while on Earth. I played a great part in developing the church of Christ, and in so doing I encouraged many erroneous beliefs and understandings of Jesus' true message." What he does now is undo it: "so today I work with souls on the Earth and souls in spirit to bring this Truth and clarity, the Truth of God's Love, which is the message that Jesus brought to the world." This is the thread's claim in the most direct form it can take. The other members argue that inherited religious teaching is in error; Augustine is one of the people who taught it, saying so himself. He does not defend the error, explain it away, or describe himself as having been misled. He says he encouraged it, and that it was his doing.
- `incremental-journey` **[Elaboration]** : Adds the threshold the registry spine does not carry: the increments accumulate before the journey proper begins, and there is a moment when it starts. "The blessing of Divine Love is an incremental thing. Each step brings you closer to God, every dollop of this Love brings greater light to your soul and enlightenment to your being. Yes it takes time, it takes prayers, it takes longing." Then the turn: "when the awareness of this Love enters your being, when you truly know that God has placed something beautiful within you, then you begin the journey, a journey of transformation, of letting go of all of those things which are not in harmony with this Love." His own case underwrites it: he prayed, ascended through the spheres, and arrived. -- the spine runs leaps-then-trickles, oscillation, reinforcement, swiftness in eternity's measure; the threshold is a genuine addition. Confucius anchor (2015-05-03) not read in full; demote to witness at build if it already carries this.
- NOTE (witness, `self-love-foundation`): supplies the step above the thread's premise. The roster argues that loving oneself is the prerequisite for loving others; Augustine gives the source of the self-love: "you are truly loved by God and in this you will truly love yourselves. And in this you will love your brothers and sisters." Being loved by God comes first, self-love follows from it, and love of others follows from that. -- stated in one sentence, so witness, not the anchor; but this message names where self-love comes from, and that order is worth carrying into the anchor decision.
- `spiritual-fellowship` **[Foundation]** : the message's largest single block argues for fellowship rather than commending it, giving two reasons: it holds the seeker against falling "back into the human conditions," and it supplies perception the seeker cannot reach alone, since "each has a unique perception and understanding and journey to take and in sharing this with one another, it opens new awarenesses." Section anchor. The second reason is the bar every later member is measured against: encouragement to gather is everywhere in the archive, and the claim that other souls reveal what one's own cannot is rare.
- NOTE (sighting, `soul-purpose-emergence`): "each soul is unique and each soul will come to understand and express its unique qualities which are truly a gift from God. These will flourish, and expand within your being." The thread's claim asserted, not developed. Note the verb: the qualities are *come to be understood*, which is the emergence shape rather than a revelation.
- NOTE (sighting, `prayer-soul-longing`): prayer as desire rather than words or place, "Prayers that go to your Heavenly Father at any time and in any place - these prayers can be sent forth on wings of desire to be with your God."

### 2015-03-29-af-seretta-kem | The Greatest of All Types of Healing Is Divine Love Healing | 2015-03-29
- `divine-love-healing` **[Foundation]** : Seretta Kem introduces himself as a healer who studied the art for a very long time in Egypt and who now walks the Divine Path in the Celestial Kingdom, and from that standing makes the thread's central claim: "The greatest healing of all is obtained through the inflowing of God's Essence, His Divine Love." What makes it the greatest is its reach, which he states in full: "every cell within your body, all the energies of your spirit, all the aspects of your mind, both material and soul are influenced by this Love." He also names what the healing feels like from inside, so that the seeker does not mistake it for going backwards: "when you feel stray emotions rising up from within you, this is the power of God's Love, in its qualities of expiation of the soul, cleansing your soul." -- this message does not set Divine Love against healing energies that can be passed on: there is no comparison with other healing energies anywhere in the text, and the claim is a superlative, not a contrast. The claim that the blessing cannot be passed from person to person is made at 2016-04-13-af-seretta-kem ("one blessing cannot be passed from person to person"). The Foundation and presumptive anchor rest on the superlative and how far the healing reaches.
- `free-will-and-self-responsibility` **[Elaboration]** : The message's largest argument, stated plainly: the spirit world could remove the obstructions and deliberately does not. "We in spirit who have great powers of healing, can with each day bring you to a place where you are free of these inhibitions, these obstructions, but would this strengthen you on your journey? It would be gratifying and make many challenges easier, but it would not strengthen your souls, your resolve, and those aspects of your beings which need transformation, and discipline, and efforts which must rise from within you." He anticipates being misread and heads it off: "Do not take my message as a message that indicates that we do not have care for you... for we do, in great measure. But in partnership with our efforts, your efforts are required and necessary." The same restraint God shows in `free-will-and-gods-plan`, here shown by the angels and given its reason: help that bypasses effort would leave the soul unstrengthened. -- fence at build against `free-will-and-gods-plan`: that thread is about God declining to override the plan's route, this is about helpers declining to shortcut a soul's growth.
- `soul-expiation` **[Elaboration]** : Names the mechanism by which cleansing becomes conscious, and states the ratio: more Love means more visibility of what is wrong. "As more Love flows into your soul, it brings to your consciousness a greater awareness of that which is not in harmony with this Love. Again you must choose to change." Read with 2015-03-27-af-andrew, which explains the same ups and downs from the other side, conditions flaring up into consciousness. Andrew explains why the feeling comes; Seretta Kem explains why more Love produces more of it.
- NOTE (witness, `prayer-soul-longing`): the gift is not imposed, "God does not give His Love to those who do not choose to receive it. It is your choice. It is your prayer. It is your longing."
- NOTE (witness, `earth-conditions-and-prayer`): the human condition as an active opponent rather than a passive weight, "it does indeed work against the human condition which is always conspiring to distract and detract from this burning Love."
- NOTE (sighting, `soul-faculties`): obstructions as the reason the soul's knowing does not surface, "there are many obstructions within the mind and the body and spirit which often shield and veil the awarenesses of the soul," and when they clear, "the awareness from deep within you, within your souls, will blossom."
- NOTE (pattern, described and not penned): the body as a participant in soul work. Seretta Kem asks for changes at the level of "the very cellular structure of your bodies," and names diet twice alongside thought and prayer as things requiring discipline: "your choices in your thinking, in what you eat, in how you pray, and how you are in the world." He grounds it in the body as the soul's housing, "those needs of your bodies which would make greater harmony with this temple that God has placed your souls in." Not healing of the body as a result of soul work, but care of the body as a contribution to it. -- Suggested slug `body-as-partner`.

### 2015-05-03-af-confucius | The Ups and the Downs of the Journey | 2015-05-03
- `incremental-journey` **[Foundation]** : presumptive section anchor, a developed statement of the pattern: "Indeed your journeys are incremental and you walk a bit and grow a little more... Your understanding will come in leaps and bounds, and in trickles, and then slow, and then running. It is ever thus." The cause is named, "the conditions of this world often impede you, and the conditions that you carry within yourselves impede your progress. Yet you struggle and God lifts you up," and so is the gain each round keeps, "each time you make this effort, the struggle for something higher, you feel a little more joy within your souls... and you become a little more immune from it, and stronger."

### 2015-05-11-af-andrew | Seek the Knowledge of God's Creation Through Love | 2015-05-11
- NOTE (witness, `mind-vs-soul`): the mind's limit given as a missing dimension, "It is like looking at something with only two dimensions where there are many dimensions," and the soul as the source of what the mind lacks, "truly it is your souls that can impart knowledge and truth to your mind." States the thread's claim without developing it.
- NOTE (sighting, `soul-faculties`): "Great insight is given through the soul perceptions which are ignited by the Love within your souls."

### 2015-05-18-af-augustine | Recognizing Your Two Selves, the Soul and the Material Mind | 2015-05-18
- NOTE (witness, `mind-vs-soul`): the two-selves premise stated pastorally, "Do you recognize your two selves, the one which is human and the one which is your soul? When you are born these two selves are distinct," the human self hardened by the world while the soul yearns for God. Sits beneath the Foundation, 2015-10-12-af-augustine.
- NOTE (build, `mind-vs-soul`): this message asks the human part "to essentially fall away in harmony with your soul's ascension," which sits against the Reframe at 2018-05-17-af-augustine, where the soul predominates within a blend rather than replacing the mind. The same spirit speaks both, and the Reframe can be read as refining this; worth weighing at build.
- NOTE (witness, `self-love-foundation`): "The next step is to truly love yourselves, beloveds, to see the Light within you and recognize what a beautiful creation you are," with loving oneself and others named together, "you love yourselves in accordance to this, and love others," and "Do not condemn yourselves."
- NOTE (witness, `preparation-to-be-a-rock`): readiness for what is coming tied to the soul's strength: "if you are still anchored in the human condition, if you cannot rise above this to that soulful awareness, you will have great difficulties adjusting and dealing with what is to come. Yet if you are in the flow of God's Love and your souls are strong in this Love... you will be embarking on a great journey that will foster the change that is coming."

### 2015-06-15-af-faith-nyquist | Walk in the Light and Be the Beacon of Light despite the Daily Challenges | 2015-06-15
- NOTE (witness, `challenges-as-blessings`): the daily test as the way forward, "It is a testing time for each one of you... Each day is a challenge and a test. Each day you move a little further along in your understanding and your faith," with the tests located in small things, "your tests lie in these small situations within your life that aggravate and compel you off this Path of Light." Given as encouragement rather than argued.
- NOTE (witness, `judgment-to-love`): the cost of judging falls on the one who judges, "if you get caught up in judgment and react to the behaviors of others which you do not approve of, you counteract the influence of God's Love, you take away from this Light, you divert the flow of God's Love."

### 2015-06-15-af-joseph-smith | The Imaginings and Ideas of Men Create Falsehoods and Misinterpretations | 2015-06-15
- `religious-error` **[Elaboration]** : Joseph Smith speaks about the church he himself founded, having come into the Celestial Heavens and the truth of Divine Love, "though I had glimpses of this Truth while on Earth, I did not fully understand the workings of this Love." He does not disown the founding, and he confirms that the help was real: "the angels did indeed come to support me and influence me in my work in creating this great church." The error came afterward. "Now I see that this church and its workings have become too infected by the human condition. It is indeed a great machine desirous of consuming all of humanity in what it perceives to be the truth. And its workings are complex and its understandings unfortunately contain more error than truth." He then generalises it to the thread's central claim, in the words the message takes its title from: "so it is with many religions that are organized in the world. The will of man usurps the Will of God. The imaginings and ideas of men create falsehoods and misinterpretations of the laws and Love of God." Two facets. First, the mechanism is named as appetite rather than mistake: "This desire often is for power and recognition," which he traces to the beginning, "The source of the great fall of mankind is to be powerful, to not acknowledge the power of God." Second, the instruction is to stay and work from inside rather than to leave: "continue to challenge that which you see is error, for if you do so in Love and integrity, you will make some changes which are necessary... if you, who say you are a part of this church, become an example of Love and Truth within it you will have an opportunity to change the conditions within it." -- sits with the Augustine Testimony (2015-03-28) and the Martin Luther entries: three founders and reformers correcting their own work. Augustine says he encouraged the error; Luther says he has released much of what he fought for; Smith says the angels really did help and the institution decayed anyway. The three should be read together as a set at build.
- `spiritual-fellowship` **[Elaboration]** : Defines what a church actually is, which the Foundation (2015-03-28-af-augustine) does not attempt. Not a building, an institution, or a label: "you seek it together and this is a true church, a church of Love, a community in fellowship that seeks Light and true understanding." He also names the cost of getting it wrong, and it is the reverse of the usual warning: the danger is not isolation but the trade people make to avoid it, "some seek community and long for that fellowship and are willing to abdicate their desire to seek truth in a clear way and adopt that which they are told to be truth." So fellowship is necessary and also the thing most likely to cost you the truth, depending on what it is founded on. -- it develops the Foundation rather than repeating it: Augustine argues that fellowship is needed, Smith argues what makes it genuine.
- NOTE (witness, `conduit-not-scripture`): the authority is the soul's own enlightenment rather than any office or text, "true understanding comes from the enlightenment of the soul and the enlightenment of the soul comes from the inflowing of the Father's Divine Essence."
- NOTE (witness, `healing-channel`): the calling stated without institutional apparatus, "To be a channel of God's Love in the world is the highest of callings. And this does not require labels or positions within any church."
- NOTE (witness, `vanguards-of-change`): the small group with long reach, "the reverberations of your efforts will spread through time throughout this world. Many generations to come will feel the effects of a handful of true souls who sought to be channels of Love," with the circle named as first-comers, "It is coming to mankind and you are the harbingers."
- NOTE (sighting, `bell-toll-instrumentality`): "It is in your being and in your loving service to God that your great message will be felt and many will be drawn to you because of your trueness, your Light, and your Love."
- NOTE (sighting, `the-fall`): the fall attributed to the appetite for power, "The source of the great fall of mankind is to be powerful, to not acknowledge the power of God." A different account from the Judas Foundation, where the refusal came from contentment. -- worth fencing at build: contentment and ambition are not the same diagnosis, and the thread should not blur them.

### 2015-06-21-af-martin-luther | God's Will Is above Man's Will | 2015-06-21
- `free-will-and-gods-plan` **[Elaboration]** : God's Will prevails over human will without overriding it: "although the free will of man is strong and powerful, the Will of God is stronger. And there will be resolution... that are a part of this great plan that God has for the salvation of mankind." The willing soul cannot be thwarted by the will of others, "There is no need for you to feel as if you are being thwarted by the will of others, for this is not true. You walk within the Will of God, and this shall always be true as long as you are willing." His own life is offered in a sentence, "in my day I had great opposition, but I was persistent and I felt I was doing God's Will."
- NOTE (witness, `prayer-changes-world-conditions`): "the power of your prayers, the intention of your prayers, the sincerity of your prayers are more powerful than all of these obstructions and conditions laid about by mankind... this power can indeed change the conditions of this world."
- NOTE (considered and set aside, `religious-error`): the message does not address religious error or doctrine; its subject is God's Will prevailing over human will.

### 2015-06-29-af-confucius | You Must Prepare Yourselves for What Is Coming | 2015-06-29
- `preparation-to-be-a-rock` **[Foundation]** : the calm-before-the-storm call: "you are entering the calm before the storm, my beloveds," with the soul's cleansing hastened for what follows, "This expiation is necessary and has indeed been accelerated, for with what comes next you will be required to be strong and centered in the Love of God." Prayer redoubled, "This effort can no longer be a casual one," and attachments released, so that "when you enter the storm you must be clothed in the raiments of Light." In the Foundation section, beneath the anchor, 2015-08-17-af-jesus.
- NOTE (witness, `provision-for-service`): provision promised for those who let go of their attachments to serve: "all you require will be provided, all your obligations in this world will come into the care of God's Loving Hand, and you will not feel as if you are sacrificing and compromising in your life."

### 2015-08-17-af-jesus | Jesus Speaks Seriously of Changes to Come: Be a Channel of Love through the Storms | 2015-08-17
- `preparation-to-be-a-rock` **[Foundation]** : establishes the premise the thread builds on: great change is coming, "The world is fast approaching a time of chaos. There is a great change coming, the storm is just upon the horizon," and the gathered must prepare themselves and prepare places of refuge, "You must prepare those places, those lifeboats of Light for those who are caught in the storms of change... you must focus your efforts to prepare yourselves and to prepare places and to prepare those who will listen," so they can be channels of Love through the chaos. Anchors the thread at its most urgent.
- `earth-changes` **[Foundation]** : a developed statement of the thread's premise. The cause is the abuse of the Earth, "The Earth writhes in pain, it has been abused so greatly by the will of men, by the greed of men and the Earth is fighting back," and the agent is God's Creation answering it, "this will not be caused by men but by God and by God's Creation, which responds to the actions of men," since "God does not punish those who do wrong but the laws of creation are inexorable." The purpose is cleansing and renewal, "what is to come is the cleansing of the world, a starting anew, a time of regeneration," followed by "a thousand years of peace upon this world."
- NOTE (sighting, `mind-vs-soul`): "you seek to understand through the faculties of your mind rather than the perceptions of your souls," and "For the soul informs all. The soul is everything. And then the mind will be subjugated by the soul"; the message's own contributions are the call to prepare as refuges and the earth-changes premise.
- NOTE (context): the message closes with a prayer Jesus offers for the circle, "Heavenly Father, bless these, Thy children, who seek the Light...", preserved as a standalone practice file (content/practices/prayer-2015-08-17-af-jesus.md).

### 2015-08-17-af-peter | Seek out Your True Purpose | 2015-08-17
- `soul-purpose-emergence` **[Foundation]** : God has embedded in each soul a purpose fitted to its own gifts, with the reason given, "He does not give you that which is too difficult for you to fulfill"; ignoring it brings inner strife, "in ignoring your purpose, you are denying a part of yourself"; and the Love is the agent of its surfacing, "as the Love flows into your soul, as your faculties of the soul become enlivened... this great purpose becomes a very powerful part of your motivations", arriving finally with a clarity "so powerful that even your minds will not contradict what you know within your souls." Adds prayer as the active instrument of discovery and frames the purpose as a task on behalf of mankind, neither of which the argument as written carries. Does not carry the free-will step: the alternative here is incapacity, not refusal. Augustine 2019-01-29 holds the anchor.
- NOTE (witness, `knowing-your-own-soul`): the exclusive form, "knowing your souls you are able to know God. As God puts within you His Essence, your soul is empowered with knowledge and wisdom... There is no other sure way to do this, my beloveds. There is no other way to at-onement with God." Held at witness rather than member under the scope test: the claim is a clause inside a message arguing purpose.
- NOTE (sighting, `pentecost`): the thread's own two modes, applied to purpose rather than to awakening, "for some of you, the awareness of this purpose will come swiftly, and for others it will emerge as you progress upon your life's path in this world."
- NOTE (sighting, `bell-toll-instrumentality`): "you will shine forth wherever you go. And many will notice and see you, will be drawn to you, will seek out what you have and you will indeed teach many to seek this Path."
- NOTE (sighting, `vanguards-of-change`): "mankind needs you, needs you to forge this Path and walk this trail so that others may follow."
- NOTE (sighting, `mind-vs-soul`): the soul as the route to God stated as exclusive, "knowing your souls you are able to know God... There is no other way to at-onement with God. This is the way."

### 2015-08-20-af-faith-nyquist | Encouragement to Establish Centers of Light | 2015-08-20
- `provision-for-service` **[Foundation]** : provision promised for a named work, the Centres of Light, with the material spelled out and a condition attached: "God will provide the means, the buildings, the finances, all that is required will be provided given your intention to work and support in loving harmony the possibilities of creating these Centres." The supply is tied to the work being God's, "they are required in the world," and to intention and harmony on the human side. Stated rather than argued, so it sits in the Foundation section beneath the anchor, 2016-04-04-af-augustine, which develops the claim.

### 2015-09-21-af-mary | Each of You Has Been Given a Special Gift | 2015-09-21
- NOTE (witness, `soul-purpose-emergence`): "Each soul here has a Light. Each soul here has a purpose, and each soul here has within them many Gifts which they are not yet aware of, which await the transformation of your consciousness to that of soul awareness," with the Love clearing the way to knowing them, "as it pours within you it flushes away all that keeps you from this understanding and knowledge."
- NOTE (context): the published text begins mid-sentence ("…Love."), so the opening of the message is lost.

### 2015-10-12-af-augustine | The Difference Between the Material Mind and the Soul Mind | 2015-10-12
- `mind-vs-soul` **[Foundation]** : Delivered to answer a question put to the circle that evening about the difference between the two minds, which makes it the one message in the thread that sets out to define the distinction rather than to apply it. Two entities, not two faculties of one: "these are two distinct entities within one being, within all of you." The material mind described as developed and dominant, "all its attributes of the intellect and the perceptions of the senses... making sense of your lives, of the world and functioning within these human conditions. And this is what is predominant in your beings and consciousness." The soul's faculties present but unlit: "the soul is imbued with many qualities and attributes which mostly lie dormant without being ignited and brought to life by God's Divine Essence." The incapacity stated as a limit in kind rather than degree, which is the claim the whole thread rests on: the things of God "cannot be comprehended by the material mind, it requires a deeper understanding, something more expansive, something that is not the result of deduction and analysis. It is a knowing." What ignites it is not effort: "this Gift comes on the wings of Love as the Heavenly Father's Holy Spirit touches your souls, ignites this flame within you and opens your eyes. Eyes that have remained firmly shut without this touch of Love." And the outcome is not the mind's defeat but the two working together: "this awakening... brings about a symbiosis of consciousness that allows you... to comprehend with great and expansive understanding God's creation." Fence against `soul-faculties`: that thread argues what the awakened soul can do, this one argues that the two consciousnesses are different in kind and that only one can know God.
- `two-paths` **[Elaboration]** : Maps the fork onto the two minds: the natural path perfects the mind it already has, "they seek perfection of the material mind which, in essence, is the spiritual mind," while the Divine path grows the other one, "you, my beloveds, who choose the Divine Path will seek the expansion of your soul and that which is within your soul, the soul mind." Puts a ceiling on natural-love comprehension in the same terms 2018-05-12-af-luke gives in faculty terms: "those upon the Natural path do indeed get glimpses of these realities, but it is not a full and comprehensive understanding and never can be." Choice framed as perfecting the given versus receiving the new: "to carry on perfecting what God has given them or to seek beyond that, to what God can give."
- NOTE (witness, `soul-faculties`): the endless expansion of comprehension, "This awareness, this consciousness goes on for all eternity... Those little flickers and glimmers of understanding and vision shall grow and grow"; the thread's growth claim asserted.
- NOTE (sighting, `unlimited-reception`): "you will continue to receive this blessing for all eternity... shall come again and again and again, never ending, always expanding."
- NOTE (sighting, `prayer-soul-longing`): "there is something else within you that longs to be with God"; the soul's longing as the thread names it, asserted in passing.

### 2015-10-26-af-aman | The First Father Talks about Reincarnation and the Progression of Life | 2015-10-26
- `reincarnation-progression` **[Foundation]** : Aman answers a woman in the circle who believes in reincarnation, and grounds the answer in standing rather than argument: he is the first parent, has "lived for many thousands of years in spirit," and has "spawned the many generations upon generations that followed our beginnings in this world." The claim itself is stated as testimony, and he is careful about its limits: "I have never observed a spirit returning to this Earth in the flesh. And, though I do not know all of my children, for there are many billions of them who have followed in our footsteps, yet I have been witness to many, many spirits and mortals." Then the positive doctrine that replaces reincarnation. Souls pre-exist, and the one incarnation exists to individuate them: "The earth plane is but one plane of existence. Its function, primarily, is to give a soul identity for there is a pre-existence of souls. It is necessary for that soul to incarnate into the flesh to bring about a flowering and manifestation of its true self." Once that is done the life has served its purpose, however long or short it was, "it may be accomplished in a very short life or a very long life," and growth continues through the planes of spirit instead: "there are many more lives to live in spirit as you pass from one plane to another." The finality is stated plainly: "never more will you dwell in the flesh once you walk upon this Path Divine."
- `death-transition` **[Elaboration]** : Death is a change of conditions rather than of kind, and the lower planes are said to resemble Earth closely: "When the soul sheds its body of flesh is there such a great difference in existence? No, my beloveds, it is merely a transition. And, in the lower planes of spirit those spirits who exist there experience something very close to your own fleshy existence. There is very little to discern between the two." What changes as the soul purifies is the character of the environment itself: "the laws which determine life in these spheres, come into play and life becomes a more ethereal existence, less bound to the conditions of the world."
- NOTE (witness, `two-paths`): Aman places himself on the Divine road and names what it opens, "for those who follow this path of Love, of which I am a part of as is my dear and beloved soul mate, there are even greater opportunities for enlightenment and growth and experience in coming ever closer to the Creator." -- read with 2016-05-29-af-aman, where the same spirit describes reaching the sixth sphere by the natural road and then crossing over; that message is the Testimony, this is the affirmation.
- NOTE (sighting, `soul-purpose-emergence`): the incarnation exists to bring "a flowering and manifestation of its true self," which is the thread's emergence claim placed at the level of why anyone is born at all.

### 2015-10-26-af-faith-nyquist | Guidance about Our Choices in the Flow of God's Love | 2015-10-26
- `mind-vs-soul` **[Elaboration]** : Belief is assigned to the mind and, on that basis, set aside: "Do not judge another soul for what they believe, for belief is of the mind and this, in time, you will see is irrelevant." What matters instead is a connection that runs beneath belief entirely, "It is that soul connection, your loving soul to their soul that is what is meant by being a channel of Love in the world." She then names what obstructs it, and the obstruction is also mental: "remove the restrictions that come with your mental prejudices and fears." The thread's distinction applied to other people rather than to one's own knowing, which is where its other members sit. -- feeds `divine-love-without-knowing` by implication: if belief is irrelevant to the soul connection, holding the wrong beliefs cannot bar a soul from receiving.
- `judgment-to-love` **[Elaboration]** : Not only that judgment should be released, but why, and what to do with the impulse instead. Error in another is real and she does not pretend otherwise, "there are times when you see error and disharmony in another." The correction is simply not yours: "It is not for you to correct this, it is for you to show Truth within yourselves and who you are in the world, to be an example. It is for God to lead them into the Light and for their souls to draw them into the Light." Two agents are named, God and the person's own soul, which is what makes the restraint something other than indifference. The instruction that follows is active rather than passive: "Do not concern yourselves with that which you see are obstructions upon another's path, but pray for them, my beloveds, love them and guide them through your example." -- sits closely with 2016-04-12-af-moses, which reaches the same ruling from a different direction: Moses argues from the relativity of perceived truth, Faith from the irrelevance of belief. Moses also carries the exception for gross error and blatant negativity, which Faith does not; the pair covers the rule and its limit.
- NOTE (witness, `prayer-changes-world-conditions`): prayer as the thing to do instead of intervening, with assurance that it lands, "your prayers... are answered and are always being answered as the angels draw close," and "all the souls in your lives benefit from your prayers."
- NOTE (witness, `soul-purpose-emergence`): the thread's claim in its opening line, "every soul must take its unique journey, an enfoldment and an unfolding of his path and purpose upon their journey which is guided and touched by God as the soul becomes infused with the Father's Love and your Gifts emerge accordingly."
- NOTE (witness, `bell-toll-instrumentality`): the example as the medium of the message, "guide them through your example," with the person's own life as the instrument rather than anything they say.
- NOTE (sighting, `free-will-and-gods-plan`): worry dissolved by surrender, "As you begin to release your will to the Father's Will, so your worries and cares are absorbed in your faith and trust in your Heavenly Father."
- NOTE (witness, `preparation-to-be-a-rock`): the readying conducted quietly and in advance of the work it is for, guidance given "in subtle, yet powerful ways to help you along your path and the work that awaits you."

### 2015-11-09-af-faith-nyquist | Persist in Your Faith in Bringing about Centers of Light in the World | 2015-11-09
- NOTE (witness, `provision-for-service`): the same Centres of Light work, met with negotiations and setbacks, and the supply described as help rather than money: "we in spirit are working diligently to clear away the obstacles," and "you will be surprised at the insights and blessings and inspirations that will be given for you to accomplish this work." Asserted as encouragement, with persistence and prayer asked of the people serving.

### 2015-11-28-af-faith-nyquist | The Power of Prayer to Bring Change and Healing | 2015-11-28
- `prayer-soul-longing` **[Foundation]** : Faith establishes that sincere prayer and soul-longing for God's Love brings real change in both the material and spirit worlds; strips prayer to its essence ("just longing, just prayer"). Anchors the thread.

### 2015-11-30-af-andrew | The Work Begins and You Are Ready | 2015-11-30
- `bell-toll-instrumentality` **[Foundation]** : Andrew introduces the bell-toll image: God's Touch flowing through awakened souls reaches many like a bell toll awakening, with the gathered souls holding the rope that rings it. Anchors the thread.

### 2015-12-14-af-augustine | Free Will versus God's Will | 2015-12-14
- `free-will-and-gods-plan` **[Elaboration]** : God's Will cannot be known from the mind: "you cannot know God's Will from your mind, for a mind that is not informed by the soul filled with God's Essence cannot possibly understand the Will of God. It is incapable." The willful mind's attempt to shape God's Will in the world is "the great folly of mankind," and the world as it stands is "the manifestation of the willfulness, the power of free will... the power of all the choices made through the ages, creating this reality." The answer is to pray for the Love, so that a soul may "know with great certainty and clarity God's Will for you."
- `mind-vs-soul` **[Elaboration]** : applies the distinction to knowing God's Will: "The faculties of the mind were not gifted to mankind for this purpose. The faculties of the soul, however, enlivened by God's Touch of Love are quite capable of understanding God's Will," with the soul informing the mind "in a harmonious balance."
- NOTE (witness, `soul-purpose-emergence`): "each soul is imbued with a great purpose which is reflected by the many Gifts which God has given you," known through the soul enlivened by the Love rather than through the mind.

### 2016-01-04-af-seretta-kem | On Being a Comforter in This World | 2016-01-04 [not publicly posted]
- `healing-channel` **[Foundation]** : Healing begins in the soul not the mind; dogmatic mental constructs lower the group's resonance and, via the Law of Attraction, draw lesser influences; singleness of purpose makes a clear channel. Anchors the new `healing-channel` thread. Distinct from `divine-love-healing` (what Divine Love healing IS) - this thread is HOW a person becomes a channel for it.
- `mind-vs-soul` **[Elaboration]** : Applies the soul-not-mind distinction to the motivation and intention behind service.

### 2016-01-08-af-jesus | Encouragement from Jesus | 2016-01-08 [not publicly posted]
- `healing-channel` **[Elaboration]** : Jesus commissions the group as vanguards and a "great channel of service," carrying God's Love and healing to those in darkness. Applies the channel teaching as encouragement and commissioning rather than mechanism. Also a possible witness for `free-will-and-gods-plan` (God's plan, perfect timing, doors opening); confirm against the full text.
- `vanguards-of-change` **[Foundation]** : establishes the commission: "You are the vanguards," said twice; God "will open the doors" in the timing of His Will; their souls already sense what is coming; the commission serves "that great plan of our Heavenly Father's for the salvation of mankind." The commissioning material also logged under `healing-channel` belongs here; weigh demoting that Elaboration to witness at build ("great channel of service" keeps a witness-level tie).
- NOTE (considered and set aside, `free-will-and-gods-plan`): an encouragement-level echo only, God's plan and doors opening in His timing, with no development of the will-reconciliation argument, so not a member.
- NOTE (sighting, `provision-for-service`): doors opened, "God will open the doors, my beloveds, He will open the doors," and the soul nourished, "shall provide for you the great nourishment of your souls," with nothing said about supplying the work. Opportunity rather than provision, on the far side of the thread's fence.

### 2016-01-16-af-jesus | Blessed Are You Who Seek to Be a Channel of Love | 2016-01-16
- NOTE (pattern, described and not penned): wordless presence as service, which this message shares with 2019-01-15-af-augustine. A service benediction that advances no thread; it would be penned when a message builds an argument from it.

### 2016-01-18-af-confucius | How the Gift of Love Can Touch a Soul and Change the World | 2016-01-18
- `bell-toll-instrumentality` **[Elaboration]** : Confucius deepens the mechanism: the Love within a soul is an active agent that ignites longing in others unawares; the stronger the Love, the more resonant the bell toll, and this silent instrumentality is named the single greatest gift and the agent that will change the world.
- `free-will-and-gods-plan` **[Elaboration]** : "You are given the gift of free will... but this is your destiny." Applies Augustine's premise to the individual's inner struggle: the soul determines the timing, but alignment with God's Will is the destiny free will exists to choose; resistance burns away as the soul gains ascendency. Pairs with the Augustine foundation.

### 2016-01-23-af-augustine | The Power of Prayer | 2016-01-23
- `prayer-soul-longing` **[Elaboration]** : Augustine draws the central distinction: prayer is the anchor and foundation of the Path Divine, but "not a prayer that is a recitation from your minds, but prayer that is a longing from your soul"; it is the longing that elicits God's response.

### 2016-01-24-af-augustine | Lesson on Soul-Desire | 2016-01-24
- `prayer-soul-longing` **[Elaboration]** : Augustine refines the chain on a specific point: the desires of the soul and the mind often differ, and this mismatch is what derails prayer (a soul may long for God's Love while the mind feels unworthy; the mind may think it receives where the soul does not truly desire). The remedy is to plumb the soul's true desire and make it the focus of prayer.
- `mind-vs-soul` **[Elaboration]** : Frames the material mind as the chief obstacle - its clutter, fear, and sense of unworthiness obscuring the soul's true desire - and calls for disciplining the mind so the soul can commune directly with God.

### 2016-01-28-af-francis-of-assisi | How I Became a Channel of Love in the World | 2016-01-28
- `bell-toll-instrumentality` **[Testimony]** : Francis offers his own Earth life as evidence for the mechanism: as a soul seeks God's Love it brings a special Light into the world that "rings the bell and the bell tolls and the souls have a flicker of awakening and respond." First-person witness that the awakened soul awakens others, from one who lived it.

### 2016-02-02-af-jesus | Allow God to Touch Those Wounded and Dark Recesses Within Your Souls | 2016-02-02
- `guidance-as-seed` **[Foundation]** : the whole argument in one message, from how God's Will arrives to what the soul owes it. It arrives small, "God's Will comes to you as a seed of a thought, a small inkling within you and it grows as His Love nurtures this seed and brings it to fruition into your consciousness." The soul's part is to take it seriously, "It is important for you to listen, to grasp, to honor, to allow these things within you to take hold within your consciousness," and then to "act upon this in loving faith honoring the Will of your Heavenly Father." Jesus gives an example from the instrument's own life, a seed of a thought given when he asked how to widen his ministry, which he was reluctant to accept: "this is true guidance and this is another door opening."

### 2016-02-10-af-confucius | Remember to Nourish Your Soul | 2016-02-10 [not publicly posted]
- `healing-channel` **[Elaboration]** : Confucius gives the working method: pray for God to touch the soul through you, acting in resonance and harmony with the flow of Love, and nourish your own soul foremost as the precondition for clear channelhood. Distinguishes the soul's emanation of love from the many other energies and emanations of body and spirit body - the soul-aligned flow is what makes healing powerful.

### 2016-02-15-af-augustine | Forge That Path to Your Souls and Thereby to God | 2016-02-15 [not publicly posted]
- `knowing-your-own-soul` **[Foundation]** : The one message that states the gap, the claim and the measure together. The gap: "it is not easy for you to know your souls, a powerful and subtle Gift from God that brings you life. For you know your minds so well, this well-trodden place which you value and nurture and revere." The claim, which is also the title and the excerpt: "you must forge that path to your souls, for in doing so you forge a path to God." The measure, and the thing that makes this an argument rather than an exhortation, is the mind itself: "We urge you to know your souls, to know them well, to know them every bit as well as your minds." What the mind obstructs is specifically self-knowledge, "the mind's interpretation of God's creation and of who you are and why you are and where you are going." Mechanism: "Continue, my beloveds, in your prayers for the Father's Love... this is the key to opening the door to the soul." The message argues two things and keeps both roles: the `mind-vs-soul` Elaboration below stands unchanged.
- `mind-vs-soul` **[Elaboration]** : Augustine sharpens the contrast: the mind is well-trodden, revered, and nurtured while the soul lies cloistered and encrusted; the mind's interpretations of God and self are distorted, but the soul enlivened by Divine Love perceives truths the mind cannot reach. To forge a path to the soul is to forge a path to God.
- `two-paths` **[Elaboration]** : Lays out the choice directly: the natural love path brings a real but lesser closeness to God, while the Divine Love path brings a fulfillment the natural love cannot equal. "I wish for you to know these distinctions." Pre-dates the Andrew capstone (2016-05-02); chronology and argument agree.

### 2016-02-29-af-confucius | You Struggle but You Do Make Progress | 2016-02-29 [not publicly posted]
- `soul-expiation` **[Elaboration]** : Confucius reframes the painful "welling up from your souls" as the cleansing itself: releasing the burdens to God is real progress toward lasting joy. Frames the surfacing of buried pain as forward motion, not setback.
- `self-love-foundation` **[Elaboration]** : being loved by God as the ground of self-love, and self-love as what opens compassion for others: "You are precious to God, loved by God and you must start to love yourselves in this way. For when you can truly love yourselves you can look upon yourself with compassion, you can allow yourself to feel compassion and love for others and to be a great a mighty channel of Love in this world." The failure state is suppression, covering "your own soulful longings for Love and to love," which leaves the soul stuck until it acknowledges what it holds and lets God heal it.
- NOTE (witness, `judgment-to-love`): "thoughts of judgements and anger, fear" named among the human conditions that pull a soul from the Light, "this is when God makes effort to reach you to pull you back into the Light." Nothing here on judging others or the impulse to correct, so not a member.
- NOTE (context): the closing prayer, introduced with "This is the power of faith, my beloveds, to say:", is preserved as a standalone practice file (content/practices/prayer-2016-02-29-af-confucius.md).

### 2016-02-29-af-luke | Fighting Back the Darkness of the Earth Conditions | 2016-02-29 [not publicly posted]
- `earth-conditions-and-prayer` **[Foundation]** : Luke anchors the thread: the thoughts and actions of mankind generate a darkness-field that, like weather, comes in waves and buffets every soul, accounting for why one feels close to God one day and burdened the next; daily prayer neutralizes these conditions both within and around the soul, and the seeker must "fight" as a warrior of Light.
- `soul-expiation` **[Elaboration]** : Luke names the internal target of cleansing: "the accumulation and detritus of a life" - conditions not in harmony with God's Love - which prayer neutralizes as part of the soul's salvation toward at-onement. Touches the expiation thread, though the message's spine is the external earth-conditions argument.

### 2016-03-06-af-jesus | A Greeting | 2016-03-06 [not publicly posted]
- `vanguards-of-change` **[Elaboration]** : adds the requirement the commission imposes: release material burdens to have "the capacity and the flexibility to walk wherever God will guide you," compressed as "in this world but not of this world," with the change arriving "wave upon wave." The vanguard title recurs, tied directly to the unburdening.

### 2016-03-07-af-augustine | Sensitive Souls Are the Saviours of This World | 2016-03-07
- `sensitive-souls-redemptive` **[Foundation]** : anchors the thread. Given to a circle after the passing of a gentle man, whose swift progress in spirit is "often so for these gentle souls who find it so difficult to be in the world." The circle shares the sensitivity, and the claim follows: "it is the sensitive souls who are the saving grace in this world," those "who carry the pain of the knowledge of the cruelties of this world, but who overcome these," refusing "that place of judgment, fear and anger, error." The protection is stated, "you will not be hurt by the world... you are not vulnerable to the evil," and so is the redemptive example: "Your example will turn heads, will bring curiosity... many will be touched in some way as you sow these seeds of Love in the world."
- NOTE (sighting, `self-love-foundation`): "to love yourselves, to love all around you", already carried in that thread's roster rather than a distinct contribution here.
- NOTE (sighting, `judgment-to-love`): "to choose compassion over judgment," among the choices the sensitive soul makes; an adjacency, not a distinct contribution.

### 2016-03-10-af-augustine | Struggle of Soul and Mind | 2016-03-10 [not publicly posted]
- `mind-vs-soul` **[Elaboration]** : Augustine adds the practical decision-making dimension: facing a choice, the mind races through many plans and outcomes, but the seeker must go to the soul and ask there - choosing knowing over believing, seeing over creating a reality, peace over agitation. A soul-led step feels like gaining understanding, not losing control. Part of the tight Augustine mind/soul cluster with 2016-01-24 and 2016-02-15.

### 2016-03-10-af-confucius | Struggle of Mind | 2016-03-10 [not publicly posted]
- `mind-vs-soul` **[Elaboration]** : Confucius adds the free-will/willfulness angle: the mind's powers (free will, creative thought) burden the world when exerted without love; the soul must take precedence and inform the mind. Resolves not by rejecting the self but by strengthening the soul in Love through prayer. Companion to the same-day Augustine "Struggle of Soul and Mind."

### 2016-03-11-af-luke | Encrustations of the Soul | 2016-03-11 [not publicly posted]
- `soul-expiation` **[Reframe]** : Luke recasts the source of the soul's encrustations: they are NOT the mark of sin or depravity but simply the residue of having lived in this world, so the seeker is "in a way a victim," not to blame, and should meet their own cleansing with compassion. Distinct from the thread's other Reframe (2018-07-14, which recasts difficult times as greatest progress); this one reframes the cause of the conditions.
