Also gives the vivid mechanism: inner Light presses against the hardened shell, Divine Love raises the pressure, the cracks widen, the encrustations burst away.

### 2016-03-12-af-john-the-beloved | God's Touch in the World | 2016-03-12 [not publicly posted]
- `bell-toll-instrumentality` **[Elaboration]** : John teaches that prayers and loving intentions cause ripples that touch souls the one praying will never know of: "this is how change happens, true change happens by touching the souls of your brothers and sisters as God's channels." The same radiating-instrument mechanism as the bell toll, framed through prayer and Circles of Light. Names it the seeker's "greatest resource."

### 2016-03-16-af-jesus | The Resonance of Love Amongst You | 2016-03-16 [not publicly posted]
- `bell-toll-instrumentality` **[Chrysalis]** : Jesus opens the transformed vista the thread builds toward: once the soul sheds its conditions there is "no resistance, no fear, no concern for self, merely a passion, a passion so hot" to be "God's purveyor of Love, and in so doing this becoming the instrument of change in the world," continuing "for all eternity." Adds the loving-across-difference entry point the thread lacked, greet the soul not the surface, a channel of Love opens, the bond creates a Light, and God weaves the connections into a tapestry that changes the world soul to soul. -- logged as Chrysalis on the judgment that this is the vista rather than the mechanism; downgrade to Elaboration if it reads as the latter. Companion to the John the Beloved ripples teaching, 2016-03-12.
- `mind-vs-soul` **[Elaboration]** : Applies the distinction twice: in relationship, surface differences "are of the mind, yet the soul resonates with love," so one greets the soul beneath the foreign appearance; and in discernment, "the fantasies of your minds projecting how you will do God's Work will be replaced by a sure knowledge of your souls."
- `free-will-and-gods-plan` **[Elaboration]** : the plan worked out at the scale of one life. The willing soul is named as the means, "Every thought, every deed will be a part of this work, this flow, this great, great effort which is God's Plan for the salvation of mankind," and the surrender step is given in a form the thread does not otherwise hold, the seeker's own method for serving God superseded from within: "the fantasies of your minds projecting how you will do God's Work will be replaced by a sure knowledge of your souls, how you will proceed with each day. Each day will build upon the other and the other as you walk within God's Plan and serve within His Will." The unpredictability follows, "ways unanticipated," and "great works will be accomplished, surprising in their effects." The circuitous road is then denied the meaning a hearer would give it: "none of you are lost upon a circuitous road but each walks his road, guided by God and you shall accomplish what your heart desires." -- shares the fantasies passage with the `mind-vs-soul` Elaboration in this entry; the two are separable, that role resting on which faculty knows and this one on whose plan is followed. 2016-03-17-af-alec-gaunt rules out human plan-making for the world; this rules it out for the individual's own method.
- `provision-for-service` **[Elaboration]** : provision stated against a named work rather than as general assurance. "God provides for you all that you require to do so. He opens doors, many, many doors. He provides the means for you to travel to connect with one another and to connect with many others. He gives all that you require, my beloveds." The work is named, "you will expand your work which is God's work in the world," and so are the means of travel, a concrete case of the thread's claim about material provision, as at 2016-08-29-af-faith-nyquist. -- weigh this beside 2016-04-04-af-augustine in the Foundation section when the anchor is settled.
- NOTE (witness, `spiritual-fellowship`): what binds a circle, given as shared longing rather than shared belief, "in your small circle you each long for God, you long for His embrace, for His Love to flow into your souls and this desire that you share amongst you, my brethren, makes for a powerful bond which shall last for all eternity." 2016-11-28-af-luke carries the same claim developed, that the received Love is what makes sharing possible across real difference.
- NOTE (pattern, described and not penned): the tapestry as the structure the bonds compose, "as these connections of Light spread and multiply, God will weave a wondrous tapestry of Light surrounding this world in Light, bonding each soul to another, to another, multiplying and multiplying, changing the world in this way, soul to soul."
- NOTE (witness, `preparation-to-be-a-rock`): the readying named as such and given its content, "This is preparation for what is to come and we need for you, my beloveds, to be strong and clear, unfettered by error and the human condition, to be strong and powerful Lights in the world." -- promotion candidate at build; the readying is stated with content rather than in passing.
- NOTE (sighting, `soul-expiation`): the shedding in progress, "you are shedding those conditions which hold you back. It is happening in its own way for each of you and this shall continue for a while."
- NOTE (sighting, `incremental-journey`): "each day you will inch forward to that place of freedom," with the accumulation named, "Each day will build upon the other and the other."
- NOTE (sighting, `judgment-to-love`): judgment set aside as the precondition of the encounter, "put aside your thoughts of judgments, of reticence, of fear but greet the soul with love." The same passage carries the Chrysalis above and is not counted twice.

### 2016-03-17-af-alec-gaunt | You Must Be the Examples | 2016-03-17 [not publicly posted]
- `bell-toll-instrumentality` **[Elaboration]** : The most powerful tool for reaching others is not argument but exposing them to the experience of Divine Love and being the example oneself: "You must be the examples... at least half of that teaching is through your example." Truth is conveyed delicately, soul to soul, "in a way that will reach the soul of that individual." The teach-by-radiating-Love mechanism rather than by mental persuasion.
- `mind-vs-soul` **[Elaboration]** : Sets the soul-flow method against mind-structure: rather than "creating a structure within your mind which in its way restricts and contains the Will of God," one conveys "the wisdom that is inherent within this flow that comes from your souls." Names the daily work of strengthening "that channel of information that comes from your soul to your mind."
- `soul-expiation` **[Reframe]** : Recasts the felt slowness of soul awakening: even when the mind cannot reach soul-awareness, "this does not mean that your soul is dormant or not growing, or that the encrustations are not falling away." The vivid double-ended image - inner Light pressing out, prayer loosening the debris from within a clogged channel until "a great geyser of Light" breaks through. Reassurance that hidden progress is real, distinct from the thread's other Reframes (cause of conditions, 2016-03-11; difficult times as progress, 2018-07-14).
- `free-will-and-gods-plan` **[Elaboration]** : "You cannot devise a plan to save the world. This is not possible for any human being or even any angel. It is God's plan." The willing soul serves by surrendering ego and ambition into the flow of God's Will; Alec offers his own Earth-life ambition as the cautionary case. Companion to his fuller biographical treatment (2018-05-09).

### 2016-03-18-af-john-the-beloved | Talk on Service to God | 2016-03-18
- `free-will-and-gods-plan` **[Elaboration]** : Adds the pre-incarnation choice to the thread: "you have chosen this... Each one of you has incarnated into this world for this purpose. You chose this," paired with the willful-self-release move - "you continue to release that willful part of yourself and you continue to allow the knowing of the soul to eclipse the ideas of the mind." Frames alignment with God's Will as the joyful fulfilment of a choice the soul already made, not a surrender imposed from outside.
- `bell-toll-instrumentality` **[Elaboration]** : The subtle-instrumentality humility: "God uses you in ways that you do not even know... it may be very subtle as God uses you to touch others, to touch their souls," and as the channel clears, "you will find intense responses to your presence... many will be drawn to you." Carries the "chain of Love... a very long chain" image of blessing passing soul to soul. -- Companion to the John 2016-03-12 ripples teaching.

### 2016-03-25-af-augustine | Importance of Prayer | 2016-03-25 [not publicly posted]
- `prayer-soul-longing` **[Elaboration]** : Augustine affirms that sincerity is what counts: "each sincere prayer given to God will strike the mark and obtain a response from your Heavenly Father," offered "in earnest longing and clear intention." Sits between his own central distinction (2016-01-23, prayer as longing not recitation) and the Keea Atta Kem capstone (2018-07-30); confirms the soul-longing argument and ties prayer to clarifying the soul's purpose and growing the Light each time the circle gathers.
- NOTE (witness, `portals`): portal language in the circle's prayer, "this channel, this portal of Light in the world." Informal and metaphorical, unlike the developed teaching at 2018-11-21-af-james and 2019-01-26-af-seretta-kem. Not a chain member; useful for tracing how the word comes to its technical meaning (see the build note at 2016-08-25-af-brother-mandus).
- NOTE (context): a fourth charter candidate for the possible "Life of Prayer" collection in Open Questions, alongside 2016-01-23-af-augustine, 2018-07-30-af-keea-atta-kem, and 2015-11-28-af-faith-nyquist.

### 2016-03-27-af-seretta-kem | Mediumship, Communication and Rapport | 2016-03-27 [not publicly posted]
- `divine-love-mediumship` **[Foundation]** : A developed statement of the thread: how communication crosses from spirit to the mortal at all, and why it is never perfect. A truth held wordlessly in the soul travels up into the material mind, where the encrustations, biases, and fears narrow the passage, so every message is incomplete and no medium is perfect; as the soul grows the channel clears. Establishes the mechanism the thread's later entries build on (soul development governs reception quality; discernment; universality).
- `mind-vs-soul` **[Elaboration]** : Seretta Kem gives the communication mechanism: a truth held wordlessly in the soul must travel up into the material mind, where it is "filtered and distorted by those conditions within the mind" - the encrustations, biases, and fears narrow the passage, so what arrives is incomplete, not so much error as a partial picture. "The truths of the mind are transient and often illusionary but the truth of the soul is solid and can be relied upon." As the soul grows the channel clears, until "the mind capitulates and allows this greater, deeper consciousness to inhabit all aspects of the mortal." Applies the mind-soul distinction specifically to how knowing crosses from soul to consciousness; adjacent to the integration end-state the thread's Chrysalis candidates describe (2018-06-18, 2018-10-09), but framed through the communication channel rather than the merging itself.

### 2016-03-28-af-joseph | The Power of Commitment | 2016-03-28 [not publicly posted]
- `mind-vs-soul` **[Elaboration]** : Adds the complication-versus-simplicity angle: the mind "wishes to elaborate, to bring more complex ideas into the matter," forever "dancing in this desire to complicate," while "it is the soul that understands and accepts and is satisfied, fulfilled if you will, by the simple Truth." Ties the distinction to commitment - the seeker wavers because the mind complicates a simple truth the soul has already accepted - and looks toward the integrated end-state where "your souls will inform your mind," anticipating the thread's Chrysalis candidates without fully opening that vista. Joseph (earthly father of Jesus) frames it through his own Earth-life failure to commit to his son's simple Truths because "the tenets of my religion made it difficult."

### 2016-03-29-af-james | The Gift of Life | 2016-03-29
- `reincarnation-progression` **[Elaboration]** : Gives the positive side of the thread's incarnation doctrine without the reincarnation-correction polemic: "a soul incarnates into the world so that it may acquire identity, a sense of self, personality and expression of their soul... existing as an individuated soul," passing through this plane "very briefly" as "the first step in the flow of life that goes on and on." Same identity-acquisition purpose Aman states at the Foundation (2015-10-26, "incarnates once in the flesh to establish its identity, then continues its journey through many planes"), here framed as the gift of life rather than as a rebuttal to reincarnation. Strengthens the thread's account of why the soul takes flesh.
- `soul-purpose-emergence` **[Elaboration]** : Adds the framing of gifts ignited by God's Love to the know-your-true-self-and-express-your-gifts argument: "to know who you truly are is the greatest of gifts to yourself and the greatest gift of all is God's Love which ignites further aspects of your identity with the Gifts inherent within your soul." Consistent with the Augustine Foundation (2019-01-29); where Augustine stresses the slow gravitation toward one's purpose, James stresses the gifts already inherent in the soul being lit up and drawn out by Divine Love.
- NOTE (witness, `knowing-your-own-soul`): the value claim, "To know your souls, to know who you truly are is the greatest of gifts to yourself," stated inside a message about the gift of life and identity rather than argued.

### 2016-03-31-af-judas | The Purpose of Divine Love | 2016-03-31 [not publicly posted]
- `the-fall` **[Foundation]** : The refusal told from the offer's side, and stated as a rejection rather than a transgression. God placed "a reflection of God Himself in the form of a soul" in the first two human beings, gave them free will, and set them in an environment that "nurtured them fully," with the wisdom a pure soul carries. Then came a greater offer: "another Gift, a more beautiful Gift, the Gift of His Essence. Not the mere reflection of His Being, but the Essence of His Being." What defeated it was contentment, not disobedience: "it was rejected, for the happiness of those original beings was great and full in their existence as it was. And so they decided that they were indeed fulfilled in their lives as they were." The consequence is withdrawal rather than punishment, and it is temporary: "the Gift was withdrawn until another beautiful soul was incarnated into this world free of sin and error." No serpent, no fruit, no curse, no expulsion. The doctrinal weight sits on the fact that the offer was declined by souls who were doing well, which makes the refusal a live possibility rather than a historical mistake.
- `redemption-universal` **[Testimony]** : Judas names his own motive without softening it: "the essence of my betrayal to my dear and beloved brother was a naïve perception that he would turn the tables upon the oppressors and become the ruler of our land, a true king. You can imagine my horror at what happened." He calls Jesus "my dear and beloved brother" throughout and speaks from inside the Celestial company. What the thread gets here is the mechanism of the betrayal rather than only the fact of the redemption: it was a political misreading, not malice, which is a harder and more useful case than a monster redeemed.
- `who-jesus-was` **[Testimony]** : Why Jesus was different, from someone who could not work it out at the time: "We saw embodied within our dear brother great power and wisdom, love and affection, gifts of healing and insight and perceptions, which we did not have. And we glorified our brother, we put him upon a pedestal and we saw him as different and far above us. But we did not understand why he was different." The pedestal is named as the disciples' own error, which locates the beginning of the divinity misunderstanding among the people closest to him. His purpose is stated as reopening the offer: the Gift "was offered to Him and he accepted for he knew that this was his purpose in the world to bring this Gift again to humanity." Testimony rather than Elaboration: the facet is first-person experience offered as evidence, a disciple naming his own misunderstanding, and it is that section of the thread which answers why the record needed correcting at all.
- `conduit-not-scripture` **[Elaboration]** : Judas speaks of the book bearing his name as a collaboration he is still involved with: "I have been with this instrument often when he reads the book which I collaborated in writing... I am well satisfied with the efforts that we have made in this publication which is indeed meant to be widely distributed." A spirit describing his own published text as something he participated in producing, and continuing to attend its reading, is the thread's claim demonstrated rather than argued. -- open question for build: if the thread's argument is specifically that no text is scripture, this may be a witness, since the passage shows the conduit relationship without denying scriptural status. Demote if the registry spine is narrower than this reading assumes.
- `free-will-and-gods-plan` **[Elaboration]** : Grounds the free-will premise in the design of the soul itself. God gave free will so that the soul's reflection of Him would be "full and rich," and made the Essence a choice rather than an imposition: "there is no sure way to bring another to these living waters and to get them to drink, for God gave each soul free will. God allowed each soul to choose. And so it is, my beloved friends, and so it shall always be - a choice." The plan is that everyone be offered, not that everyone accept: "That all humanity will be given this choice and you are a part of God's plan to bring this choice to humanity." The history the message narrates, the Gift offered, declined, restored through Jesus and held open since, is that freedom working itself out across generations.
- NOTE (witness, `pentecost`): Judas gives his own account of the event: the grief, the reappearance, the instruction to pray, and the result. "He reappeared (to us) and again reiterated his message and beseeched us to pray for this Love, this Love Divine. Yes, my brothers and sisters came together in prayer and received the Pentecost, it changed them. It gave them the insight which Jesus longed to show us in his ministry." -- adds nothing the Foundation and Testimony do not carry, but it is an independent account of the same event; keep for corroboration.
- NOTE (witness, `divine-love-mediumship`): the reason the truth cannot be demonstrated, "You can perceive the manifestations of it, you can feel the power of it, but can you touch it and see it? No, my friends, it is at the soul, it is a power within the soul. It must be experienced in an awakening."
- NOTE (witness, `bell-toll-instrumentality`): the telling done without words, "may your smile and the twinkle in your eyes reflect the great Truth of God's Love that resides within your soul. In this way you speak Truth."
- NOTE (sighting, `incremental-journey`): the one-way claim, "you cannot turn back on this path, you only can move forward."
- NOTE (sighting, `soul-purpose-emergence`): purpose stated at the level of creation rather than the individual, "this was God's intended purpose for every soul to receive this Gift of Love, to come to that place of at-onement."
- NOTE (sighting, `divine-love-history`): the message lays out the whole arc of Divine Love, the soul as God's individuated reflection placed in the first parents, the Essence offered and rejected, withdrawn, restored by Jesus, confirmed at Pentecost, and held open to every soul as a perpetual free choice. -- decide at build whether that arc is an argument in its own right or simply the sum of `the-fall`, `who-jesus-was` and `pentecost`.
- NOTE (sighting, `comfort-dulls-longing`): the first parents refused the Essence because they were content, "the happiness of those original beings was great and full in their existence as it was." Contentment, not sin, is what declines the gift, stated here as cosmology.

### 2016-04-02-af-jesus | Jesus' Ministry and the Teachings of Divine Love | 2016-04-02
- `religious-error` **[Elaboration]** : the correction of the vicarious atonement (the belief that Jesus' death paid for the sins of humanity), given by Jesus himself: "nothing could be further from the truth that my death and my blood could save humanity," an interpretation that "has wrought much pain and error in the world." It stands beside Mary's eyewitness correction (1963-12-26-ds-mary, "not as a blood sacrifice") and the Testimonies of the reformers, and here the correction comes from the person the doctrine is about. Presumptive anchor material for the atonement step of the argument.
- `who-jesus-was` **[Elaboration]** : major record material: he incarnated "with a unique attribute of having a purified soul," protected by God from the conditions of the world; his purpose was "to re-open the door of His Love"; he taught in allegory and stories; and he interprets his own scripture: "When I say, 'Seek ye the Kingdom' I mean, seek God's Love, which will create the Kingdom within you."
- `two-paths` **[Elaboration]** : where the natural path ends, stated sharply: natural-path souls "will perfect every aspect of their being, but they will not go beyond this attainment. Their road ends where your road begins. And your road goes on for all eternity, for God's Love is infinite." Adds the soul's unlimited capacity and the will as the only limit. Read with 2016-05-02-af-andrew, the Capstone.
- `mind-vs-soul` **[Elaboration]** : the migration doctrine: consciousness migrates "bit by bit" to soul awareness; the soul mind "begins to operate with the infusion of God's Love"; and the endpoint claim that "an angel redeemed in Love does not require a material mind." NOTE for build: this sits in sequence with, not against, the 60/40 harmony teaching (2018-05-17), "your mind will find its place within this new world and be in harmony" is the mortal integration; dispensability is the redeemed angel's estate. The Chrysalis section will want these ordered as stages.
- NOTE (sighting, `soul-faculties`): the dormant seed "igniting all the faculties and perceptions" at God's touch.
- NOTE (sighting, `divine-love-without-knowing`): "first they must know and understand what it is that they yearn for", the why-teach complement to the without-knowing doctrine.
- NOTE (witness, `incremental-journey`): "Bit by bit... slowly but surely" and "This comes gradually."

### 2016-04-04-af-augustine | God Provides for Those Who Serve* | 2016-04-04
- `free-will-and-self-responsibility` **[Elaboration]** : draws out what follows from the law that each soul is responsible for itself: some people "stubbornly hold on to what they carry" when "they may indeed find healing and respite and an unburdening", keeping their condition by choice, and therefore "you are not required to have a sense of responsibility towards their choice, for each soul is responsible for what they choose, just as you are responsible for what you choose." Where 2019-02-22-af-augustine turns the law inward, on the seeker's own healing, this message turns it outward: the same law that makes your healing yours releases you from carrying theirs.
- `provision-for-service` **[Foundation]** : the developed statement of God's provision for guided work, provided "on every level, including a monetary level"; worry, guilt, and conflict as barriers we raise ourselves; provision meeting what is needed, not indulgence; harmony as the sign of God's Hand upon the work. Presumptive section anchor, and the anchor is not settled: 2016-03-16-af-jesus also states the claim, against a named work, so both belong in the Foundation section and are weighed at build. 2016-08-19-af-andrew supplies why the barriers work.
- NOTE (context): "the high road of which our master speaks", Jesus referenced mid-message (spirits field). Also strong flow language, "to walk in that flow, to be carried by that flow," which bears on the Alignment with Divine Flow subject.

### 2016-04-12-af-andrew | Be the Purveyors of Joy | 2016-04-12 [not publicly posted]
- `bell-toll-instrumentality` **[Elaboration]** : names the perceptible form the radiating signal takes: joy is the carrier that does the drawing ("They feel the outward expression of Love through your joy and they are drawn to this Light"), and what draws them is the wish to possess that lightness themselves. -- Closest member 2016-01-18-af-confucius (the mechanism working unawares) and 2016-03-17-af-alec-gaunt (the seeker as example); Andrew supplies the visible channel by which the signal is actually read.
- NOTE (sighting, `affect-as-soul-marker`): joy as a sign by which the soul's condition can be known ("it tells you that all is right within you, that you are in harmony"), and joy as the soul's lasting state once it has been touched by the Love.

### 2016-04-12-af-keea-atta-kem | Talk on Receiving Guidance | 2016-04-12 [not publicly posted]
- `conduit-not-scripture` **[Foundation]** : establishes the conduit claim: the angels make "a rapport with your minds and your souls" and the rapport continues past delivery, "We are with you as you read them and we are with all who read them and help to impress upon you the importance of any particular message," attended to each reader's particular life. "All of it is a training, an education, a direction to your lives."
- NOTE (witness, `divine-love-mediumship`): the rapport-and-influence claim, stated in two sentences inside a broader talk; does not develop the thread's mechanism or discernment strands; held at witness level (revised down from Elaboration during batch review).

### 2016-04-12-af-moses | Lesson on the Falsity of the Vicarious Atonement and the Unsure Nature of Written Truth | 2016-04-12
- `religious-error` **[Objection-removed]** : Moses repudiates the Vicarious Atonement from inside the tradition that produced it. He names the practice as his own people's: "In our Jewish culture and religion, shedding of blood was a common attempt to appease God," and gives the reason it was done, "we feared God and in many ways we blamed God for our mishaps." He then traces the Christian doctrine directly to it: "when our brother Jesus, a Jew, and a prophet, and the Messiah, was put to death, the concept of the blood having power to wash the sins of humanity arose from these old beliefs." The denial covers both the original practice and the doctrine built on it: "They are entirely erroneous, even when we practiced in the old days this sacrifice, it had no effect in our world and still today, the idea of the sacrifice of our dear brother has had no effect and will never have any effect upon the souls of men." What replaces it is personal responsibility: "each soul, each man, each woman must be responsible for their actions."
- `religious-error` **[Objection-removed]** : The scripture claim, which is the larger of the two repudiations. Again from inside: "We, the Jews, took great import upon the written word. We felt that the written word was the Word of God." His verdict on the Bible is neither dismissal nor endorsement, and he is careful about the distinction: there is truth in it, "There are moral laws which, if heeded, will bring a soul to a place of purity of action," but "many of the words written within this document have little or no consequence to the progression of a soul," and the whole "is not entirely false nor is it entirely true. It is old concepts and understandings from many, many years ago which have been transmuted into erroneous beliefs." He adds a practical warning: "an individual living in this present time would have a great deal of difficulty discerning Truth from this document." -- logged as a second Objection-removed line rather than merged, since the two repudiations are independent and either could stand without the other.
- `conduit-not-scripture` **[Objection-removed]** : Clears the belief that would distort the conduit claim into bibliolatry. Names the error from the inside, denies it, "the word is not sacred. No, words are words," relocates the sacredness, "What is sacred is your belief and connection, your rapport with your Heavenly Father," and then preserves the conduit rather than discarding it: "to bring a suggestion of Truth in the written word, for that is merely all that it is, is a productive action." That last clause is what makes it a member here and not only of `religious-error`. -- given the same evening as the thread's Foundation (2016-04-12-af-keea-atta-kem), to the same circle, both turning on the word rapport; the pairing looks deliberate.
- `conduit-not-scripture` **[Elaboration]** : The positive doctrine behind the denial. Truth is not a fixed object to be transmitted but something perceived: "Truth is not altogether absolute. It is perceived by each individual soul, and although the foundation of Truth can be universal, the perception of Truth is not necessarily so." From that follows the tolerance of difference, "though their truth, their understanding, their perspective may be slightly different from yours, it is no less valid," and the futility of codification, "to try and standardize Truth with words in a book is a fruitless effort." He includes himself and his own kind in the limitation: "You do not know the Absolute Truth, my beloveds... And we, who have tread this road for thousands of years, continue to evolve in our understanding of Truth." He also describes how truth actually arrives, soul first and mind second, incrementally: it "will emerge from your souls, find lodgment within your minds, and will incrementally develop and unfold and evolve." -- this is the thread's account of how truth is known, stated outright, and likely to be load-bearing at build.
- `judgment-to-love` **[Elaboration]** : Applies the thread to the specific case of someone who is wrong, and rules against correcting them. "Do not confront others and say: 'you are wrong'. No, my beloveds, you must merely live your truth and speak your truth in loving ways." He gives the reason from the hearer's side, "most often these precious souls who mean well are not open to your truth," and from the seeker's own limits, since none of them holds absolute truth either. He also draws the one boundary, which keeps this from becoming passivity: "there are times when a gross error, when those around you speak blatant error and express profound negativity, in these times you must confront error... for you must never enter a negative condition unless you make an effort to bring greater Light." So silence is the rule for honest error and speech is required against active darkness. -- the exception clause is the useful part; a thread arguing against judgment needs to say where its limit is, and few messages do.
- NOTE (witness, `redemption-universal`): error delays but does not prevent arrival, "though they have been shown a false path, and possibly will walk a circuitous route to Truth, they will come to God in time, and you must not put roadblocks to that journey."
- NOTE (witness, `two-paths`): both loves count toward progression, which is unusually generous for this thread, "the love that shines through - whether it be the Natural Love or the Divine Love, all loves, which will have a bearing upon the path of that individual in their spiritual life." -- bears on the natural-love rehabilitation watch (see Open questions in chains-threads): Moses does not rank natural love lower here, he includes it. Whether it counts is the curator's decision, since the passage is about progression generally rather than about the two paths as such.
- NOTE (witness, `bell-toll-instrumentality`): "It is by the Love and the manifestations of God's blessings through you that you will touch others. It is not through argument or correction or judgement."
- NOTE (witness, `free-will-and-gods-plan`): the plan needs people and the person need not understand their part in it, "God will guide you to those who are seeking and open and who will receive, even those with false beliefs, will receive something from God through you. It is not for you to understand in a complete way what that is."
- NOTE (sighting, `incremental-journey`): truth itself arrives incrementally, "will incrementally develop and unfold and evolve." Same passage as the `conduit-not-scripture` Elaboration above; recorded once.

### 2016-04-13-af-seretta-kem | The Greatest Healing Energy of All Is God's Divine Love | 2016-04-13
- `divine-love-healing` **[Elaboration]** : the second developing witness that returned the thread from the holding pen (checkpoint resolved at full text; the message is already in the repository). Restates the Foundation's claim in its own terms (the one blessing which "cannot be given from one to another"), then develops where the Foundation does not go: affirms the other healing modalities as real and usable, places Divine Love among them without demoting them ("This Gift enhances all other gifts"), grounds the primacy in eternality ("what else in the universe of God's creation is eternal? Nothing, my beloveds, only this Touch"), and instructs practitioners to "Add this to your understanding."
- NOTE (sighting, `prayer-soul-longing`): "it is in the asking and the longing that it is given," with the prayer quoted in full; the heart of the thread given as an instruction, without being developed.

- NOTE (sighting, `bell-toll-instrumentality`): "many souls will be touched by your example"; echo only, Francis already carries the developed Testimony at 2016-01-28-af-francis-of-assisi.

### 2016-04-19-af-goldie | Be with God in the Garden | 2016-04-19 [not publicly posted]

### 2016-05-02-af-andrew | Two Paths Towards Love and Purification | 2016-05-02
- `two-paths` **[Capstone]** : Andrew picks up directly from Mary, who had just spoken in the same circle, and gives the thread its complete doctrinal statement. The premise: "when you pass over into spirit your soul has the memory of every minute and second of your life," and those memories flood into consciousness. The fork, stated as a choice: "there are two choices in dealing with these deep-seated injuries within the soul." Natural path, by full conscious recompense: "each and every aspect of these memories lodged within the soul must come up into your consciousness and depending upon the nature of it... one must atone for these words spoken, for injuries received and for injuries put upon another... All souls must recompense for these actions and in many respects, the journey through the spheres of spirit are determined by how one willingly takes on these great efforts to purify, to release and to make recompense." Divine path, by transformation: "to have these conditions within the soul healed, transformed and released through the power of God's Love. This can be a very quick and powerful journey... burning away all those conditions that are not in harmony with the Love." The choice named as such: "it is the choice to take this journey of recompense or to take the journey of faith and love." The Celestial-reach claim with its exponential mechanism: "as you release those conditions that are pushed out by this Love... you are then more free to receive greater inflowings of the Love and your progression becomes exponential and your journey swift to those spheres of the Celestial Heavens where each soul is redeemed." And the facet that makes this the Capstone rather than another Elaboration, the ceiling on the natural road: "that place within the sixth sphere of existence is the very limit and apex of these efforts to purify the natural being, that which you were born with, the potentials and gifts and possibilities of the natural soul," against which the Divine road is unbounded: "there are unlimited possibilities, for God's Love, this energy, this Gift is unlimited. The potential for your soul to receive this Gift is unlimited." Closes on the open invitation the registry spine ends with: "It is free to you for the asking. It merely requires that you choose, that you choose the gift of Love." Fourteen months after the Foundation (2015-03-27-af-andrew, same spirit), which lays the premise and the fork but carries neither the compensation machinery nor the sixth-sphere ceiling.
- `soul-expiation` **[Elaboration]** : Supplies the spirit-side account of the process the thread otherwise describes from the Earth side: what is not released here surfaces there in full, memory by memory, and must be recompensed. Adds the total-recall premise ("the memory of every minute and second of your life") and the reason mental work does not substitute for it: "though many feel that they have dealt with these issues through their mental reckonings and what you refer to as therapy, I say that within the mind these issues are settled but within the soul they are not." Closest members: 2016-05-09-af-augustine (the three origins of the conditions) and 2018-07-14-af-seretta-kem (Reframe, the difficult passage as where progress is made). Andrew supplies the deferred-cost argument, the conditions travel with the soul and are paid for either way.
- `mind-vs-soul` **[Elaboration]** : A facet no current member carries: mental resolution counterfeits soul resolution, so an issue can be genuinely settled in the mind while untouched in the soul, "within the mind these issues are settled but within the soul they are not," with therapy named as the instance. Distinct from 2018-12-17-af-mary (Objection-removed: the mind counterfeits soul *consciousness*, so inner awareness is mistaken for soul knowing); Andrew's counterfeit is of *healing* rather than of awareness, and it carries a consequence Mary's does not, that the unresolved condition surfaces in spirit and must be paid for. Fence the two at build so the counterfeit facet is not collapsed into one.
- `redemption-universal` **[Elaboration]** : States the universal end point for those who refuse the short road: "for those who have led a difficult life here on Earth, whether of their own choosing or have been victimized by others and carry the hurt and the pain, the resentment and the anger but choose not to go to God with these issues and conditions, they choose a long road, a difficult road, but they will in time come to that place of purification, they will in time, for this is part of God's intention for all humanity to come to a place of purity." That is the thread's conclusion stated as God's intention rather than as a possibility, and it arrives by its own route: not "no soul is beyond reach" but "every soul in fact arrives." -- open question for build: if other members already state that every soul is sure to arrive, demote this to witness.
- NOTE (witness, `death-transition`): the condition-determines-your-place mechanism, "the journey through the spheres of spirit are determined by how one willingly takes on these great efforts to purify," and "your journey of life upon this Earth-plane is short but has a great bearing upon where you will continue life in spirit." Same facet as 2019-02-10-af-augustine (Elaboration) and the 2016-05-11-af-james-padgett witness. Andrew's variant makes the determinant willingness to recompense rather than soul condition as such; a shading, not a new facet.
- NOTE (witness, `fear-of-full-immersion`): "Do not hold back from God, but open yourself completely to His Touch and allow His healing Hand to release you from your burdens." The holding-back named without the fear diagnosed; 2016-08-15-af-alec-gaunt supplies the diagnosis. Witness rather than sighting because the holding back is the behaviour the thread exists to explain, stated here in the imperative.
- NOTE (sighting, `short-life-long-road`): "your journey of life upon this Earth-plane is short but has a great bearing upon where you will continue life in spirit." Andrew calls it "a great advantage here on Earth": as you pray for this Love and it shapes your choices, "you are creating for yourself a wonderful heritage of Light," and "in the world of spirit, life is much, much longer." The same message gives the other side, the "long road, a difficult road" of those who do not take their conditions to God (see the `redemption-universal` line above).
- NOTE (build, `incremental-journey`): "your progression becomes exponential and your journey swift" sits against the thread's spine, growth advancing in leaps then trickles, two steps forward and one back. Probably reconcilable by location, the incremental pattern describing the Earth-side struggle against the mind and world, the exponential describing progression in spirit once conditions are released. Recorded so the apparent conflict is visible at build rather than discovered there. Not logged as a member.
- NOTE (build, `two-paths`): Andrew opens by affirming Mary, who spoke immediately before in the same circle, "the dear and beloved soul Mary speaks the truth and it is true that the law of compensation is transformed in its effects upon the soul who is redeemed by the Father's Love." The companion message 2016-05-02-af-mary is already logged as a same-day `two-paths` witness for this Capstone's section; the opening line makes the pairing explicit and the two should be read together at build, Mary stating the claim and Andrew supplying the machinery beneath it.

Closest member 2016-03-11-af-luke (one origin, blame removed); 2018-04-03-af-luke develops the inherited strand alone; Augustine sets the three side by side.

### 2016-05-29-af-aman | Aman Speaks of the Original State of Humanity and the Fall from Grace | 2016-05-29
- `the-fall` **[Foundation]** : The refusal told by the one who made it. Aman gives the original state in physical detail: bodies "perfectly proportioned, lean and fit," taller than people today, "without flaws or disease," minds "keen and clear," souls "untainted with sin," no need to forage or eat except for pleasure, "in part ethereal in our spiritual and physical existence." God and His angels were companions, teaching them to build shelters they did not need. Then the offer, put as a question: "We were asked if we would care to receive a further gift of His Love, the essence of His soul into our hearts so that we may progress further in our natures and happiness." He does not claim to fully understand the refusal, "The great mystery is why we chose not to receive this gift," but he names two causes and they are precise. Self-absorption: "we were so enamoured with our own beings that such a gift seemed unnecessary." And then the thing itself, which he calls the first sin and which is not disobedience: "We felt suspicion. This was our first sin and it grew into a dark canker upon our souls." The fall follows as consequence rather than punishment, and it is physical as well as moral: pain felt for the first time, then "anger and resentment," the dispensation lost, and "our bodies reflected this choice as we became more flesh and blood than spirit and grace." The arc closes with the second chance taken: after passing into spirit they regained the sixth sphere, and "it was not until after the coming of Jesus and the reopening of the doors to the Father's Love that we were able to choose, this time more wisely, the way of the Divine Love." -- 2016-03-31-af-judas describes the refusal; Aman made it, and gives the fuller account in the first person. Reopen the anchor question at build: on this reading Aman anchors, and Judas becomes the Foundation-section member that supplies the theological frame.
- `the-fall` **[Foundation]** : Aman's own verdict on the choice, which only he can give. He does not excuse it, "I have always carried deep regrets about how we chose to turn away from God," and he accepts the consequences as his: "I cannot condemn those who are immersed in darkness because I sowed the seeds to your fate many years ago." But he also reads the refusal as close to inevitable given what God had already given them: "in my own soul wisdom I see that this choice was almost a forgone conclusion as God gifted all with free will…..a most powerful Gift." That is a claim about free will itself, that a gift real enough to matter is a gift real enough to be used wrongly, and it converts the fall from an accident into a demonstration of what free will costs.
- `free-will-and-self-responsibility` **[Elaboration]** : The consequences of choice stated at the scale of history rather than of a life: "all of humanity must come to recognize the power of this Gift and how it can lead to decisions which may have profound effects upon yourselves and others," and "Millennia upon millennia of disharmonious choices and unloving acts have made this perfect Earth into a howling mass of unloved and injured souls." The diagnosis of the present world is not misfortune but alignment: "The world is rife with sin and error because the children of God do not desire to be in alignment with Him and His laws of creation."
- `two-paths` **[Testimony]** : One soul travelling both roads, told in sequence. Aman refuses the Divine Love, falls, lives a human life, dies, works his way up the natural path to "our previous exalted state within the sixth sphere or natural heavens," and then, once Jesus reopens the door, takes the other road: "we were able to choose, this time more wisely, the way of the Divine Love." The sixth sphere is named as the ceiling reached and then passed, which is the thread's own architecture demonstrated by a single soul across the whole span.
- `redemption-universal` **[Testimony]** : the case made by the speaker's existence. The man who refused God's Essence at the beginning of the world speaks from the Celestial Kingdom and prays for the circle's success, having taken the other road once the door reopened, "we were able to choose, this time more wisely, the way of the Divine Love." Where Judas is the soul who betrayed the Master, this is the soul who declined the Gift at the start, so the thread holds its two extreme cases: the betrayal of the Master and the refusal at the beginning. -- the redemption is a closing clause in a message whose subject is the fall, which is why it is a section member rather than the anchor.
- NOTE (witness, `vanguards-of-change`): "You who are attempting to turn this tide are brave and blessed warriors who are moving in Light given by God to assist you in your efforts to bring change."
- NOTE (sighting, `earth-conditions-and-prayer`): the world's state traced to accumulated choice rather than to any single cause, in the millennia passage above; recorded once.
- NOTE (sighting, `comfort-dulls-longing`): the refusal attributed directly to how good things already were. Aman's circumstances were perfect by his own account, and the gift "seemed unnecessary" because of it. This is the same claim as 2016-03-31-af-judas ("the happiness of those original beings was great and full") and 2017-05-11-af-jesus (a comfortable circle, needs met, longing insufficient), now from the person it happened to. -- the claim runs through cosmology (2016-03-31-af-judas), pastoral diagnosis (2017-05-11-af-jesus), and here first-person testimony.
- NOTE (build, `the-fall`): this message bears directly on the contentment-versus-ambition tension. Aman names contentment and suspicion, not ambition; the desire for power that Joseph Smith calls the fall's source appears here only afterwards, as part of the degeneration ("we chose to follow our own wills ever more deeply into a confused and darkened state"). That ordering would reconcile the two accounts: contentment and suspicion caused the refusal, and the appetite for power characterises what the fall produced. Recorded as evidence for the open question rather than as a resolution.

### 2016-05-30-af-augustine | Staying in the Light | 2016-05-30 [not publicly posted]
- `earth-conditions-and-prayer` **[Elaboration]** : names the prayer's effect as lawful: "a law is enacted by these prayers, when you earnestly long for the Father's Love and so you bring the angels close," and frames the gathered circle as a constructed respite from the field ("this respite from the human condition"). Luke (2016-02-29, Foundation) establishes the field and that prayer meets it; Augustine supplies the mechanism, a law enacted by earnest longing, and adds the circle as collective defence rather than only individual.
- NOTE (sighting, `incremental-journey`): the oscillation and the ratchet ("with each choice for Light you reinforce this Path, you make it easier for you to make this choice"), the facet carried by 2019-04-04-af-matthew; place beneath it at build.

### 2016-06-04-af-confucius | Be Strong in the Light and Withstand the Challenges | 2016-06-04
- NOTE (sighting, `sensitive-souls-redemptive`): touches the protection facet from a different side: "when your soul glows with this Light and when you serve mankind... you become a target for negativity and you must ensure that you are protected and strong in Light." The thread's spine holds the indwelling Love as itself the protection, passive and by law, and sensitivity as strength; this calls for active daily self-protection under targeting. Not forced in as a member; the facet belongs to `opposition-to-the-light-worker`, which this message now anchors.
- `opposition-to-the-light-worker` **[Foundation]** : presumptive section anchor. The thread's original statement, opposition as organized and the daily fortress of prayer built against it, with the posture qualified so the practice is not fear: "there are many forces that conspire in many different ways, on many different levels to draw you out of the Light... not in a way of fear... but in a way that you reinforce the Light within yourselves."

### 2016-06-04-af-marjorie-fike | Your Loved Ones Live On in Spirit | 2016-06-04
- NOTE (sighting, `death-transition`): the continuity doctrine enacted in a personal message rather than taught ("though they are not with you they are still alive, experiencing life in a different way... whether it is here in spirit or in the flesh, it does not matter really"). Close in genre to the 2019-04-08-mc-mary Testimony but adds no doctrinal facet; its distinctive weight is pastoral, the griever's own healing, comparable to the 2018-09-29-af-goldie memorial NOTE.
- NOTE (sighting, `self-love-foundation`): "the weight of your lack of self love" named as part of what must be released for healing; witness level, and as a personal message not an anchor candidate.

### 2016-06-06-af-john-the-beloved | Continue on This Path | 2016-06-06

- NOTE (sighting, `prayer-soul-longing`): "May you all find what your deep soul longings long for and already know"; the already-knows nuance stated in one clause; sighting against the Foundation (2015-11-28-af-faith-nyquist).

### 2016-06-27-af-faith-nyquist | You Are a Harbinger of Love | 2016-06-27

Read with 2016-03-12-af-james, 2016-03-16-af-jesus, 2016-08-02-af-augustine, and 2016-08-19-af-andrew, which argues the principle as a step toward `provision-for-service`.
- NOTE (sighting, `free-will-and-gods-plan`): "God is waiting for you to be in alignment with His Will... each soul will manifest God's Will and this great Grace in unique and beautiful ways"; asserted, witness level. The unique-manifestation facet is also close to `soul-purpose-emergence`.

### 2016-06-27-af-jesus | Love with God's Heart of Love | 2016-06-27
- NOTE (sighting, `mind-vs-soul`): "revelations come and understanding blossoms without the struggle of the mind but the knowing of the soul"; the thread's core restated in brief; witness level. The message is short and primarily a blessing; light coverage is deliberate.

### 2016-07-04-af-jesus | Receiving Guidance | 2016-07-04 [not publicly posted]
- `divine-love-mediumship` **[Elaboration]** : Jesus states the fidelity ceiling: guidance through a medium is "often colored by that individual and represents an approximation of what is intended to be given," explicitly not error ("This is not to say that what comes through is in error, for this is not true"), but "the complete truth cannot be given through a soul that is not entirely cleansed and purified by the Love and at the level of a celestial angel," and "there are no perfect mediums in this world." Closest member 2016-03-27-af-seretta-kem (Foundation, the conditions that make reception clear); new facet: the ceiling on clarity, and the instrument's colouring reframed as approximation rather than error, the premise the discernment strand rests on. Anticipates the 2019-01-30-af-jesus Testimony by two and a half years, in Jesus' own voice.
- `judgment-to-love` **[Elaboration]** : speaks to the one on the receiving end of criticism: "You must love all those who would criticise you... for I, too, was the object of such ridicule when I walked the Earth," and places the harm in what the seeker allows: "It is only when you allow the criticism of others, the negative thoughts of others, to bruise you... that you allow negativity to enter into your being." Closest member 2018-06-26-af-matthew; new facet: permission as the point where darkness enters, stated as a law (negativity gets in only when we allow it), along with Jesus' own experience of being ridiculed. The 2017-06-15-af-augustine Capstone also speaks to being judged ("When someone is judging you... say a prayer for them. Do not allow these conditions to infect your own mind."), so what this message adds is the law of permission, not the receiving end as such.
- `earth-conditions-and-prayer` **[Elaboration]** : denies that outer calm exists: "You cannot hope to sail your ship upon the calm waters for they do not exist in this world, only in that place between you and your Heavenly Father is there that great and deep peace." Closest member 2016-02-29-af-luke (Foundation); new facet: the expectation of calm waters is itself the error, and the only still water is interior, from which the seeker becomes "a calm centre in these rough waters."
- NOTE (sighting, `bell-toll-instrumentality`): "a beacon of Light and hope" in one sentence; asserted, not developed.
- NOTE (considered and set aside, `guidance-as-seed`): checked and NOT a match; that candidate concerns guidance arriving inwardly as a seed of thought, this message concerns guidance arriving through a human instrument. Negative on the record.

### 2016-07-11-af-confucius | Pray Daily | 2016-07-11 [not publicly posted]
- `prayer-soul-longing` **[Elaboration]** : adds the cadence requirement and its cost: "You must take the time, make the effort, say the prayers, sit in silence to receive God's answers to the longings of your souls," measured as "at least one portion of your day," and without the daily Touch "you become lost in the human condition, in the ebb and flow of all the disparate energies of this world." Closest member 2016-01-23-af-augustine (prayer as longing, not recitation); new facet: no existing member makes the daily interval itself the argument or states the cost of omitting it.
- NOTE (witness, `mind-vs-soul`): the claim that what the soul takes in lasts ("that which you entertain within your minds... is transient and changeable, but that which you absorb within your souls... will remain forever"). Held at witness because 2016-07-18-af-martin-luther, an Elaboration, makes the same point about what lasts as a whole-message argument, and each facet takes one member.
- NOTE (sighting, `earth-conditions-and-prayer`): one thin real facet, the disruptive field partly self-generated ("in many ways you bring your beings out of harmony through all of these pursuits and distractions"); promote only if the self-generated-scatter strand recurs.
- NOTE (context): a possible founding message for a "Life of Prayer" collection; it argues the daily discipline specifically. See the "Life of Prayer" note in Open questions in chains-threads.md.

### 2016-07-13-af-seretta-kem | Expiation and Purification of the Soul | 2016-07-13 [not publicly posted]
- `soul-expiation` **[Elaboration]** : widens the field: purification is tri-level ("what you require to be whole in body, spirit and within your souls," with blessings that "cause your bodies to expiate toxins"), and adds a second source of what must go, material that never originated in the seeker: "you are all sensitive people, you all feel things intensely and you absorb many energies in the world and these also must be released." Closest member 2016-02-29-af-confucius; new facet: the thread's members treat expiation as release of the soul's own accumulated conditions; this extends it to body and spirit body and to absorbed external energies. Elaboration over Reframe: extends rather than recasts.
- NOTE (witness, `divine-love-healing`): develops what the healing does (cleansing reaching body, spirit, and soul together) without the comparative claim that defines the thread; roster witness.

### 2016-07-18-af-martin-luther | Pursuit of Truth | 2016-07-18 [not publicly posted]
- `religious-error` **[Elaboration]** : supplies both the reason the written record cannot hold and the faculty that succeeds it: "your journey is to understand truth in your relationship with God," and the mentally assembled paradigm is serially superseded, "do not rely upon this chimera of understanding for as you journey forward it will disappear and something else will emerge," so the remedy is not a better doctrine but a different faculty. Closest member 2016-04-12-af-moses (Objection-removed); distinct from Luther's own 2015-06-21 sincerity facet. Testimony considered, but Augustine 2015-03-28 already holds it; Elaboration the modest fit.
- `mind-vs-soul` **[Elaboration]** : the durability axis as the message's spine: mental constructions are "but a shadow of truth," "the mind, the material mind, is incapable of knowing it in this way," against "that great mind that lies within your soul, activated." Closest member 2017-08-21-af-matthew (the mind cannot articulate what the soul knows); new facet: the mind's constructions are not merely inadequate but impermanent and self-replacing, which is why they cannot serve as a foundation. The adjacent 2016-07-11-af-confucius durability claim stands as a witness beneath this entry.
- NOTE (sighting, `soul-faculties`): names the soul's perceptions and the soul mind directly, stated rather than developed.
- NOTE (build, `religious-error`): the same spirit's 2015-06-21-af-martin-luther does not address religious error; this message is where Luther carries the thread.

### 2016-07-25-af-seretta-kem | Power of Prayers Multiplied in Healing Circles | 2016-07-25 [not publicly posted]
- `prayer-changes-world-conditions` **[Foundation]** : the mechanism, given step by step: angelic healers and circles "which rise up through the spheres of spirit" join the gathering; "with this comes a great Light, a portal of Light, directly from God"; "amongst you within this circle is a great vessel, a vessel that collects these energies"; the energies "overflow from this vessel into various streams travelling in all directions in the world"; "many angels are directing and dividing this energy to various places in the world"; and "this one great beam of Light is multiplied over and over again exponentially and touches many, many thousands in the world, not only the children you pray for and those loved ones and those you know, but many, many more," with the intended end named: God "will place within them the desire for His Love." The same spirit sketches the route at 2016-05-09-af-seretta-kem.
- `healing-channel` **[Elaboration]** : the downstream distribution architecture: the thread's members work the upstream question (how an individual becomes a clear channel); this shows what the cleared channel's output does once it leaves the circle, the thread's closing claim that even a few such souls change the world. Closest member 2016-01-04-af-seretta-kem (Foundation, same spirit).
- NOTE (witness, `portals`): the portal here is not a name for the circle or its prayer: it arrives, comes from God, and functions as the conduit the healing energies flow through, the technical sense that 2018-11-21-af-james and 2019-01-26-af-seretta-kem teach, already present in Seretta Kem's usage. In tracing how the word comes to that meaning, start here rather than at 2016-03-25-af-augustine.
- NOTE (witness, `divine-love-healing`): carries the comparative claim in a single closing sentence ("that great Healing Touch of Love is the most powerful healing of all and the greatest change agent in the universe"); witness, not trigger. Read with 2016-07-13: one develops without comparing, the other compares without developing; 2016-04-13 does both.

- `bell-toll-instrumentality` **[Elaboration]** : turns the bell around: the seeker is also the signal's recipient, "listen to that chime within you that draws you to be awake, that draws you to God, that draws you to the Truth," the seeker's own awakening as the same signal heard from the receiving end, so the signal is heard as well as sent. -- Closest member 2016-01-18-af-confucius.

### 2016-08-11-af-alec-gaunt | Your Obstacles Strengthen You in the Light | 2016-08-11 [not publicly posted]
- `challenges-as-blessings` **[Foundation]** : the anchor, a message that builds WHY hardship strengthens. Mechanism: "each time you overcome an obstacle, each time a lesson is learned, each time you turn your pain into joy you become stronger... you gain more wisdom as you come to understand more fully what it is that the world is made of, the world of man, its complexities, its error, its fears and its lack of love." Stake: "For without knowledge of these things, without the ability to navigate through these issues you will not be an effective instrument of change in the world." Failure mode: "You will be weakened, weakened because rather than humbly face what is set before you, you withdraw or you become angry or you do not express love in some way." Closest member 2019-02-22-af-keea-atta-kem (roster witness); new facet: what the strengthening consists of, competence in the world's conditions, and why it is required, an instrument ignorant of them cannot serve.
- `preparation-to-be-a-rock` **[Elaboration]** : the external proving ground: "And now the time has come and you see how strong you must be, how courageous and brave and full of trust and faith," completed by being tested in the world's resistance, "the error, the hurtful allegations, the difficulties, the complexities that are placed before you." Closest member 2018-09-01-af-seretta-kem (readiness completed inwardly through alignment); new facet: the outward half of the process, the world's resistance as the proving ground. Its "time has come" is the same readiness turn that 2018-09-01-af-seretta-kem marks in the roster.
- NOTE (sighting, `judgment-to-love`): a first-person pattern to follow ("I will not stand for negativity; I stand for Love. I will be love"), plus an option: "to engage with love or to disengage where there is no possibility of love. And this is not being weak." The 2017-06-15-af-augustine Capstone offers only inner remedies (seeing without judging, catching yourself, choosing Love, praying for the one who judges), and none of them allows stepping away, so the option is logged as a member in the line below.
- `judgment-to-love` **[Elaboration]** : adds the licensed exit: "to engage with love or to disengage where there is no possibility of love. And this is not being weak, backing down, this does not injure you but it strengthens you." Closest member 2017-06-15-af-augustine (Capstone); new facet: every Capstone remedy keeps the seeker inside the interaction; this licenses a bounded behavioral exit, conditioned on no possibility of love and framed as strength.
- NOTE (witness, `vanguards-of-change`): the commission materializing in the present tense ("You will travel the world to bring these truths to mankind and now you see this coming about"), asserted in two clauses; roster witness carrying the commission half, paired with 2016-08-15-af-alec-gaunt which carries the release half.

### 2016-08-15-af-alec-gaunt | The Reluctance to Step Fully into the Light | 2016-08-15 [not publicly posted]
- `free-will-and-self-responsibility` **[Elaboration]** : names what the will weighs when it stalls: the fear of annihilation, "to lose who you are and not knowing who you will become, to lose all that you have built over a lifetime," answered by enhancement ("it will not crumble and fall away as you imagine, but all will be enhanced"), with the no-penalty clause: "God respects your will. He awaits your decision and He loves you. Whatever you decide, you are always loved." Closest member 2019-02-22-af-augustine (Foundation, why the prayer for relief fails); new facet: the content of the hesitation itself, and that withholding the decision costs no love, which the Foundation's sterner register does not carry.
- `fear-of-full-immersion` **[Foundation]** : the near-universal reluctance to full immersion ("It is a reluctance to enter that pool of water... This is almost universally true with each soul in this world. So few wish to be immersed in God's great Love so fully"), the feared losses named down to identity itself, vulnerability, possessions, security, and the answer, that nothing in harmony is lost and all is enhanced. The obstacle is specifically not unworthiness, which is the boundary the thread is built on. Presumptive section anchor; the answering message is 2016-08-28-af-francis-of-assisi, thirteen days later in the same travelling circle and the same water image.
- NOTE (witness, `vanguards-of-change`): the release half ("You are still attached and longing to be loved by others, to be acknowledged, to have the material security that the world offers"), set against the commissioning to publications, talks, and Circles of Light; roster witness paired with 2016-08-11.
- NOTE (sighting, `soul-expiation`): the outcome stated as reassurance ("oh what freedom you will feel"); the distinctive material here is the fear that precedes it, carried above.

### 2016-08-19-af-andrew | Blessings Are with You in Your Travels | 2016-08-19 [not publicly posted]
- `mind-vs-soul` **[Elaboration]** : the agency axis: the mind's failure here is not perception but governance, "You are so used to having control, to being on top of things, as you call it, and I tell you God is in control," with the soul named "the proactive force within your lives." Closest member 2017-08-21-af-matthew; new facet: where 2017-08-21-af-matthew asks what each faculty can know, this one asks which faculty is allowed to direct a life.
- `provision-for-service` **[Elaboration]** : develops the barrier claim causally: worry is not merely unhelpful, "In this way you do not attract negativity. In this way you allow God's full Touch, within each thing that you do, to be present and to bring harmony," which is why the barrier is the servant's and not God's; closes on harmony as the mark of God's Hand. Closest member 2016-04-04-af-augustine (Foundation, states the barriers); new facet: why they work as barriers.
- NOTE (sighting, `channel-of-love-in-the-material`): the everyday task as a place where God's Touch is already present ("There are material matters to be taken care of and this is your contribution to this effort... But God's Touch even is within this. God's blessing even is within this"), with the attitude we bring to the task deciding whether that Touch can work. Held at sighting because here the idea is a step in the argument of `provision-for-service`. What this candidate needs is a message that makes the holiness of the everyday task its own argument, apart from provision. Two arguments meet in one passage.

### 2016-08-25-af-brother-mandus | Embrace All in the Flow of God's Love | 2016-08-25 [not publicly posted]
- `judgment-to-love` **[Elaboration]** : the consequentialist reason for restraint: "you are to meet other souls there who are primarily within their mind, they have very set beliefs and are rigid and I would request that you do not confront these individuals in a way that would continue to harden their perspectives. No, you must embrace them in love." Closest member 2017-06-15-af-augustine (Capstone); new facet: the members treat judgment as damage to the judger's own soul; this treats confrontation as causally damaging to the person confronted, hardening the position one hoped to soften, making restraint a matter of effectiveness as well as love.
- NOTE (build, `portals`): "there's been talk of the great portal of Light within the sanctuary and this is your task, beloved souls, to help bring forth in more powerful ways this portal." Four features: a portal already in place and talked about in the circle; at a named place, the sanctuary at Brother Mandus's former home in Blackpool; with a stated purpose ("The intensity of this Light will bring many manifestations of God's Will, and healing, and Love into this part of the world"); and human prayer strengthening it without creating it, the relation 2019-01-26-af-seretta-kem teaches, given here as a task. How the word "portal" gains its full meaning, message by message: 2016-03-25-af-augustine (a loose image) -> 2016-05-09-af-seretta-kem (the circle as a portal) -> 2016-07-25-af-seretta-kem (a portal arriving from God) -> this message (in place, at a named place, given as a task) -> 2018-11-21-af-james and 2019-01-26-af-seretta-kem (the teaching) -> 2019-02-01-af-confucius (the word in everyday use) -> 2019-04-06-af-yogananda (where it stops: communication does not need a portal).
- `portals` **[Elaboration]** : the standing portal in practice, with the four features described in the note above: a portal already in place, at a named place, with a stated purpose, and strengthened by prayer that did not create it. That last is the law 2019-01-26-af-seretta-kem teaches, that God gives a portal and prayer sustains it: "You cannot build a Portal of Light... sustained with prayer."
- NOTE (sighting, `preparation-to-be-a-rock`): the occasion named as a test by the spirit arranging it; the same proving-ground facet as the 2016-08-11-af-alec-gaunt Elaboration; held at sighting so one facet does not carry two Elaborations.
- NOTE (sighting, `healing-channel`): serve without understanding your own part ("it shall flow through you"); the unawareness point overlaps bell-toll rather than the channel mechanism.

With 2016-08-19-af-andrew, the teaching comes just as the travelling work begins.

Consistent with portal vocabulary being specific to particular spirits at this date.

### 2016-08-25-af-matthew | The Difference Between Mind and Soul | 2016-08-25 [not publicly posted]
- `mind-vs-soul` **[Elaboration]** : the constitution-and-hardening axis: every current member describes what the mind does wrong; Matthew describes what it is made of. Whatever the intellect pours in "colors that water and in some cases changes the composition of that water," so the mind's consciousness is constituted by its own inputs and sets over time into "an evolving and somewhat crystalizing way of viewing the world," while the soul is light whose substance brightens by absorbing God rather than by receiving contents from man. Closest member 2016-07-18-af-martin-luther (durability). Weigh at build against 2018-12-02-af-augustine (the mind "constructs reality"): adjacent, but that entry concerns a materialist framework's instability, not the mind's own composition changing with its inputs, and it carries no hardening claim.
- NOTE (witness, `two-paths`): the innateness denial, stated twice and not developed: "No, it does not reside within you as a natural attribute of your being... this special blessing is given through prayer and longing and opening." Held at witness: no spheres, no recompense machinery, no destinations. Facet named for possible promotion at build: an Objection-removed candidate clearing the belief "the Love is already in me, I need only uncover it," which no current member clears in that form.
- NOTE (sighting, `soul-faculties`): "ignites the soul's awareness, perceptions, capacities like nothing else can do"; asserted.
- NOTE (considered and set aside, `divine-love-healing`): "this redeeming Love, this healing Love" is an epithet in a closing list, with no primacy claim and no comparison; weaker than 2016-08-25-af-confucius. Not recorded as a witness.

### 2016-08-26-af-augustine | Seek the Light of God's Love | 2016-08-26 [not publicly posted]
- NOTE (witness, `free-will-and-self-responsibility`): the non-coercion law in its invitational form: "God does not command you to love him but God beseeches you to know his Love. It is your choice." The Foundation (2019-02-22-af-augustine) states the law from the withheld-relief side; this is the same law from the offer side, one sentence, asserted. Promotable at build if a message develops the beseeches-not-commands framing as an argument.
- NOTE (witness, `divine-love-healing`): "Love is the highest gift. Love is the greatest gift. Love is the most healing gift that be given." The primacy claim in a weak form; compare 2016-08-25-af-confucius. Caveat: the sentence says Love and the context is God's Love and "that Divine Grace," so the reading is contextual; strike from the roster if it is kept to explicit Divine Love claims.
- NOTE (witness, `simplicity-of-the-path`): "It is a simple opening of yourselves to this blessing of Love... Powerfully, beautifully, simply." Asserted; roster witness.
- NOTE (witness, `prayer-soul-longing`): response-follows-longing in one clause; the Foundation carries it developed.
- NOTE (witness, `preparation-to-be-a-rock`): the seeker's own steadiness when external security is gone, which is the thread's outcome turned inward rather than toward others. -- no commission here; recorded so this is not mistaken for the commission material.
- NOTE (sighting, `purpose-of-life`): the italicized closing, "To seek Love always, to place this as your great desire in life, and with this all else will follow"; stated crisply, not built.
- NOTE (pattern, described and not penned): relationship with God as the only reliable constant: "This is the only constant in a life that can be relied upon - your relationship with your Heavenly Father," with the securities of the world explicitly substituted. Fenced from `preparation-to-be-a-rock`, `challenges-as-blessings`, and `fear-of-full-immersion` (this is the answer to what remains after loss). Close to 2016-08-15-af-alec-gaunt. Suggested slug: `security-in-god`.

### 2016-08-26-af-brother-mandus | This House Is Blessed by God | 2016-08-26 [not publicly posted]
- `simplicity-of-the-path` **[Elaboration]** : the path's simplicity applied to ministry, with the reason it matters and the way it fails: "to espouse upon the values and understandings of esoteric religions falls at the feet of those who come for nurturance and comfort and healing. It has no effect; it is the power of love, the simplicity of a simple hello, I love you, that reaches the soul, that nurtures the heart." Closest member 2016-04-17-af-francis-of-assisi (Foundation, the simple life chosen, with Testimony); new facet: the complication lies in what the servant says, and that is why it fails.
- NOTE (witness, `portals`): the maintenance requirement. The beam needs continuous prayer to "revitalize and build"; "Continue to pray for the Light to build within this place... and continue to pray that many will come through these doors." A standing obligation of the house's people rather than a fixed installation; the referent is the portal named the day before (2016-08-25-af-brother-mandus). Weigh against 2019-01-26 when the portal material is gathered.
- NOTE (sighting, `love-retained-in-the-channel`): the candidate's claim stated in full: "As you allow that love to flow, so a part of that love is retained within you, accumulating within your soul, bringing enlightenment, true knowledge and understanding." See the pen entry in chains-threads.md.
- NOTE (witness, `self-love-foundation`): the entailment in its positive form ("when you love yourself completely you can not but love all in this way"); 2019-04-05-af-augustine carries it developed from the other side; not an anchor candidate.
- NOTE (witness, `judgment-to-love`): "Do not judge, but embrace and allow that love to flow freely to all you meet"; pairs with the sibling 2016-08-25-af-brother-mandus, the stronger of the pair.

### 2016-08-26-af-jesus | You Are Part of God's Salvation for Mankind | 2016-08-26 [not publicly posted]
- NOTE (witness, `portals`): "you shall come together in many places in the world, to bring these great portals of Light into the world. For God needs a circle of dedicated souls in prayer... and you will bring with you wherever you go as part of this Gift and blessing." It adds three things: portals plural and replicable; the precondition named, a circle of dedicated souls in prayer; and the Gift carried by the people. Here portals are something a travelling group carries with it, in tension with the reading that the word belongs to a place; the tension is left open.

No current member is about being re-formed into groups; held at witness (one forward-looking sentence in a message whose spine is the salvation plan); promote at build if wanted alongside the same-batch member.

- NOTE (witness, `preparation-to-be-a-rock`): "poised upon a threshold," with the readiness inventory framed as substantially accomplished. -- promotion candidate at build, on the readiness-turn facet the thread carries at 2018-09-01-af-seretta-kem.

Closest member 2016-03-06-af-jesus (release of material burdens); new facet: one's own intentions as an encumbrance of the same kind as possessions. The fence against `preparation-to-be-a-rock` holds cleanly: this is mobility for where you are sent ("you will be needed soon in many ways").
