### 2016-08-27-af-brother-mandus | A Description of the Soul and Its Enduring Nature | 2016-08-27 [not publicly posted]
- `mind-vs-soul` **[Elaboration]** : the apologetic facet, the soul defined against a materialist account the circle had just heard: "It is not a linear process; it is not synaptic; it is not chemical; it does not have the mechanisms so clearly understood by the sciences... there is no material aspect to the soul," the consequence drawn ("If this man's theories were correct, then upon your death you would be as nothing") and answered ("the soul retains your individualized consciousness forever"). Every current member argues from inside the teaching; this argues from outside it. Closest members 2016-08-25-af-matthew (the composition axis, positively) and 2018-12-02-af-augustine (framework instability, without the survival argument).
- `earth-conditions-and-prayer` **[Elaboration]** : the spirit-side view of the field, in answer to his own question: "And do you know what this looks like from our perspective? Dark clouds. It is not of Light; it is heavy," with the inside-and-outside duality, "They not only surround your planet but they reside within your beings"; and a widened remedy: "the energy of love is the only energy, whether it be the Natural Love or the Divine Love, that will disperse those clouds of the human condition," enacted through ordinary contact, "a look in your eyes, an arm reached around another." Closest member 2016-02-29-af-luke (Foundation). Registry-weigh at build: the spine says daily prayer neutralizes the conditions; this widens the remedy to love in action, with natural love admitted as sufficient.
- NOTE (sighting, `two-paths`): the pair named without ranking, "whether it be the Natural Love or the Divine Love"; an instance for the natural-love rehabilitation watch (see Open questions in chains-threads).
- NOTE (sighting, `soul-faculties`): the potential "barely, barely understood by any of you, or any scientist... maybe a handful in the world of mystics"; asserted.
- NOTE (sighting, `death-transition`): "the soul retains your individualized consciousness forever. It is what carries through into the world of spirit," stated as doctrine in one sentence in service of the apologetic above; held at sighting so the facet is not double-counted.

### 2016-08-27-af-jesus | This Is Hallowed Ground | 2016-08-27 [not publicly posted]
- NOTE (build, `portals`): every prior sighting describes a portal already in place; this says how it got there, accumulated human prayer over time: the blessing was "brought about by many prayers, said by many souls, brought about by the deep longings that have been expressed here," with thanks to "all those who've gone before you, who have through their efforts and their faith and their love have come to draw God's special blessing and dispensation upon this ground." With 2016-08-26-af-brother-mandus this gives the build-and-maintain account: prayer draws the dispensation, prayer sustains it. The consecration vocabulary enters the record here (consecrated, hallowed ground, dispensation); read this and 2019-01-26 against each other when the material is gathered.
- `portals` **[Elaboration]** : how the portal came to be: built up over time by human prayer, told in the language of consecration (hallowed, consecrated, dispensation). Read with 2019-01-26-af-seretta-kem, the two agree: prayer draws down what God gives and the spirits build. Also worth keeping in view: 2019-01-26-af-seretta-kem says Jesus ignited the foundation of each portal known to this circle, and here is Jesus at such a portal, thanking "all those who've gone before you" whose prayers "drew God's special blessing and dispensation upon this ground." The portal's origin, told by the one who ignited it.
- NOTE (sighting, `bell-toll-instrumentality`): "expanding beyond this room and this building and this place you reside within, reaching many"; the radiating body is a place rather than a soul; assigned to the portal layer, not the thread.
- NOTE (build, `portals`): the Blackpool messages, all about one physical place and from several spirits, are the evidence the `portals` thread rests on; a separate `consecrated-ground` candidate was considered and set aside as a duplicate.

### 2016-08-28-af-francis-of-assisi | Come Down to the River | 2016-08-28 [not publicly posted]
- `fear-of-full-immersion` **[Reframe]** : The obstacle recast as clothing, worn and removable, not a condition of the soul: "I not only ask you to bend down and sip of these waters but to remove the clothing of your mental assumptions... Release the yoke of your fears. Release the judgements of your mind and come and wade within these waters of Love. Let them carry you," closing "it is for each one you, beloved souls, to release all that which holds you from being immersed in this wondrous Gift." Adds the half-measure the candidate lacked, sipping from the bank, the name for what stands between reluctance and immersion. Reframe over Objection-removed: no contrary belief is cleared; the obstacle is re-imaged.
- `armour-of-the-world` **[Foundation]** : names the clothing and says why it is worn: the mental assumptions a person wears as protection, which the message calls removable. This message is the hinge between two threads and holds a role in both: the Reframe above rests on the fear of full immersion, this one on the clothing itself. -- presumptive anchor
- NOTE (sighting, `mind-vs-soul`): "remove the clothing of your mental assumptions... Release the judgements of your mind"; the mind-as-obstacle facet the thread holds many times over.

Where 2016-04-04-af-augustine answers worry, guilt and conflict as self-raised barriers, this answers the belief that serving will cost you. Secondary facet: the law applied to an institutional negotiation with trustees and an explicit consideration of the material, the nearest the monetary clause comes to a concrete test. - NOTE (pattern, described and not penned): "threshold" recurs in 2016-08-26-af-jesus, this message, and 2016-08-27-af-augustine, in the same register; recorded so these messages can be found together if the archive is asked about commissioning.

### 2016-08-31-af-martin-luther | Welcome to Frankfurt | 2016-08-31 [not publicly posted]
- `religious-error` **[Elaboration]** : the successor-reformation facet, the reformer's verdict on the mechanism of reform itself: "In my time my thinking was different and my efforts were born mainly of the mind, the material mind," and therefore "those of you who are willing, must again fight for change... to start a new Reformation," whose method reverses direction: "It is not from the mind to the soul, beloveds, in these days ahead, it is from the soul to the mind." Closest member his own 2016-07-18 (the chimera and the faculty); new facet: the thread's members end at the correction; this turns the correction into a programme and names its faculty, the thread's only forward-looking statement. Kept at Elaboration over the staging caveat: a programme is not a restatement.
- NOTE (witness, `mind-vs-soul`): the soul-to-mind directional phrasing; the argument is already carried by his own 2016-07-18 Elaboration, and the same passage does the work in the religious-error role above; not counted twice.

- NOTE (witness, `divine-love-mediumship`): "I regret that I can not speak my language through this instrument who is unprepared to do so, so we will have to make do with English." The preparation of an instrument includes the languages it can speak; a concrete limit on how the channel works; one sentence, an aside.

- NOTE (witness, `judgment-to-love`): "Forgiveness, beloved souls, will open the door, forgiveness of yourselves, of all those around you and in place you must put love... Love will open the door to God and God's Love will come rushing in like a mighty river." The Capstone (2018-07-16-af-andrew) makes forgiveness the consequence, arriving reflexively as the Love infills; this makes it the precondition that opens the door. Held explicitly; settle before the thread is built; see the Open Questions entry, where a dissolving reading (an initial act of will versus a matured reflex) is recorded as a reading to weigh, not a ruling.
- NOTE (witness, `self-love-foundation`): "forgiveness of yourselves" placed first, with "each one a beautiful soul, each one is worthy of God's Touch of Love" ahead of it; the self-first ordering is the thread's own claim; not an anchor candidate.

### 2016-09-01-af-seretta-kem | Group Prayer Multiplies Blessings | 2016-09-01 [not publicly posted]
- `prayer-changes-world-conditions` **[Elaboration]** : the upstream condition, from the same spirit as the Foundation, 2016-07-25-af-seretta-kem: what strengthens the offering is harmony rather than volume, "it is your longing, your passion, your focus, and your efforts to receive and to be in harmony together to create those conditions amongst you that draws this great blessing," by "harmonizing and resonating with one another," naming the Law activated: "you generate and activate a great Law of Attraction." Two further additions: the reciprocal benefit, "You benefit from the prayers of your brothers and sisters who join you," where the Foundation is entirely outward-directed; and the thread's own limit, "This is not the only way to receive Divine Love... it is in your personal and private prayers that the substantial portion of Love is given," to be weighed against the registry wording at build.
- NOTE (witness, `prayer-soul-longing`): the proportionality form, "the capacity to receive this Love is unlimited, only restricted by your own desires and longings"; a sharpening rather than a new facet. Recorded also so the Law of Activation (this thread's registry line) and the Law of Attraction (named here for an adjacent effect) are not conflated at build.
- NOTE (sighting, `bell-toll-instrumentality`): "you generate a Light through your prayers that spreads out through this network"; the medium of spread is the lattice rather than the soul's own radiance; belongs to the lattice material.
- NOTE (considered and set aside, `divine-love-healing`): "receiving the healing from God" in a benefits list, no primacy claim, no comparison.

Its "if" clauses are completed only by the sentence that follows it, and the message text is kept as received. - NOTE (sighting, `affect-as-soul-marker`): same spirit as 2016-04-12-af-andrew, a different feeling and the same move, the feeling read as a sign of the soul's condition: "when you feel this great and deep peace you know that God has touched you, you know that your soul has been awakened to some degree... for His mark is the peace that passes all understanding," the state as God's mark, a sign left by the toucher. Held at sighting, since a 75-word blessing states this and does not develop it.

### 2016-09-02-af-augustine | Natural Love and Divine Love | 2016-09-02 [not publicly posted]
- `divine-love-without-knowing` **[Elaboration]** : the misattribution account of why recognition fails: not ignorance of doctrine but indistinguishability, "it is difficult to delineate between the mind, the natural love, and soul experiencing the Divine Love"; the unknowing is misattribution, not absence, with the diagnostic case stated (a visitor who "does have Divine Love within him, yet he does not clearly understand the differences between what resides within his soul and the understandings of the love that is of the natural kind") and the remedy, yearning "innocent as a child," called "the beginning of true faith, true soul awakening." Closest member 2019-02-05-af-keea-atta-kem. Build note: same spirit as the Foundation, 2019-01-31-af-augustine, asking the same question; both could stand as Foundation-section members with the anchor chosen at build, as with `who-jesus-was`.

- NOTE (witness, `mind-vs-soul`): the ineffability claim, "there are no true words to explain, to transfer to you the experience of this Touch"; 2017-08-21-af-matthew carries it as a member; restated, so witness.

### 2016-09-02-af-confucius | God Has Lit a Spark Within You | 2016-09-02 [not publicly posted]
- NOTE (witness, `judgment-to-love`): judgment declined on a new ground. Elsewhere in the chain, non-judgment comes through what judging reveals about the person judging, or through conscious choice as the antidote; Confucius reaches it because no two paths can be compared: "that expression will be unique for each individual. For each soul is unique and the path that they walk is unique," therefore "Do not judge another's path but walk firmly upon your own." The thing judged is not comparable, so the comparison is void. Stated across two sentences without being built out.
- NOTE (sighting, `bell-toll-instrumentality`): "you will do God's Will in this respect: that you will live by example, by your own being live the truth." One sentence holding both sides of the question this chain leaves open, since "live by example" reads as active expression and "by your own being" reads as radiance without action. It settles nothing, and is recorded so the evidence is complete.
- NOTE (witness, `guidance-as-seed`): the thread's argument, that an inner prompting is to be honoured and acted on rather than awaited as a dramatic sign: "Listen to the desire of your soul. Listen to that truth that beats within you, each one of you, and be compelled to act upon that which is within you." The image differs from the Foundation, 2016-02-02-af-jesus, which has a seed and a droplet where Confucius has a spark and a flame, ignition rather than germination. Under the argument-not-image ruling, this is a witness.
- NOTE (sighting, `soul-purpose-emergence`): the unique-expression premise asserted, "that expression will be unique for each individual," carrying nothing of the emergence claim itself.
- NOTE (considered and set aside, `divine-love-healing`): "which will in turn heal your wounds and your errors," healing named as the third term in a sequence following God's Touch. No comparison with other healing and no mechanism, so it sits below this chain's bar.
- NOTE (considered and set aside, `two-currents-cosmology`): "each soul upon this planet must be ignited into change to release the dark conditions that beset humanity" and "as you struggle forth beating back the darkness of error." Light against darkness at world scale, without the two-currents contest or the coalescing movement that candidate turns on.

### 2016-09-02-af-jesus | I Am Your Brother | 2016-09-02 [not publicly posted]
- `who-jesus-was` **[Elaboration]** : the denial of incarnate divinity in his own voice, with the positive account attached and a directive. "And I was never God incarnate. No, my beloveds, I was a man who discovered the truth of God's Love and in my discovery opened the doors to its existence amongst mankind and all spirits," followed by "do not worship me in any way for I am not God. I am your teacher. I am with you in my love for you but I am with God as you are with God." Nearest to it is 1963-12-26-ds-mary, which corrects the same doctrine from outside and at second hand, describing his Messiahship as attained through the inflowing of God's Love rather than possessed from birth. What this adds is the correction made by Jesus himself, with a practical consequence: worship is prohibited and relocated to God, and his own position is stated as identical to the hearer's.
- NOTE (witness, `religious-error`): the worship-misdirection facet, "it is God you must worship; it is God you must reveal your heart and your souls to". This corrects a practice rather than a doctrine, which none of that chain's messages do, and the message never turns to religion, churches, or the history of the error. Worth reading beside 2016-04-02-af-jesus, the same spirit correcting the same family of error.
- NOTE (witness, `the-fall`): the reopening stated in the first person and extended beyond mankind, "in my discovery opened the doors to its existence amongst mankind and all spirits". 2016-03-31-af-judas gives the gift offered, declined and withdrawn "until Jesus"; this is that clause from the other end, and it adds a further reach, that the reopening covered spirits as well as the living.
- NOTE (sighting, `divine-love-without-knowing`): the longing-suffices premise, "every soul open to this great blessing of the Divine Love will receive this gift, for in their longings, in their great desire to be with God in love, to be with God directly, they will be blessed". It carries the chain's second step, that the soul can long for God on its own, but not its first, since the souls described are already open and desiring rather than unaware.
- NOTE (sighting, `bell-toll-instrumentality`): "you will manifest in powerful ways God's Love and this will influence and bring Light to many, to be a channel of Love, to be as I was upon this Earth." Asserted, with the mechanism left unstated.
- NOTE (considered and set aside, `portals`): "opened the doors to its existence amongst mankind and all spirits" is door language used as a figure for the availability of Divine Love, not for a conduit of concentrated blessing. Recorded as a negative so a later search on the word does not collect it.

### 2016-09-03-af-james | Pray for Mankind | 2016-09-03 [not publicly posted]
- `prayer-changes-world-conditions` **[Elaboration]** : the chain describes how intercession travels, collected in a vessel, overflowing, divided and multiplied by angels, arriving at thousands never met. James supplies why mankind is what is prayed for, the diagnosis: "almost all souls residing in this place have a great lacking within them," so "Pray for the children. Pray for those who are lost. Pray for your fellow man, for each soul in their own way, is lost," with the deprivation universal rather than confined to the visibly suffering, "Look into the eyes of those in the world and you will see it." Nearest to it is 2016-09-01-af-seretta-kem, at the same gathering, which gives the condition of the offering, harmony rather than volume. That one conditions the sending; this one names and grounds the target.
- NOTE (witness, `earth-conditions-and-prayer`): the generating side of the darkness-field stated actively, "You add to the conditions, you add to the energies of this world in ways that are disruptive, that will bring chaos in time," with humanity as an ongoing contributor rather than an inheritor. That chain is defensive, the field met by daily prayer for respite and protection, and James offers no defensive remedy; his remedy is outward and belongs to the entry above.
- NOTE (witness, `soul-purpose-emergence`): the growth-to-knowing link, which is the chain's own claim rather than its periphery: "You will know what to do as your souls grow in this Love. You will know what to do because what is necessary and required will be obvious as you look at the world through the eyes of your souls." Knowing what to do arrives as a by-product of the soul's growth and is described as becoming obvious rather than being revealed.
- NOTE (sighting, `two-currents-cosmology`): a contest of trajectories stated explicitly, "the world will continue to darken to the point that all will unravel" set against "This tiny droplet of Light will expand and expand." Both directions in one message, still without the two-currents vocabulary or the coalescing movement.
- NOTE (sighting, `vanguards-of-change`): the doors-opening claim in general form, "God will use you in ways yet unanticipated, opportunities will come your way so that you may be a change agent in the world." The commission and the opening without the release requirement or the mobility that chain turns on.
- NOTE (sighting, `bell-toll-instrumentality`): "Focus on being a channel of Love and Light"; asserted, no mechanism.
- NOTE (sighting, `portals`): "come together, beloved souls, in prayer as often as you may, to bring further Light to this place and to yourselves." Light attached to a place and built by repeated gathered prayer, which is the shape of 2016-08-26-af-brother-mandus and 2016-08-27-af-jesus. Against counting it: no portal vocabulary appears anywhere in the message, "this place" most plainly means the room, and the same sentence directs the Light at the people as well.
- NOTE (context): "Pray for the children," bears on the collection question also raised at 2016-03-14-af-mary. One clause, with no teaching about children attached.
- NOTE (considered and set aside, `earth-changes`): the darkening and unravelling here are moral and social, with the cause in human attitudes and priorities. Nothing about the planet, its laws, or its cleansing.

### 2016-09-03-af-mary | Love Brings Resolution to Disturbances | 2016-09-03 [not publicly posted]
- `judgment-to-love` **[Elaboration]** : judgment measured by its effect on the person judged, where the chain's other messages measure it by its effect on the one judging. Their line runs inward: judgment reveals our own soul state, beneath it lies hidden hurt, the antidote is conscious choice, and compassion arrives reflexively as the soul grows. Mary argues outward and practically: "Confronting her will not create the effect that you wish but will entrench her in her belief, but if you are channels of love, acceptance, humility and prayer you will assist us in keeping the conditions at a higher level," summed up as "Judgement does not bring resolution, love always does." Nearest to it is 2017-06-15-af-augustine, the developed lesson, which supplies the inner diagnosis and the choose-Love antidote. What this adds is the efficacy argument: love is prescribed not because judgment corrupts the one judging but because confrontation hardens the other person and love is the only thing that resolves.
- NOTE (witness, `healing-channel`): the channel described as someone who raises the conditions of a room, working with the spirit side, "if you are channels of love, acceptance, humility and prayer you will assist us in keeping the conditions at a higher level," with the four constituents of the channel named unusually precisely. That chain concerns what makes an individual a clear conduit for a healing act; this concerns what a group of channels does to the atmosphere of a gathering, and names the spirits as beneficiaries of that work.
- NOTE (witness, `prayer-changes-world-conditions`): intercession aimed at one named person, with protection rather than awakening as its object: "you must pray for her and send her your love. Put your love around her and ask for the cloak of protection to surround her and those who are close to her, that this disturbance may be quelled." The same outward direction as the chain's other messages, at a scale of one, and the effect asked for is a shield rather than a kindling.
- NOTE (witness, `opposition-to-the-light-worker`): protection asked for a third party rather than mounted by the seeker, "ask for the cloak of protection to surround her and those who are close to her, that this disturbance may be quelled". Held at witness because the subject is exposed by her own delving, "she knows not the conditions that she is delving into and is very vulnerable to all influences", with nothing conspiring against her, so the message carries the defence without any account of what opposes.
- NOTE (considered and set aside, `earth-conditions-and-prayer`): "keeping the conditions at a higher level" uses that chain's vocabulary, but the conditions are those of a room and a group rather than the world's darkness-field, and no daily-prayer remedy is prescribed.
- NOTE (considered and set aside, `sensitive-souls-redemptive`): "very vulnerable to all influences" describes exposure through unwise delving rather than the acute feeling of the world's cruelty that marks that chain, and no redemptive function is claimed for her.
- NOTE (considered and set aside, `divine-love-healing`): "allow God to heal whatever is not in harmony" names no Divine Love, makes no comparison, and describes the repair of a social disharmony.

### 2016-09-04-af-augustine | Continue in Your Service | 2016-09-04 [not publicly posted]
- `bell-toll-instrumentality` **[Elaboration]** : the unwitting radiance stated at its widest, with its scale named. "Yes, you serve many in many different ways; when you walk down a street, though you are not aware, souls are touched. When you sit in a circle, though you do not see everything that is manifest through your prayers and your efforts together, there are many, many blessings given to many, many souls far greater in number than the souls within your circle." -- Nearest to it is 2018-09-01-af-augustine, the same spirit, where the signal goes out even in idle silence at home. That one establishes that the radiance never stops; this one locates it in ordinary unremarkable motion, walking down a street, and makes a claim about reach, that the blessings exceed the number of people in the room.
- NOTE (witness, `vanguards-of-change`): the commission stated as a travel programme rather than a readiness condition, "For we will be calling you to many places in the world. There will be many such groups, circles, discussions," with "you come as missionaries" naming the role. That chain turns on the release requirement, mobility at a moment's notice and the fears that block it, and this message asks for no release; it assumes the mobility and describes the work.
- NOTE (witness, `simplicity-of-the-path`): the ministry half of that chain's scope, "your message is simple, your love is accepting," with the failure mode put as a prohibition rather than a warning: "Do not argue with others. Be sure of the Love. Do not express contrary opinions, but be sure in the love." -- Nearest to it is 2016-08-26-af-brother-mandus, which warns that expounding esoteric religions falls at the feet of people who came for comfort. This forbids argument itself rather than complexity.
- NOTE (sighting, `prayer-changes-world-conditions`): the drawing mechanism from the praying side, "You draw to the group, through your prayers, through your intentions and desires and gifts these great blessings so that others may drink deep as the great well of love is filled and filled overflowing." The overflow image matches that chain's route, but the direction differs: the blessing is drawn into the room here rather than discharged outward from it.
- NOTE (considered and set aside, `portals`): "The Love of God is concentrated within the circle" is concentration language with no conduit, no attachment to a place and no conferral.

### 2016-09-04-af-john-the-beloved | God's Love Is the Key | 2016-09-04 [not publicly posted]
- `fear-of-full-immersion` **[Elaboration]** : the fear caught at the point of turning away, and named as fear of the change itself rather than of any particular loss. "Many decide to turn away from this once they feel the power and the wonderment and the potential of this great journey of Love. They are fearful, fearful of change. For if there is one major component of the gift of the Father's Love it is that it changes you." Nearest to it is 2016-08-15-af-alec-gaunt, which itemizes the feared losses, vulnerability, possessions, security, identity, and answers them with the assurance that nothing in harmony is lost. That one describes a soul hesitating at the threshold; this describes souls who have already tasted the gift and retreat because of what it did, which puts the fear after the first reception rather than before it. The chain treats the obstacle as clothing that can be removed; here people put the clothing back on.
- `prayer-soul-longing` **[Elaboration]** : the floor and the rate, neither of which the thread holds elsewhere. "It is a simple choice to sit in prayer. To long for this Love requires nothing but your effort and time. Even a brief prayer for God for Love will eventually set you upon this course and the more effort you apply in this journey, the faster will be the awakening." A floor, that a brief prayer suffices to begin, and a rate, that effort governs speed. -- decide at build whether the two belong together: the floor lowers the barrier to entry while the rate makes speed depend on effort, and a reader can hear the second undoing the first. The floor is the part that is this thread's own claim.
- NOTE (witness, `mind-vs-soul`): the account of what the Love does to perception, "It draws your attention from the surface of your mind's perceptions of the world to a deeper place, a deeper understanding, where the layers of illusions fall away and the truth is revealed," with the cost named, that the first thing seen clearly is the darkness of the human condition. The same passage does the work in the entry above and is not counted twice.
- NOTE (sighting, `divine-love-without-knowing`): "Some of you had no clear understanding of why" they came, set against others who "were firmly guided and clear in their intentions". The unknowing here concerns the motive for attending rather than the reception of the Love.
- NOTE (sighting, `incremental-journey`): "Slowly your eyes are opening. You are able to see more clearly... The Light that shines within you is a little brighter." Asserted, with no oscillation and no mechanism.
- NOTE (context): the message carries words to pray, italicised in the source and introduced with "Are you willing to say": "Father, I need you. I need your Love. I need you to help me rise above these conditions, my own condition and all that is around me to something higher, to something more true and real. I need to be nourished in my soul by your great Essence."
- NOTE (considered and set aside, `portals`): "Use this key to unlock the doors" is door language used purely as a figure for access to the gift.

### 2016-09-04-af-unnamed-spirit | Blessings for Each | 2016-09-04 [not publicly posted]
- NOTE (context): sixty-six words, every claim asserted and nothing developed. Recorded so the message is not silently absent.
- NOTE (sighting, `bell-toll-instrumentality`): "Embrace all as a channel of His Love." The claim in a weak form: no mechanism, no radiance, no effect on others stated.
- NOTE (sighting, `judgment-to-love`): "See all you meet with eyes of love," the instruction the Mary message of the previous day argues at length, here in a single clause with no ground given. Recorded so the two are findable together, since they close consecutive days of one gathering.

### 2016-09-05-af-andrew | Be Secure upon the Path | 2016-09-05 [not publicly posted]
- `mind-vs-soul` **[Elaboration]** : the conflict described across a population rather than inside one person, with a consequence attached. "And some fall away because they are not strong enough. Others excel because within them is a deep trust and faith in God. But most waver from one to the other; the mind and soul, the mind and the soul, trying in their own way to make sense of the truth," with the resolution stated as an order of governance, "in time your lives are governed by your soul and your soul is governed by God." Nearest to it is 2016-08-25-af-matthew, which describes what the mind is made of and how it hardens. Where that message looks at the faculties, this one spreads the outcomes across people, most of them wavering, and attaches the practical consequence that God uses the instruments who let the soul take precedence.
- NOTE (witness, `free-will-and-gods-plan`): the willing soul as the means, with the qualification that willingness need not be perfect: "God will use the instruments that are secure upon this path... And this is not to say that God does not use those who waver somewhat, for you are human and we do not expect perfection." That chain has the willing soul ignited by Divine Love as the treasure through which God's Will manifests; this message adds that God also works through the imperfectly surrendered.
- NOTE (witness, `preparation-to-be-a-rock`): "Prepare yourselves, beloved souls, for what is to come. There is much work that awaits willing hands," with the widening detail that "there are others who are coming, who will join you in this great task". The coordinated network is already in that chain; the arrival of unknown others is stated here without development.
- NOTE (sighting, `redemption-universal`): "God has a plan for the salvation of mankind." That chain argues about souls in the dark spheres and their reachability, so this collective statement is adjacent at best.

### 2016-09-06-af-joseph | Be with God | 2016-09-06 [not publicly posted]
- NOTE (witness, `prayer-soul-longing`): what prayer produces afterwards, stated three ways, "The answers will come in prayer. The answers will come in inspiration. The answers will come as the breath of God blows upon each one of you and clears away the error and the mysteries so that you will truly know," with the receiving end specified, "The understandings that God has placed within your souls will emerge into your consciousness." That chain establishes what prayer essentially is and what it elicits; this describes what surfaces afterwards, and locates the content as already placed in the soul rather than newly given.
- NOTE (witness, `soul-purpose-emergence`): two statements in one message, and together strong support for the chain from the Frankfurt messages. First, "within that call, that desire, that awareness is the answer to all your questions," which puts the answer inside the prompting rather than at the end of a search. Second, "The understandings that God has placed within your souls will emerge into your consciousness and be your guiding grace," which is the emergence mechanism in compact form, God-placed content surfacing as the soul awakens rather than arriving as revelation. Read with 2016-09-03-af-james, which carries the same growth-to-knowing link.
- NOTE (witness, `bell-toll-instrumentality`): the example as mechanism, "and then you walk in Light, you become that example and others will notice," followed by the propagation claim, "many souls will be drawn just as you were drawn to one another and these souls will be drawn to you." Being noticed is a weaker mechanism than radiance and a stronger one than bare assertion. Comparable to 2016-09-02-af-confucius, and the two belong together.
- NOTE (sighting, `earth-conditions-and-prayer`): the damage attributed to humanity's turning away, "Too long humanity has turned away from God and in this turning away they have done themselves and this world great damage." The generating claim without the field, and without the daily-prayer remedy that chain prescribes.
- NOTE (context): an italicised address to God introduced with "One soul saying": "I am with You beloved Father; I am with You in Your Love. I will walk with You, I will talk with You, I will listen to Your response, I will be open to the gifts You have to give me, Your Love to flow towards me, Your guidance showing me the way." A declaration of commitment offered as an illustration rather than a petition given to the circle.
- NOTE (considered and set aside, `divine-love-healing`): "the healing will flow towards you" and "there is no other source that will bring the healing and the answers to mankind". The second is a sole-source claim about God, which is nearer this chain than most of the surrounding material, but it concerns the source of answers and healing generally rather than Divine Love as one energy among healing energies, and makes no comparison.

### 2016-09-06-af-mary | You Will Be Healed | 2016-09-06 [not publicly posted]
- `earth-changes` **[Elaboration]** : what the changes do to people, named as the reason the prepared souls are wanted. "Mankind is on the verge of great change as this world is transformed, as God places His Hand upon this planet to bring change and healing and balance. And he desires that his emissaries of Love will help ease the fears and the confusion that will ensue from these changes." Nearest to it is 2017-09-13-af-keea-atta-kem, which gives a full account of the changes themselves and sends those carrying Divine Love to scientists, leaders and seekers. The chain's other messages say what the changes are and that they cleanse rather than punish; this says what they produce in people, fear and confusion, and makes the easing of that a stated divine desire. It is also unusually explicit that the Hand is God's and the intended end is balance, where other messages describe the Earth acting on itself.
- NOTE (witness, `preparation-to-be-a-rock`): "You must all prepare yourselves. Listen to the callings of your heart. Know that God will use you to bring comfort to many," with the condition stated as closeness: "you must be close to God, for it is in this closeness, at-onement with God that you will be a mighty channel." That chain describes the readying as inner rather than informational; this names at-onement specifically as the qualifying condition, which is more doctrinally precise than its usual framing.
- NOTE (sighting, `divine-love-healing`): "Do not fear the hands of the physicians, they will heal you and we will be with them, guiding them" places spirit assistance alongside material medicine without ranking them, and the closing "you will be a mighty channel to bring healing and peace" names the channel rather than the energy. Neither carries this chain's primacy claim or its ground in eternality.
- NOTE (sighting, `soul-purpose-emergence`): "in this way you will come to know your true gifts and express them for the good of all," with the knowing arriving through a passage of guided change rather than by revelation.

### 2016-09-07-af-confucius | You Are Cradled in His Care | 2016-09-07 [not publicly posted]
- NOTE (witness, `simplicity-of-the-path`): the failure mode located inside the seeker's own head rather than in the teaching. "It is so simple, beloved souls, so very simple. Do not clutter your minds with speculation and doubt and fear." That chain locates complication in doctrine, in esoteric expounding and in how a servant speaks; this locates it in the private mental activity of someone who already believes, and names its three components as speculation, doubt and fear. Two sentences, with nothing built on them.
- NOTE (sighting, `bell-toll-instrumentality`): the claim put as a rhythm, "For every breath you take there will be a blessing, not just for you but for this world. Every step that you take, you will touch another soul." The same claim as 2016-09-04-af-augustine, which states it as an unwitting effect; not counted twice.
- NOTE (sighting, `soul-purpose-emergence`): "Your gifts just begin to open, they flower in this Love and when they are fully in bloom you will carry a great message," closing "Be with God and you will find your great purpose." The gradual flowering is this chain's shape, though the agent here is the Love rather than the soul's own awakening.
- NOTE (considered and set aside, `divine-love-healing`): "He wishes to heal every part of your being, every crevice of your soul. He wishes to heal you and make you whole." Strong healing language, and still set aside: God is named as the healer, no energy is compared, and the primacy claim is untouched.

### 2016-09-08-af-jesus | You Are My Disciple Here | 2016-09-08 [not publicly posted]
- NOTE (witness, `two-paths`): the choice put as a formal question with two named alternatives, "Every soul must face this choice: Shall I be with God and come to know His Will and act upon this knowledge or Shall I join the human condition and turn away?" That chain concerns natural love and Divine Love as paths of purification; this pair is God against the human condition, a different pair, so it is recorded as a witness to the structure of the choice rather than to the doctrine.
- NOTE (sighting, `vanguards-of-change`): the commission delivered to one person in one named land, "I have sent you, my brother, to be my disciple of Truth in this land," with the smallness of the operation acknowledged, "Although your numbers are few, the power of God's blessing is great." That chain concerns a group commissioned to mobility; this is a single resident commissioned in place.
- NOTE (context): "make this promise within your soul, that resides within your soul 'Father I am your child of Truth and Love, Father I will serve you in love, Father show me the way' and in this simple prayer you will be guided." The text names it a prayer.
- NOTE (witness, `conditions-of-a-people`): the condition given its affective content, "The hearts that mend in this country are burdened... They are burdened with such pain and grief, anger and sorrow," and widened past the nation to "this part of the world, beset by strife for so long".
- NOTE (considered and set aside, `divine-love-healing`): "There is so much healing and work to be done here" refers to a nation's wounds rather than to an energy.

### 2016-09-09-af-andrew | Put Away Your Shield and Your Armour and Lead with Love | 2016-09-09 [not publicly posted]
- `conditions-of-a-people` **[Foundation]** : the condition named, explained and answered. Andrew opens "This is a nation without trust. Every soul, it seems, in this place is fearful and finds it very difficult to trust another... always wary of betrayal, very sensitive to the smallest innuendo," attributes it to history, "conditions brought about by these pains, of generations of mistrust," and describes its transmission by projection onto whoever is met. The response has two halves, do not take the condition on and do not armour against it: "put away your shield and your armour, to trust in God who will protect you... You will not be hurt. Only when you join into this condition will you feel that way." The thread's other messages observe the condition; this one builds the argument from it. -- shares the shield-and-armour passage with the `armour-of-the-world` Elaboration in this entry; the two are separable, that role resting on the clothing figure and this one on the condition the clothing defends against.
- `free-will-and-self-responsibility` **[Elaboration]** : the lag between what the Love has already changed and what the seeker must still change by hand. "This Love is designed to expel these conditions within you. But the last vestiges of such conditions remain within your material mind, these patterns of thoughts, these erroneous conclusions, these unloving projections upon another. And it is your responsibility to change those patterns of thinking, those old habits, to catch up with the changes within you." Nearest to it is 2019-02-22-af-augustine, the chain's developed statement, where prayers for relief go unanswered while the mind keeps feeding the condition prayed about. Augustine describes a seeker whose mind is actively sustaining the problem, so the healing waits; Andrew describes a seeker whose soul has already been changed and whose mind is merely behind, which makes the residue a maintenance task rather than an obstruction. What is new here is the Love's work already done, with responsibility as what remains. He adds the failure mode: "To do God's Will does not mean to indulge in your old human ways and rationalize them in your actions and say this is God's Will."
- `armour-of-the-world` **[Elaboration]** : the armour as defence against a people that cannot trust, and the instruction to set it down, "put away your shield and your armour, to trust in God who will protect you... You will not be hurt. Only when you join into this condition will you feel that way." The first of the three things the clothing is said to be.
- `simplicity-of-the-path` **[Elaboration]** : the thread's claim argued rather than asserted, with its mechanism stated: faith itself removes the appetite for analysis. "The world is a complex place filled with many complex ideas. The truth is not complicated; the truth is simple... It does not need to be complex or difficult to understand and when one is truly of faith there is no great desire to analyze, merely to act in accordance to the knowing of the soul." Other members say the path is simple, or show a ministry being simple; this says why a person stops complicating it.  
- NOTE (witness, `mind-vs-soul`): the same passage read for the faculty claim, that the material mind is where the vestiges persist after the soul has changed. The argument is carried by the entry above and is not counted twice. Recorded because 2016-09-05-af-andrew is a member of that chain from the same spirit, and the two belong together: that one distributes outcomes across people, this one locates the residue in one faculty.
- NOTE (witness, `sensitive-souls-redemptive`): the inversion of that chain's protection claim. It has the Love within the growing soul as the protection; Andrew has the armour as the danger: "put away your shield and your armour, to trust in God who will protect you... You will not be hurt. Only when you join into this condition will you feel that way." The same conclusion by the opposite instrument, and it names self-protection as the thing that makes a person vulnerable.

### 2016-09-09-af-francis-of-assisi | Nurture Your Soul | 2016-09-09 [not publicly posted]
- NOTE (sighting, `fear-of-full-immersion`): the same spirit reusing that chain's own image with the opposite meaning. In 2016-08-28-af-francis-of-assisi the sip from the bank is precisely the half-measure to be surpassed, and he asks the circle to wade in far enough to be carried. Here he tells one man "Drink the Love deeply, for that thirst will never be quenched but with each sip of this Love you will be stronger and more fulfilled," where the sip is the unit of incremental strengthening and the unquenchable thirst is offered as a good rather than a lack. Either the sip is simply a flexible figure and this message is unrelated, or the two together show immersion and incremental sipping to be complementary rather than opposed. Recorded so the pair is not separated. -- held at sighting, and specifically not a Chrysalis: the reason is scope rather than content, since this is a pastoral aside to one man and it opens nothing. But the clause is Chrysalis-shaped, offering endless longing as the reward rather than the lack, which is a claim about what the transformed state is like permanently. If a message develops the claim that the unquenchable thirst is itself the good, that message would be the Chrysalis and this one its witness. The same clause is also a sighting for the `unlimited-reception` pen candidate.
- NOTE (sighting, `incremental-journey`): "with each sip of this Love you will be stronger and more fulfilled." The incremental claim in one clause, without the oscillation the chain's other messages describe.
- NOTE (sighting, `prayer-soul-longing`): "none of this work can be done without fervent prayer and preparation," and "Continue to pray with all of your heart and all of your soul to receive this great Gift and the barriers will be broken." The barrier-breaking is stated as a consequence without a mechanism.
- NOTE (context): "your love of animals makes us brothers" alludes to his known affinity but describes the recipient, and his spirit file already carries his communion with animals from 2016-01-28.

### 2016-09-10-af-andrew | You Have Joined a Network of Light | 2016-09-10 [not publicly posted]
- NOTE (pattern, described and not penned): what joining the network returns to the person who joins. "You have come together and prayed together and in so doing you have joined in a network of Light... Each time you pray you will benefit from this connection that you made this night. You will find that prayer will be easier, that there will be an opening to God in a deeper way." Other lattice notes describe the network as the medium through which light spreads outward to the world. This describes what flows back to the member: joining is durable, it persists beyond the sitting, and its effect is on the ease of private prayer afterwards.
- NOTE (witness, `prayer-soul-longing`): the same minimum-requirement claim as 2016-09-04-af-john-the-beloved: "All it requires is some time, some focus, to come to know that soul desire within you."
- NOTE (sighting, `divine-love-mediumship`): "if you ask for help in your prayers, help to be able to pray, help to come to know this Truth, God will send an angel to you, to help you in this struggle." That chain ends on the claim that this capacity is not confined to designated mediums, and that every soul accompanied by angels has some avenue that grows as it stays open. This is that in its request-and-dispatch form, a specific angel sent on a specific asking, and the message says nothing about communication or reception.
- NOTE (considered and set aside, `portals`): "network of Light" is the closest vocabulary in these three batches to the portal material without being it. Portals attach to places and are conferred; this network joins people and is joined by praying.

### 2016-09-10-af-augustine | Divine Love Will Change the World, Starting with You | 2016-09-10 [not publicly posted]
- NOTE (context): a full doctrinal address whose every argument is already carried at member level elsewhere. The confession is the 2015-03-28-af-augustine `religious-error` Testimony; the complication motive is the 2015-06-15-af-joseph-smith Elaboration; the simplification-of-a-life claim is the 2016-08-28-af-faith-nyquist witness under `simplicity-of-the-path`. Witness level throughout, with two facets named below.
- NOTE (witness, `simplicity-of-the-path`): two facets, both stated rather than built. The complication is given a motive: "So many wish to complicate the truth, to control it, to have great power, the power to disseminate God's Will to mankind. This is such folly and error." This puts complication down to a will to control. And the simplification runs inward: "your lives become simple, your motivations, and goals, and ambitions, become simple." The motive of complication is the facet worth promoting if it is developed as an argument, since it would also join this thread to `religious-error` at the ambition step.
- NOTE (witness, `religious-error`): the corrected term given positive content, by the man who helped build the error. "When the Master Jesus taught of redemption this is what he spoke of, it is the transformative blessing of the Father's Love, it is the Divine Essence coming from the Creator, it is the Holy Touch that awakens the soul." The thread's atonement members work by negation; this supplies what the word means once the negation is accepted. One sentence, so witness. The Testimony half of the message is already held by the same spirit at 2015-03-28.
- NOTE (witness, `redemption-universal`): the universality claim asserted without the machinery that defines the thread, "Every child is worthy of this Touch, from the very lowest to the highest, every child in this world and in the next is worthy of this Love." No hells, no condition-not-sentence, no turning.
- NOTE (witness, `free-will-and-self-responsibility`): the offer side stated for a third time by this spirit, "God gives you the choice. God allows you to turn away if you wish or turn towards this Light. It is your responsibility and your choice which way you wish to go, for God gave you free will." The 2016-08-26-af-augustine witness already carries the beseeches-not-commands form. Recorded so the repetition across August and September 2016 is findable.
- `armour-of-the-world` **[Elaboration]** : the armour as unworthiness before God, "There is a great ocean of Love which you may bathe within if you but remove your cloak and your armor and come to God as a child filled with longing to be home with your Heavenly Father." The second of the three things the clothing is said to be.
- NOTE (sighting, `soul-faculties`): "a wondrous opening to a world so different from that which the mind perceives... great awakenings of perception, gifts that lay within the soul dormant will be awakened." Asserted, and the thread holds the dormant-gifts claim many times over.
- NOTE (sighting, `mind-vs-soul`): the same passage sets soul perception against mind perception in one clause. Not counted twice; recorded so the phrasing is findable.
- NOTE (considered and set aside, `divine-love-healing`): "this blessing of Divine Love is unlimited. It goes on for all eternity" carries the eternality on which that thread grounds primacy, but makes no comparative claim and does not frame the Love as healing here.

### 2016-09-11-af-francis-of-assisi | Many Are with You in Your Efforts | 2016-09-11 [not publicly posted]
- NOTE (context): a personal encouragement whose one doctrinal passage, the formation of Circles of Light, belongs to no minted thread and is described in the note below.
- NOTE (witness, `judgment-to-love`): the judged side again, with a discount the thread does not carry. "Many turned away from me thinking me not right within my mind, but I persisted and I prayed intensely and I sought out God and did not pay much attention to the judgements of others who did not understand and could not understand the true longings of my soul. Only God knows this and only God can respond to this." Nearest to it is 2016-07-04-af-jesus, which locates the injury in the seeker's consent. The facet here is different: Jesus answers judgement with love for the judger and with withheld consent, Francis answers it by discounting the judgement on epistemic grounds, since the judger cannot see what only God can. Held at witness because the passage is offered as encouragement rather than as teaching about judgement.
- NOTE (witness, `self-love-foundation`): the thread's territory in practice form, with a provenance clause. "I would suggest that you say these three things to yourself often to counteract these negative thoughts that have been placed within you: you are worthy, you are loved, and you are beautiful." The negative self-estimate is attributed to what others placed within him, which pairs with 2016-09-12-af-jerome, the conditions "placed within you as innocent children". Not an anchor candidate.
- NOTE (witness, `soul-purpose-emergence`): "It is your destiny. You feel it; you know it; you have seen it and you will express it but you must continue to grow and to grow into this role as a teacher and a healer", with the pace attached, "Your work just begins, beloved son, and it will unfold slowly to begin with." A scope observation worth keeping: the thread's argument is that purpose is not handed over in a single revelation but emerges as the soul awakens. Here the purpose has already been seen and known, and what emerges slowly is the growth into it. Same slowness, a different thing being slow. Whether the thread covers the growing-into as well as the coming-to-know is unsettled.
- NOTE (sighting, `divine-love-mediumship`): "you sit listening in a circle of Light for God to respond, and indeed God did respond and Touch each soul"; reception by a whole gathering rather than by an instrument, asserted in passing.
- NOTE (considered and set aside, `portals`): portal vocabulary would have come naturally here, since the message names Circles of Light three times and describes a gathering where seeds were planted and many spirits attended. The word is not used.
- NOTE (considered and set aside, `divine-love-healing`): no healing comparison; "a teacher and a healer" is a role name.
- NOTE (pattern, described and not penned): "Circles of Light, beloveds. Yes, Circles of Light. They form in accordance to God's Will and the desire of others who are drawn." A formation law in two sentences, God's Will plus the desire of those drawn. It stands next to the `portals` Foundation's conferral law, where God confers and humans supply the conditions of presence and prayer, and next to 2016-08-26-af-jesus, where a circle of dedicated souls in prayer is the precondition for a portal. The circle layer itself has no thread.
- NOTE (witness, `opposition-to-the-light-worker`): the same precedent offered the day before Jerome's, "Many turned away from me thinking me not right within my mind, but I persisted and I prayed intensely and I sought out God and did not pay much attention to the judgements of others". Opposition as the servant's ordinary condition, with the defence given as persistence and disregard.

### 2016-09-12-af-jerome | You Are Supported in Your Struggle | 2016-09-12 [not publicly posted]
- `soul-expiation` **[Objection-removed]** : clears the externalizing account of one's own negative states, a belief the thread has never had to refuse. "This resistance, this denial, and often an accusation that these feelings come from another or from a spirit or from a situation. No, my beloveds, those negative reactions belong to you, those feelings of depression, those feelings of hurt, those difficult thoughts, judgements, they come from you." Nearest to it is 2016-05-09-af-augustine, which names the three origins of the soul's conditions, inherited from generations, created by your own choices, absorbed by the environment, and which Jerome repeats almost exactly, "the conditions of this world, the conditions of your bloodline, the conditions that were placed within you as innocent children". The origins account is the same but the use is not: Augustine explains where conditions come from, Jerome refuses the seeker's disowning of the reactions. The facet here: one's own feelings are not to be put down to another person, a spirit, or a circumstance. Objection-removed rather than Elaboration because the move is a refusal of a stated contrary belief, in the thread's own vocabulary of encrustations.
- `free-will-and-self-responsibility` **[Elaboration]** : decouples responsibility from authorship. "Nonetheless, no matter the source, it is your responsibility and your struggle to take the actions necessary to cleanse your soul and God's Love begins the process, ignites a great flame that will burn away these encrustations, but it is for you to acknowledge what lay within you. It is for you to be strong enough to look." The Foundation, 2019-02-22-af-augustine, rests responsibility on authorship, its conditions being those "most often you have created yourself over long years of choices". Here the same law is applied where the seeker did not author the condition, so responsibility is for the cleansing and not for the cause. It sits between the Foundation, your healing waits on your own will, and the 2016-04-04-af-augustine Elaboration, you carry no responsibility for the choices of others: this says you do carry the cleansing of what others put in you. Adjacency recorded: this passage and the Objection-removed above are consecutive in the text, and the two claims are separable, ownership of the reactions and responsibility for the cleansing regardless of source.
- NOTE (witness, `judgment-to-love`): "Go along the streets. Say your prayers as you walk. Say your prayers as you join in the services of the churches. Continue to be the humble servant of God and do not let the error that you hear uttered by those within those churches inhibit the flow of your love. True humility is acceptance. True humility is adding your love to those who are seeking the Light, however false their beliefs." Nearest to it is 2016-08-25-af-brother-mandus, do not confront the rigid, since confrontation hardens the position one hoped to soften. Brother Mandus counsels restraint inside a conversation; Jerome counsels active participation in a service whose doctrine the servant believes false, and redefines humility as that acceptance. Held at witness because it is instruction rather than argument, and because it also reads as a `religious-error` adjacency, where the error is real and deliberately not corrected on the spot.
- NOTE (sighting, `mind-vs-soul`): the deadlock stated as cost, "as long as your mind continues to push and repress what must come forth you remain in a deadlock and in a dilemma in your being that saps your energy, your spiritual Light".
- NOTE (sighting, `prayer-soul-longing`): the prayer Jerome gives is a scripted formula of surrender, "Heavenly Father, these are my burdens, this is my cloak of pain". Recorded because the thread's argument is that words alone are not prayer and it is the soul's longing that answers, and the spirits nevertheless supply words. The message does not engage the tension, so nothing is claimed. Also a practice-file candidate.
- `armour-of-the-world` **[Elaboration]** : the armour as accumulated pain, named and handed over, a full account of what the clothing is made of. "It is hard for mankind to put aside their armor, their error, their anger, and their mistrust", and in the prayer, "this is my cloak of pain, this is the armor which I don in order to navigate this life and I wish that you would take it all." Here the clothing is surrendered to God rather than simply removed.
- NOTE (witness, `opposition-to-the-light-worker`): Jerome offers his own martyrdom as precedent to a man walking the streets with a message of truth, and converts it into a prediction, "it is very unlikely you will have my fate, still the arrows of criticism and scorn will come your way". Human undirected scorn, which the narrow `fortress-of-light` candidate excluded and the widened thread includes.
- NOTE (considered and set aside, `portals`): churches, meeting places and streets are named as sites of work, with no portal vocabulary and no consecrated-place language.
- NOTE (considered and set aside, `divine-love-healing`): "God's Love begins the process, ignites a great flame that will burn away these encrustations" is mechanism, not comparison.
- NOTE (context): Jerome of Prague (c. 1379 to 1416, burned at the Council of Constance) has been raised as a possible identification, on the martyrdom and the street preaching. Nothing in the text confirms it and neither the message file nor the spirit file carries it. Recorded only because it would change what this message is evidence of: if it holds, Jerome stands beside Martin Luther, John Wesley and Joseph Smith among the institutional witnesses of `religious-error`, and his counsel becomes the most striking thing in the message, a man executed by a church council telling a servant to go into the churches, pray in their services, and let their error pass uncorrected. Do not build on it until a message names him.
- NOTE (build, `armour-of-the-world`): the same image runs through one travelling circle. 2016-08-28-af-francis-of-assisi, remove the clothing of your mental assumptions and wade into the waters. 2016-09-09-af-andrew, put away your shield and your armour. 2016-09-10-af-augustine, remove your cloak and your armor and come to God as a child, with an ocean to bathe in. And this message, put aside their armor, "this is my cloak of pain, this is the armor which I don". Only the Francis message carries the `fear-of-full-immersion` argument. The other three use the same clothing to say three different things: defence against a suspicious nation, unworthiness before God, and accumulated pain to be handed over. This is the argument-and-image question at scale. A roster that admits the image will collect all four; a chain that admits only the argument will keep one. The image appears again at 2016-10-15-af-confucius and 2016-10-24-af-confucius.

### 2016-09-13-af-goldie | Move in the World | 2016-09-13 [not publicly posted]
- `free-will-and-gods-plan` **[Objection-removed]** : clears the passivity belief in three sentences. "So many sit and wait, thinking that God will make manifest His Will without any effort upon their part. This is not true. You must move in the world." The failure mode is then named outright, "But the one who sits and waits is lost." Nearest to it is 2016-05-12-af-augustine, the working protocol, where initiative is permitted but must be brought to God and asked to be guided and blessed. Augustine licenses the seeker's initiative; Goldie makes it a condition, so that God's Will does not manifest without the seeker's movement, and names the cost of waiting. The thread's other members treat the human contribution as willingness and surrender; none says the plan stalls without motion. Recorded with the message's weight: it is 217 words and the argument occupies three sentences. Objection-removed because the text explicitly negates a stated belief, "This is not true", which is the role's definition.
- NOTE (sighting, `earth-as-healing-ground`): "So you see the healing waters did heal and then God and His joyful tears blessed you with the rain." Same spirit as 2016-04-19-af-goldie, "let God's beautiful Earth draw from you those feelings that disturb you, for the healing is there in the ground, in the trees and the flowers". What the candidate needs is a message that says how the healing works. This one does not: it reports an outcome, and the waters seem to be a real place where people bathed rather than the natural world acting on the soul. So it carries the candidate's image again without its argument, the reverse of the usual case under the argument-not-image ruling.
- NOTE (sighting, `provision-for-service`): "God will bring towards you as you move towards His intention there will be many blessings, experiences and opportunities to serve." Opportunity rather than provision. The thread answers what is supplied to a God-guided work, not what is opened to a soul in motion. Fenced so it is not mistaken for a provision claim.
- NOTE (sighting, `bell-toll-instrumentality`): "you met the beautiful soul who was delighted to be with you in conversation and who was touched deeply by your presence"; an instance of the effect rather than a statement of the mechanism.
- NOTE (considered and set aside, `divine-love-healing`): "the healing waters did heal" is physical and comparative of nothing.

### 2016-09-14-af-augustine | Being in the Light Takes Conscious Effort | 2016-09-14 [not publicly posted]
- `earth-conditions-and-prayer` **[Elaboration]** : the strand in which we help make the field ourselves, stated thinly at 2016-07-11-af-confucius and here more strongly, with consent as the hinge: "The conditions of this world and the thought energies of this world which cloud and clog the actions and minds of mankind are powerful indeed and when you choose to engage in these thoughts and these conditions, you bring yourself down from the Light and reinforce a darker place." In the Foundation, 2016-02-29-af-luke, the field arrives like weather and buffets every soul. Here its hold is consented to, engagement is a choice made thought by thought, and the seeker who engages adds to what he is standing in. One ambiguity recorded and not resolved: "reinforce a darker place" may mean the seeker's own condition rather than the field itself. The role rests on the consent claim, which is unambiguous.
- `mind-vs-soul` **[Elaboration]** : the divergence read as cost. "And often this is in conflict with the state of your soul, for your soul is in Light, it continues to engage in its progression, its shedding of conditions that are not in harmony with God's Love. It expands and grows each time you pray to God to receive this Gift of Love and yet through your free will you often place your consciousness within these mindful areas and emotions that are not productive and are counterproductive towards your soul growth." Nearest to it is 2016-08-19-af-andrew, on which faculty is permitted to direct a life. What is added is that the two faculties can hold opposite conditions at the same time, and that the placement of consciousness is itself a free-will act with a price attached to the soul's growth. Worth setting beside 2016-03-17-af-alec-gaunt, which carries the same simultaneity under `soul-expiation` as reassurance, the soul growing even when the mind cannot perceive it. Augustine reads the identical structure as a warning. The same observation with opposite pastoral force.
- NOTE (witness, `free-will-and-self-responsibility`): the law extended to the angels, and prayer named as what licenses their help. "We in spirit who watch you and watch over you can only wait for you to climb back out of your condition... Yes, we do assist you but we cannot always assist you... And indeed when you pray for assistance you do receive it. This is the first choice when one feels these heavy conditions... Prayer, prayer, my beloveds, is important, indeed crucial, for this opens the possibilities for us to assist you." The Foundation already carries the spirit-side limit, that no spirit can override God's Laws. What is named here is that assistance is gated on the seeker's asking, and that the spirits' posture in the meantime is enforced waiting. Held at witness because the limit itself is not new. Followed up at 2016-10-16-af-augustine below.
- `opposition-to-the-light-worker` **[Elaboration]** : relocates the cause inside the seeker. "When you allow inner reactivity, strong negative emotions to flow freely within you, you do indeed attract negative energies and forces to you, forces which are all too happy to reinforce these conditions. No, you are not immune, my beloveds." Where the Foundation has forces organized against the light-worker, here they are drawn by his own state, and the defence changes with the cause, from a fortress of prayer to discipline of thought plus prayer. The second of the thread's three directions.
- NOTE (witness, `healing-channel`): the maturity precondition, "Each of you must grow to a sort of maturity where you can control these conditions within you in order to be a channel of the highest magnitude, in order for us to use you in powerful ways". The thread already holds the clear-channel requirement, and what is added, thought and emotional discipline as the content of that clearing, is carried by the two roles above and not counted a third time.
- NOTE (witness, `preparation-to-be-a-rock`): the readiness register with its content given, "it is time to step ever further forward upon this Path, to walk with maturity, wisdom and strength", and "You begin to shed some of your humanness, begin to don the mantle of the celestial being". -- promotion candidate at build; this is a full statement of what the inner readying consists of outside the members.
- NOTE (sighting, `incremental-journey`): "In this there are transitions and challenges, ways of being that must be adhered to in order to continue the transitional process"; asserted.
- NOTE (considered and set aside, `portals`): with this message the Serbia stay ends, in a land the messages describe as heavy and in need, with gatherings, Circles of Light, churches and meeting places all named, and no portal vocabulary anywhere. At Blackpool the word came from several spirits. Frankfurt was silent too, and together they support the reading that the word belongs to a place rather than travelling with the people (see 2016-10-15-af-confucius).

### 2016-09-15-af-augustine | The Reaction to Your Light | 2016-09-15 [not publicly posted]
- `bell-toll-instrumentality` **[Elaboration]** : the signal is not selectively attractive. Every member describes the radiating Light drawing souls toward God; Augustine says the same Light provokes according to what it lands on, and that both responses scale together: "the power of the Light within your souls causes a response and a reaction in the world, and as your Light grows so will the reaction intensify." Drawing close: "a recognition from their soul to your soul, a recognition of the Light." The other half: "From others there is a negative response, a desire to snuff out your Light, a reaction of suspicion, of fear and anger from those who are not close to God... they look to see your flaws." -- Nearest to it is 2016-01-18-af-confucius, the Love as an active agent igniting longing in others unawares, the stronger the Love the more resonant the toll. What is new is the thread's own scaling law applied to the negative case, so that growth in Love predicts hostility as reliably as it predicts attraction.
- `judgment-to-love` **[Elaboration]** : the judger diagnosed, and the non-reciprocation rule stated as a rule. On the judger: "There are those who feel jealousy and who are competitive, and all of this is a condition of the mind, yet their souls may indeed have the Love within yet their minds continue in their reflexive responses towards you." On the response: "Do not react in the way of anger to anger, judgement to judgement, fear to fear." -- Nearest to it is 2016-07-04-af-jesus, the judged side with the injury located in the seeker's consent. What is new is that a soul may hold the Love and still run reflexive hostility from the mind, which detaches the hostility from the judger's soul condition and is the cleanest ground this thread has for refusing to judge the judger. Adjacency recorded: this entry and the bell-toll entry above share the message's opening paragraph, and are separable, the bell-toll role resting on the scaling claim and this one on the mind-and-soul diagnosis with the non-reciprocation rule.
- `opposition-to-the-light-worker` **[Elaboration]** : supplies what the thread most needed, a reason why opposition comes at all and a law governing it. The Light itself provokes, and both responses scale together: "the power of the Light within your souls causes a response and a reaction in the world, and as your Light grows so will the reaction intensify." The hostile half described: "a desire to snuff out your Light, a reaction of suspicion, of fear and anger from those who are not close to God... they look to see your flaws." The defence given as non-reaction: "Do not react in the way of anger to anger, judgement to judgement, fear to fear." -- The scaling law makes hostility a sign of growth rather than of error, which the narrow targeting version of this thread could not say. The third of the three directions, and this message also holds a `judgment-to-love` and a `bell-toll-instrumentality` Elaboration, each resting on a different part of the text.
- NOTE (witness, `sensitive-souls-redemptive`): "you are not perfect. It does not take a great deal of research to see your flaws, you are human. Yet these aspects of yourself... are minor compared to the power of the Love that flows through you." Adjacent to the thread's claim that the Love within the growing soul is its protection, but the protection here is proportional rather than energetic, the flaws outweighed rather than the soul shielded.
- NOTE (sighting, `mind-vs-soul`): the mind-soul split applied to a third party rather than to the seeker, which is unusual for the thread. Carried above in the `judgment-to-love` role and not counted twice.

### 2016-09-16-af-john-the-beloved | Will You Walk with God? | 2016-09-16 [not publicly posted]
- `preparation-to-be-a-rock` **[Elaboration]** : the footing relocated, and the safety claim stated outright. "You must hold fast, trusting in God, relying upon your faith, the power of your soul's perceptions, listening to God's Will and you will weather any storm. Anything that comes will bypass you and you will be safe. You will stand upon the rock of God's great Will and lead others in this Truth." Nearest to it is 2016-12-07-af-andrew, in the coming storms you will be the calm, steady souls others look to, with 2017-09-13-af-keea-atta-kem carrying the same claim as "you will be a rock in a world disrupted". In every member the prepared soul is itself the rock; here the soul stands on a rock which is God's Will, so its steadiness is derivative and conditional, and the message names the condition, "to truly understand what God's Will is, your souls must be fully awakened by the Love." The inverted rock image is the visible marker but is not the ground of the role. The ground is that the thread's stability is here made contingent on a prior soul-awakening, together with the personal-safety promise, which the thread otherwise holds only at sighting level.
- NOTE (witness, `knowing-your-own-soul`): the knowing listed as a requirement inside a commission, "know your own soul and God's", set among the other things the ministry asks: shed what is not in harmony, pray without fail, stay receptive to guidance, and expect none of it to unfold without effort. The thread's members argue that self-knowledge is the route to God; this places it inside a list of duties without arguing it, so it is held at witness.
- NOTE (witness, `vanguards-of-change`): "to walk this world unencumbered" and "to set aside your own needs so that God may use you in purposeful and unencumbered ways in this world". Nearest to it is 2016-08-27-af-augustine, the release of one's own forward plan. Read together, these give a series of encumbrances: material burdens (2016-03-06-af-jesus), one's own intentions and plans (2016-08-27-af-augustine), and here one's own needs, a step further in than either. Held at witness because it is one clause and because the series may be one idea restated rather than three claims.
- NOTE (witness, `prayer-soul-longing`): prayer as the precondition for service rather than for the seeker's own growth, "Without your time with God, without allowing God to infill your soul with His Love you cannot act as his emissaries of Love and Truth." Nearest to it is 2016-07-11-af-confucius, the daily cadence and the cost of omitting it. The service-precondition framing recurs in this circle's material, so it is a repetition rather than a new facet.
- NOTE (sighting, `earth-changes`): "the world is undergoing deep change and God requires those with wisdom and strength, perception and love, to help usher the children into a new consciousness"; the changes named to motivate the calling, with no account of what they are or why they come.
- NOTE (context): the words placed in the seeker's mouth in italics, "I am ready to make that commitment that you long for me to make", were considered as a spirit-given prayer and ruled not a prayer, so there is no practice file. Recorded because it marks the line for the spirit-given-prayer ruling: words given with an instruction to say them are a prayer, as at 2016-09-12-af-jerome; words placed in the seeker's mouth inside a rhetorical question are not.

### 2016-09-18-af-jesus | I Will Lead You upon the Path | 2016-09-18 [not publicly posted]
- `incremental-journey` **[Objection-removed]** : the expectation of a dramatic shift refused outright, to hearers who were holding it. The objection is named first, "your minds often speculate and wonder and create ideas of what is to be", and then cleared: "There will be no dramatic shifts of understanding, my beloveds. No, it will come incrementally within the flow of the Father's Love as it continues to change you, beloveds, and create within you a new reality, a new way of seeing the world, a new way of knowing God." Nearest to it is 2019-04-05-af-augustine, the incremental apprehension of truth, which states the increments positively, as description or reassurance; this one denies the alternative, and it locates the increment in the flow of the Love rather than in the seeker's effort or the mind's retreat.
- NOTE (build, `incremental-journey`): `pentecost` argues the two ways a soul awakens, the sudden Pentecostal infilling and the gradual way, and the readiness the sudden way requires. This message tells these hearers the sudden way is not for them, without mentioning Pentecost, the disciples, or readiness, and offers no second way at all. So it takes up half of that thread's argument and rejects the half the thread exists to explain. Both threads have a claim on this passage and neither has a fence against the other. The registry note on `pentecost` says to keep it distinct as the question of how souls awaken, but that does not say which thread owns a message that denies the sudden way without discussing it. To settle before either is built.
