- NOTE (sighting, `free-will-and-gods-plan`): "what will be will be within the Will of God and unfold in the timing of God's great Wisdom and Plan", with the counsel "Do not worry of things you cannot change or influence". The premise asserted; the Foundation holds it developed.
- NOTE (sighting, `mind-vs-soul`): speculation named as a mind activity to be set down, "Do not overburden your minds with speculation".
- NOTE (sighting, `vanguards-of-change`): "you have chosen this, this mission"; the chosen-mission claim that 2016-03-18-af-john-the-beloved carries developed under `free-will-and-gods-plan`.

### 2016-10-15-af-confucius | There Is a Purpose for You | 2016-10-15 [not publicly posted]
- NOTE (context): a homecoming blessing of 390 words. Its two substantial claims, the unseen plan and the dawning awareness of purpose, are both carried at member level elsewhere.
- NOTE (witness, `soul-purpose-emergence`): "the dawning awareness that there is a purpose for each one of you and to allow this to manifest in your lives, in beautiful flowering expressions of your soulful gifts". This is the thread's argument, purpose emerging as a dawning awareness rather than arriving as a revelation, stated in passing inside a blessing. One qualification worth recording: Confucius pairs the individual purpose with a place in a collective work, "you will find your place within this work", where the thread's other members are about the soul's own purpose. Whether the place-in-the-work facet belongs here or to `vanguards-of-change` is worth weighing at build.
- NOTE (witness, `free-will-and-gods-plan`): "though within your minds you do not see the plan unfolding, but indeed it is, beautifully and in accordance to God's Will". Nearest to it is 2015-12-14-af-augustine, God's Will cannot be known from the mind. The invisibility of the plan to the mind is already the thread's property; this asserts it.
- NOTE (witness, `armour-of-the-world`): carries the water half of the cluster rather than the clothing, "Open your souls. Drink in these living waters. Allow yourselves to be fully immersed in this great Love." -- Held at witness for a reason worth keeping: the `fear-of-full-immersion` Reframe sets sipping against immersion and makes drinking the half-measure it surpasses, while Confucius uses drinking and immersion together as one invitation. So the water images were not fixed in opposition across this circle's vocabulary, which is why the water half is treated as flexible figure and the clothing half as the argument.
- NOTE (witness, `preparation-to-be-a-rock`): the protection promise in one clause, "though the storm rages around you, God's love and protection, His peace and Light, surround you."
- NOTE (sighting, `bell-toll-instrumentality`): the travels reported as effect, "bringing the Light of God's Love wherever they went and many souls were touched by their efforts and their prayers and their conversations".
- NOTE (considered and set aside, `portals`): this message marks the circle's return from England, Germany and Serbia. No portal vocabulary appears in the Frankfurt and Serbia messages from any spirit, unlike the Blackpool messages. With 2016-09-14-af-augustine, this supports the reading that the word stayed with the place and did not travel with the people.
- NOTE (considered and set aside, `divine-love-healing`): "you will be enveloped and healed and carried and guided" is a benefits list with no comparison.
- NOTE (context): with this message the circle is back in Gibsons after Belgrade and Serbia (2016-09-10 to 2016-09-18), and the tone changes at once. The Serbia material is dominated by the servant under scorn, the armour and cloak image, and the cleansing of what the world put in a person; the homecoming messages, this one and 2016-10-15-af-seretta-kem, turn to purpose, the expanding work, and the Earth's changes. Recorded so the change can be found if the archive is asked what the 2016 travels produced.

### 2016-10-15-af-seretta-kem | Earth Changes and Your Soul Condition | 2016-10-15 [not publicly posted]
- `earth-changes` **[Elaboration]** : the purpose of the changes stated as the Earth's own readying, and the seeker's stance specified. On purpose: "There will be indeed many storms and shakings of the Earth, risings of the tide, as the Earth cleanses and readies itself for a new era of life upon your Earth." On cause, the registry's first link almost verbatim: "it is the disharmony which mankind has brought within themselves and within this world that has sparked these changes and responses... you cannot ignore the laws of creation, you cannot be immune to the world that responds to your indifference." Nearest to it is 2023-02-10-af-augustine, which moved the thread from what the changes are to what they are for. That member's purpose is the waking of humanity; this one's is the Earth readying itself for a new era of life, a purpose belonging to the planet rather than to us. A second facet, recorded but not the ground of the role: the registry's last link, the awakened soul meeting the changes as God's channel rather than as a victim, is here given its content, adaptability and hope, "be flexible, willing to change and adapt as all around you changes", "you cannot carry fear but hope".
- NOTE (witness, `preparation-to-be-a-rock`): the readying strand of an earth-changes message, which the log has split before on this spirit's material. Guidance to be listened for, "Have your ears pricked to the guidance that will be given", and the protection promise, "you will be protected and carried through this time". The readying strand is present without the changes being explained, which is why the split was made. Compare the split made at 2017-09-13-af-keea-atta-kem, where the earth-changes reading was called the primary home.
- NOTE (sighting, `sensitive-souls-redemptive`): "the eyes of your souls begin to open and the awarenesses that come with the soul enlivened with God's Divine Essence"; growing perception as a mark of soul growth, without the sensitivity-and-cruelty argument that defines the thread.
- NOTE (build, `earth-changes`): recorded so the two are not conflated at build. That thread is the man-made darkness-field acting on the individual soul and neutralized by daily prayer; this message is the planet's own response to human disharmony. The word disharmony does the work in both, which is exactly why the fence is worth writing down.

### 2016-10-16-af-augustine | The Opening of Your Soul Perceptions | 2016-10-16 [not publicly posted]
- `soul-faculties` **[Elaboration]** : the refinement has no ceiling, and the teachers are inside it. "As this Love continues to accumulate within your souls the awakening, the opening of your perceptions continues for all eternity", and then the claim that gives it force: "We in the Celestial Kingdom see in a greater capacity than yourselves, but we also understand that that seeing, that knowing of truth continues for all eternity and we too are upon a journey of understanding just as you are but we are a little further ahead than you." Nearest to it is 2018-10-16-af-seretta-kem, the Chrysalis, the gifts as seeds in chambers and the kaleidoscope ever-expanding. The Chrysalis makes the seeker's own unfolding unbounded; this removes the implied ceiling above it as well, since the Celestial spirits are on the same journey and only a little further along, which changes what the thread's endpoint is.
- NOTE (witness, `mind-vs-soul`): the provenance of the mind's contents, and the consequence for choosing. "A mind that often is distorted and manipulated and is filled with the thoughts and ideas of another, implanted within each individual through their education and experience. This creates a certain reality of life that in some respects is illusionary, has no basis in truth", closing the loop, "Your choices then are based upon shadowy realities that are often filtered and manipulated by the mind and this in turn brings inaccurate decisions based on inaccurate perceptions." Nearest to it are 2016-05-16-af-simon, the obstacle located outside the person as informational overload, and 2016-08-25-af-matthew, the mind constituted by whatever is poured into it and hardening over time. Neither traces the contents to other people's ideas arriving through education, and neither carries the decision-consequence, that inaccurate perception yields inaccurate choice. Held at witness because both neighbouring facets are already held, by those two messages.
- NOTE (sighting, `knowing-your-own-soul`): the message's subject is the difference between the awareness of the mind and the awareness of the soul, which is `mind-vs-soul`, and it does not make the soul an object of knowledge or treat self-knowledge as the route to God. Recorded so the assessment is not made twice, since the vocabulary is close enough to invite it.
- NOTE (witness, `divine-love-mediumship`): the every-soul avenue and the ways it works. "We come close to you and give impressions and visions so that you may see further... We are with you in your dreams, as you pray to God in your quiet times, as you walk the street, as you do your work." Nearest to it is 2019-01-26-af-andrew, which widens the thread from designated mediums to everyone through the Laws of Attraction. A facet worth weighing at build: Andrew's widening is about gifts and establishing communication, while this describes the spirits augmenting the seeker's perception rather than delivering anything, which joins this thread to `soul-faculties` at the point where the two overlap.
- NOTE (witness, `free-will-and-self-responsibility`): the assistance gate named at 2016-09-14-af-augustine, here from the same spirit with its law attached: "When you ask for our help you receive it. When you walk with God in His Love you open a great channel of assistance and love towards you and this is how the Law of Attraction works." Still not an argument about the gate: two sentences inside a message about soul perception. But with 2016-09-14-af-augustine, the pair carries the gate and the law that governs it, which may be enough at build.
- NOTE (sighting, `incremental-journey`): "each time you move a little forward in your understanding you come closer and closer to the truth"; the thread's claim asserted, with the mind-lagging-the-soul image, "your souls are much further ahead than you realize and indeed your minds struggle to catch up", carried above under `mind-vs-soul`.

### 2016-10-17-af-keea-atta-kem | Let Your Light Shine | 2016-10-17 [not publicly posted]
- `self-love-foundation` **[Elaboration]** : the entailment routed through perception rather than through judgment. "As you begin to turn your gaze upon yourself and acknowledge how precious you are, how beautiful you are, how wondrous is your creation... in this way you then have eyes to see the beauty in others." Nearest to it is 2019-04-05-af-augustine, self-acceptance rendering judgment of another impossible, with 2016-02-29-af-confucius carrying self-love as the way through judgment, anger and fear. Both of those reach others through the removal of judgment; this makes self-recognition a precondition of sight itself, so what is gained is the capacity to perceive another's beauty rather than the removal of an obstacle. Not an anchor candidate, since the message's spine is being a channel of Light and the self-love passage is one paragraph within it.
- NOTE (witness, `bell-toll-instrumentality`): "Do not hide your Light but allow it to shine forth, always, in all its purity and beauty... to be a Light, to bring joy wherever you go", with the hunger named, "how the world hungers for this". Nearest to it is 2016-04-12-af-andrew, joy as the carrier that does the drawing. The joy-as-carrier claim is already the thread's; this restates it.
- NOTE (witness, `healing-channel`): the method reduced to its minimum, in answer to her own question. "And how is this accomplished? By merely being close to God and in being close to God you are attuned to the flow of His Love in the world." Nearest to it is 2016-02-10-af-confucius, the working method, pray for God to touch the soul through you, act in resonance, nourish your own soul first. What this does is strip the method of technique altogether, where the thread's members give the seeker something to do. Held at witness because a subtraction is hard to distinguish from a restatement in one sentence.
- NOTE (sighting, `simplicity-of-the-path`): the same sentence read from the other side, simplicity as the whole of the method.
- NOTE (considered and set aside, `divine-love-healing`): "the healing and comfort to go out those whom God desires to touch" is a benefits clause with no comparison.

### 2016-10-19-af-augustine | Divine Love Creates a Bond with the Father | 2016-10-19 [not publicly posted]
- NOTE (context): 275 words of encouragement in a prayer meeting. Its claims, the bond formed by the inflowing Love and the healing of the wounds every mortal carries, are held developed elsewhere.
- NOTE (witness, `soul-expiation`): the universality premise in passing, "heal you from your deep wounds, wounds that come with every mortal who lives a life in this world". Nearest to it is the Foundation, 2015-03-27-af-andrew, every soul carries wounds requiring purification. Asserted, not developed.
- NOTE (sighting, `prayer-soul-longing`): the anchor image against drifting, "So many in this world drift and have no anchor... it is important to secure yourselves with the Heavenly Father." Adjacent to 2016-01-23-af-augustine, where the same spirit calls prayer the anchor and foundation of the Path Divine. Here the anchor is the bond itself rather than the practice, a small shift and not worth a role on one sentence.
- NOTE (pattern, described and not penned): the bond as a thing formed and then load-bearing, "in this the bond is created with God and that bond creates within you a great and deep strength, opens your awareness to many deep truths, brings meaning and purpose to your lives". Several threads touch it, `divine-love-without-knowing` on reception, `prayer-soul-longing` on the practice, `soul-faculties` on what opens, but none holds the bond itself as its object. Too thin to pen on one short message.

### 2016-10-19-af-goldie | Divine Love Brings Surprises | 2016-10-19 [not publicly posted]
- NOTE (context): 274 words, and its one doctrinal claim is carried at witness level below.
- NOTE (witness, `guidance-as-seed`): "God has a plan for each soul who is willing to listen to their guidance, their promptings, and their inspirations. They come from God and His angels." The thread is guidance arriving not as a dramatic sign but as a seed of thought or small inkling that the soul must honour and act on. This does not build that argument: the promptings and inspirations are named and their source given, but nothing is said about how they arrive, how they grow, or what honouring them requires. The willingness-to-listen clause is the nearest it comes to the honour-and-act step. Held at witness; the growth claim is not addressed here.
- NOTE (witness, `free-will-and-gods-plan`): the plan asserted at the level of the individual life, "God has a plan for each soul", with the seeker's part given as non-resistance, "Do not resist God's Will but accept in gratitude all the blessings that come to you". Nearest to it is 2016-05-12-af-augustine, the working protocol, initiative permitted but submitted. Asserted rather than argued.
- NOTE (build, `incremental-journey`): this message promises unanticipated change, "surprises full of change for each one of you... maybe even bringing shifts in where you live", while 2016-09-18-af-jesus told the same circle "There will be no dramatic shifts of understanding". Not a contradiction, since the Jesus message concerns the pace of understanding and this concerns the circumstances of a life, but the two will surface together on any query about sudden change, so the distinction is recorded here rather than discovered later.
- NOTE (sighting, `vanguards-of-change`): "all those places that you travel to, that you never imagined you would go"; mobility in the register of delight rather than commission.

### 2016-10-24-af-confucius | Consider Your Progress | 2016-10-24 [not publicly posted]
- `incremental-journey` **[Elaboration]** : the thread's one practical instruction, retrospection as the way increments become visible. "Beloved souls, walk in the Light with each day; mark your progress, beloveds. If you look back upon your life and see how your perceptions have changed your behaviour, your capacity to love. And, yes, these things are often subtle and gradual, but much has changed within each one of you", repeated as the message's title claim, "Consider your progress, my beloveds, even those of you who have just stepped upon this Path." Nearest to it is the Foundation, 2015-05-03-af-confucius, from the same spirit: growth advances in leaps then trickles, impeded by world and self yet lifted by God. Every member describes the increments from outside, as doctrine or reassurance; this hands the seeker a method for detecting them, and names the deliberate backward look as what makes gradual change legible. It also extends the claim to beginners, who by the thread's own logic have least to look back on.
- `challenges-as-blessings` **[Elaboration]** : the causal direction reversed, against a nearly identical sentence frame. The Foundation, 2016-08-11-af-alec-gaunt, builds why hardship strengthens: "each time you overcome an obstacle, each time a lesson is learned, each time you turn your pain into joy you become stronger." Confucius uses the same construction to say something else: "each time that you surmount a barrier, that you overcome a fear, that you change your attitude to one that is more loving and forgiving you express the change that is happening within your souls. You allow your souls greater expression in your lives." In the Foundation the overcoming produces the strengthening; here the overcoming is evidence of a strengthening already under way in the soul, which makes the same events diagnostic rather than causal, and joins this thread to the `incremental-journey` role above, since that is precisely what the backward look is reading. A tension recorded and not resolved: Reframe is the better role if the causal direction is genuinely reversed rather than loosely worded, and Elaboration is taken as the more modest of the two. Worth settling before the thread is built, since the Foundation is its presumptive anchor, and an anchor saying challenges strengthen you sits awkwardly beside a member saying they only show you what already changed.
- `armour-of-the-world` **[Capstone]** : completes the argument by making it an exchange rather than a removal. "As you shed your cloaks of error and don your cloak of Light you become a clear and effective channel." In the other messages of the cluster the clothing is taken off so that something can happen; this one says what is put on instead, which is what turns a repeated figure into an argument with an end.
- NOTE (witness, `mind-vs-soul`): the agency axis restated, "As your minds acquiesce to the will of your soul, the Light within your soul and as your soul becomes more in alignment with God's Will". Nearest to it is 2016-08-19-af-andrew, on which faculty is permitted to direct a life.
- NOTE (witness, `bell-toll-instrumentality`): "You must teach this by your example. You must bring inspiration to others through your love, through the words you speak, the actions you take." Nearest to it is 2016-03-17-af-alec-gaunt, at least half of that teaching is through your example.
- NOTE (sighting, `self-love-foundation`): "a capacity to love yourself and to love others" in one clause, the entailment named without argument; the developed statement in this span is 2016-10-17-af-keea-atta-kem above.
- NOTE (build, `armour-of-the-world`): the image recorded at 2016-09-12-af-jerome runs through 2016-08-28-af-francis-of-assisi, 2016-09-09-af-andrew, 2016-09-10-af-augustine, 2016-09-12-af-jerome, and 2016-10-15-af-confucius on immersion. This message gives it a different form: not removal but exchange. "As you shed your cloaks of error and don your cloak of Light you become a clear and effective channel." In those messages the clothing is taken off so that something can happen; this one says what is put on instead. That matters for the scope question raised against `fear-of-full-immersion`, because a cluster with its own internal development, worn then removed then exchanged, begins to look like an argument rather than a shared figure of speech.
- NOTE (context): "you gain the world, my beloveds, as you claim your soul and as your soul is claimed by God." The Francis of Assisi spirit file describes him as gaining the world by giving it up. Two spirits, the same paradox, different mechanisms. Recorded so the pair can be found together.

### 2016-10-26-af-augustine | Be Open to God's Touch | 2016-10-26 [not publicly posted]
- NOTE (witness, `mind-vs-soul`): Augustine turns the chain's premise into an instruction, and gives a reason for it: "put aside your expectations that are born of your mental ideas and go deeper to that place of your soul, that place is a place separate and different from your mind... for it is your soul that is the image of God, my beloveds. It is your soul that is able to truly know God and to recognize the blessing of His Love which is the essence of the Creator." The reason is the interesting part, that the soul can recognize God because it is made in God's image, which is a ground for the claim rather than a restatement of it. He states it in passing while telling the circle how to pray, so it sits alongside the chain; if a later message builds on it, it could move in.
- NOTE (witness, `incremental-journey`): the increment stated as plainly as anywhere, "It does not come all at once, my beloveds. It comes with each prayer, each longing you make to God and this will set you upon a road." Augustine says the same thing in 2015-03-28-af-augustine, so this restates rather than adds.
- NOTE (sighting, `prayer-soul-longing`): earnestness named as the condition of the opening, "as you are earnest and pray for this opening of Love. It will come." A clause in passing.
- NOTE (considered and set aside, `divine-love-without-knowing`): the opening line describes people arriving from different backgrounds with a shared longing, "so many who've come from different perspectives, understandings yet have a soul longing to come together." That is a gathering across traditions, which is a different thing from receiving the Love without knowing what it is, so it is not evidence for this chain.
- NOTE (sighting, `angelic-accompaniment`): "with each one of you stands an angel and with each one will come a blessing as you open." 2018-07-07-af-aaron states the same idea more fully, "you shall always be accompanied by an angel, who will protect and guide, teach and inspire." Neither argues it.

### 2016-10-29-af-andrew | The Soul Longs for God's Love | 2016-10-29 [not publicly posted]
- `mind-vs-soul` **[Elaboration]** : Andrew adds two things. First, an explanation of why the mind ends up in charge: not that it overpowers the soul, but that the soul is left untended. "The soul in most is neglected, unrecognized, un-nurtured and so the soul languishes as the mind has control and precedence and provides the modus operandi for one's life." Second, and this is what earns the message its place, he runs the argument at the scale of the whole world: "If the entire world was motivated from their soul longings, from that place of soul, all of your dilemmas and darkness and error would disappear, for the soul intuitively knows what is right and good and loving." Rather than arguing the two faculties inside one person, this one makes the mind's dominance the explanation of the world's darkness, and the soul the cure. Nearest to it is 2018-03-02-af-andrew, where the same spirit says the soul's wisdom must be allowed to rise above the mind's calculations.
- NOTE (witness, `divine-love-without-knowing`): a distinction that helps the chain and could easily be misread as contradicting it. Andrew separates being embraced from knowing you are embraced. The embrace "is always present, always there... It is more a soul must discover that this possibility exists always," while "to be embraced by God in a conscious way is to know your own soul." So knowing does not cause the embrace; it changes whether you experience it. Worth quoting alongside his harder line, "those who are ignorant of the facts... do not receive this embrace," so that line is not read against the chain.
- NOTE (sighting, `redemption-universal`): the widest form of the universality claim, "There are no limitations to God's Love, there are no exceptions to God's Love." It carries none of this chain's actual subject matter, the dark spheres and the soul that can still turn toward God, so it stays outside.
- NOTE (sighting, `soul-expiation`): the soul described as buried, "that place is covered by layers upon layers of conditions, encrustations," with nothing said about how the covering is removed.

### 2016-11-06-af-jesus | Be Disciples of the Truth | 2016-11-06 [not publicly posted]
- NOTE (witness, `vanguards-of-change`): the commission restated with the promise of opened doors, "each must speak these truths where the opportunity and God's guiding hand brings you forth in this world... for God will take you by the hand and show you the way, open the doors and connect you with all the loving souls." This chain is about being sent and being free to go, and neither the release of possessions nor the readiness to travel appears here, so it sits alongside. Jesus says the same at 2016-01-08-af-jesus.
- NOTE (witness, `soul-purpose-emergence`): two things worth keeping. Jesus ties the emerging of purpose to the Love's own work, "As the Love within builds and cleanses and ignites your great souls, you will see more clearly, more emphatically, what it is that you must do in this world. You get glimpses and see with eyes hardly open." And he makes the gifts collective as well as individual, "each of you have great capacities and gifts and perspectives and perceptions which in combination will make you a powerful teacher of these truths." Logged as a witness rather than a member: 2016-03-29-af-james already carries the gifts lit up by the Love.
- NOTE (sighting, `bell-toll-instrumentality`): the chain's own phrase used for something else, "to bring the bell toll that will awaken the souls and show the way to joy and light and life everlasting." Here the bell is the intended result of speaking, teaching and working openly, where the chain is about a soul that reaches others without a word. Recorded so an appearance of the phrase is not mistaken for the teaching.
- NOTE (sighting, `provision-for-service`): doors opened, with nothing said about provision.
- NOTE (sighting, `healing-channel`): "to bring the blessing of healing" appears in a list of what a channel does; an item rather than a teaching.

### 2016-11-07-af-andrew | Love Is the Key | 2016-11-07 [not publicly posted]
- NOTE (witness, `judgment-to-love`): the chain's argument run forwards. "How can you judge your brothers and sisters if you feel the joy and acknowledgement of how much you are all loved, how much you are loved?" Nineteen months later 2018-06-26-af-matthew runs the same line backwards, finding the hurt of feeling unloved underneath judgment. What Andrew adds is the positive form: knowing yourself loved is what makes judgment impossible. It is one question inside a message about receiving, so it sits alongside for now.
- NOTE (witness, `self-love-foundation`): this message meets the chain from the receiving side rather than the giving side. The chain holds that you cannot love others while condemning yourself. Andrew holds that you cannot receive abundantly while doubting you are loved: "All that inhibits this blessing from flowing into yourselves in great abundance is that doubt within you, that you are loved." The two agree that a settled self-regard opens everything else, and differ on whether it comes from loving yourself or from accepting that you are loved. Whether the chain's argument admits the receiving side is an open question.
- NOTE (build, `fear-of-full-immersion`): that chain's founding message, 2016-08-15-af-alec-gaunt, says the obstacle to full reception is the fear of being wholly immersed, and specifically not unworthiness. Here Andrew names the one obstacle as the belief that you are not loved, reinforced by the world: "So much of the world distracts you and brings a message to you that you are unworthy." Either two different obstacles are being described, or believing yourself unloved is a distinct thing from believing yourself unworthy. Both passages are worth quoting when this is settled.
- NOTE (witness, `bell-toll-instrumentality`): the proportion stated plainly, "Yes, as you change so the world changes. As you are touched so a Light increases in this world because it increases within you," with the means named, "so that it shines through your eyes, that is felt with your embrace... That all you meet, and all you encounter including your own self is touched." What is new is where the light travels, in ordinary bodily contact rather than as an ambient signal. 2016-08-27-af-brother-mandus uses the same image of a look and an arm around another, so the two belong together.
- NOTE (witness, `earth-conditions-and-prayer`): the world's conditions are usually described in this chain as weather, heaviness, dark clouds. Here they say something: "So much of the world distracts you and brings a message to you that you are unworthy, that humanity is not a beautiful creation of God and this is not true." No prayer remedy is attached, which is what the chain's argument turns on, so it sits alongside.
- NOTE (sighting, `worthiness-as-gate`): a person's belief about being loved treated as the gate on what they can receive. This message builds a whole address on it, and 2016-10-26-af-augustine puts it in a clause: "each and every one of you is worthy, is precious and beautiful. Each one of you is able to receive this gift as long as you allow this Touch." `fear-of-full-immersion` and `self-love-foundation` each touch it without holding it: `fear-of-full-immersion` rules unworthiness out of the obstacle it names, and `self-love-foundation` is about how a person regards themselves rather than what they can receive from God.

### 2016-11-21-af-confucius | The Blessing of the Father's Love | 2016-11-21 [not publicly posted]
- NOTE (witness, `divine-love-healing`): the chain rests the primacy of Divine Love on its being eternal. Confucius rests it on what it is made of: "it is the highest blessing, the most powerful of all blessings, for Love is the most powerful of all substances in this universe." He states the healing directly too, "as God touches you within your souls, so it heals those wounds within, those conditions brought about by your experiences in life and the conditions of this world that cling to your souls... gathering you into His bosom, giving you deep respite." He does not compare Divine Love with the other healing energies, which is the move this chain is built on, so it sits alongside. A second ground for the primacy claim is worth having if another message argues it.
- NOTE (witness, `soul-expiation`): the healing described from the gentle side. What is removed is named twice, "those wounds within, those conditions brought about by your experiences in life and the conditions of this world that cling to your souls," and the register is relief and nurture rather than the surfacing of buried pain that the chain's other messages describe. Worth keeping beside them.
- NOTE (witness, `incremental-journey`): the increment itemized, "with each blessing of Love you receive as you pray to your Heavenly Father seeking His Touch, His Love within you, your consciousness shifts, your soul awakens, your understanding grows, your perceptions deepen and you are changed." Augustine states the same increment in 2015-03-28-af-augustine; what is new here is the list of what changes each time.
- NOTE (witness, `purpose-of-life`): not another statement of what the purpose is, but a statement of where a person has to stand to see it. "your lives in this world, so fleeting, yet as your soul grows you will be able to see from that perspective of the soul and understand the true purpose of your life, the true unfolding of your existence."
- NOTE (sighting, `soul-faculties`): "your perceptions deepen," in a list.

### 2016-11-26-af-andrew | When You Follow Your Guidance, and Be a Channel of God's Love, Many Blessings Follow | 2016-11-26 [not publicly posted]
- `healing-channel` **[Elaboration]** : this chain holds that a divided purpose clouds the channel. Andrew names the particular division that does it, which is choosing for yourself who should be blessed, and he gives words to say instead: "it is not for you to say 'this person must be blessed, that soul will be touched', no my beloveds, it is for you to say 'Father I am a channel of your love, whomever you desire to touch, to ignite this longing within the souls of others, so be it. Make me that channel for you.'" He states the result in the chain's own terms, "In this way you will not be entangled within your mind as to what you must do," and repeats the criterion, "Together with a singleness of purpose you fulfill God's desire." The founding message, 2016-01-04-af-seretta-kem, says healing begins in the soul and that singleness of purpose makes a clear channel. This says what singleness of purpose actually consists of, and supplies the surrender as something a person can pray. Note for the build: this message contains no healing language, and its outcome is ignited longing rather than healing.
- NOTE (witness, `bell-toll-instrumentality`): the mechanism restated with two differences worth recording. God is the one who ignites, rather than the soul's own radiance: "God is igniting a desire within the souls who are in proximity to you." And what makes it work is simply being near people. The chain's own note about not knowing it is happening is here too, "you will touch many souls, often unknowingly."
- NOTE (witness, `simplicity-of-the-path`): "It is so simple, my beloveds, so simple," with the manner named, "you will be that channel of love in innocence, simplicity and grace." Said twice, not built out.
- NOTE (sighting, `vanguards-of-change`): the commission being lived rather than taught, "You would not be here without His guidance and you are here because it is sanctioned and guided." The circle is travelling; nothing is said about what had to be released to make that possible.
- NOTE (context): Andrew gives the circle words to pray, recorded separately as a practice. The same passage is the evidence for the `healing-channel` role above; one passage, two uses.

### 2016-11-27-af-augustine | Drawing Many Kindred Spirits | 2016-11-27 [not publicly posted]
- `divine-love-without-knowing` **[Elaboration]** : the chain's other messages are about a soul not recognizing what is already in itself. This one is about two souls recognizing it in each other. Augustine places people without the teaching inside the light without qualification, "Though they may not share your perspective, your clear understanding of the mechanisms of Divine Love, nonetheless they are in the light and know the light, and are drawn to the light," and makes the recognition mutual and immediate, "there will be deep within you and deep within the others an acknowledgement of this common spiritual light and love." He attributes the drawing together to the Law of Attraction and to God's Will. The nearest message is 2016-09-02-af-augustine, where the same spirit explains why a person fails to recognize the Love in themselves; here the recognition happens from outside, between people.
- `bell-toll-instrumentality` **[Elaboration]** : Augustine says where the signal travels and then changes what the person carrying it is doing. The transmission is in ordinary contact, "this is done not just in words but in a smile, a look, a gaze of love, a touch, an embrace." The effect is the chain's own, "You bring the bell toll of truth and this bell is heard within the souls whom you are with and excites a desire and brings an opening door." Then the shift: the person is not the source of the signal but the completion of a circuit, "you must simply be in harmony with God and attuned to His Love, a channel thereof, so that you may complete the connection between God and the soul whom you gaze upon." -- The nearest message is 2016-04-12-af-andrew, which names joy as the thing others actually perceive. New here: the seeker's part is to conduct rather than to emit. Weigh together with the note at 2016-11-07-af-andrew, which records the same carriers.
- NOTE (witness, `vanguards-of-change`): the promise of travel in the spirit's own voice, "We will take you to many places and show you many things and connect with you many souls," with a release named, "as you lay down your resistance and completely embrace the Will of God." What is released here is resistance rather than the material burdens the chain's other messages describe.
- NOTE (sighting, `judgment-to-love`): the closing list, "do not judge, do not resist, enfold, embrace, accept, and love."
- NOTE (sighting, `simplicity-of-the-path`): "you must simply be in harmony with God." The word is used in its ordinary sense here rather than as the chain's claim.

### 2016-11-27-af-confucius | Confucius Welcomes the Flock | 2016-11-27 [not publicly posted]
- NOTE (witness, `portals`): the circle described as forming a portal, "When you come to pray together and come to form a great channel of love, a portal of light to this world." 2016-05-09-af-seretta-kem says the same of a praying circle. Both sit against the chain's founding teaching, 2019-01-26-af-seretta-kem, which is explicit that "You cannot build a Portal of Light... you have not the capacity or the ability to do so." The two fit together the way that chain already resolves them: people supply the conditions and the prayer, spirits build, God confers.
- NOTE (witness, `provision-for-service`): the human side of the bargain stated as the whole of it, with the worry named and located on logistics and personnel: "Do not fret, my beloveds, over the details of how this will be accomplished and who will be involved, my beloveds, God is involved, God will accomplish this task. All that we require of you is for you to come together in harmony and prayer." The chain's messages answer worry with provision; this answers it by shrinking the requirement to harmony and prayer and nothing else. It is asserted in a short greeting, and provision itself is never mentioned.
- NOTE (considered and set aside, `affect-as-soul-marker`): joy is here in the candidate's own terms, "a new joy will awaken within you... Your souls feel the joy of this, allow the joy to enfold you and become the light that is true to you." But joy appears as something God's touch produces, never as a sign to be read about the state of one's soul, which is what that candidate is about.
- NOTE (sighting, `prayer-changes-world-conditions`): "a portal of light to this world," pointing outward, with the mechanism belonging to the portal material rather than to this chain.

### 2016-11-27-af-james-reid | The Value of Knowing Your Souls | 2016-11-27 [not publicly posted]
- `mind-vs-soul` **[Testimony]** : the chain's claim offered as evidence by a man who spent his Earth life on the mind's side of it and now reports back. "Things are so much clearer in these higher spheres and simpler and I see now that the power of God's Love is the ultimate blessing and truth and much of what I perceived when I was in the flesh was just a lot of clutter around this truth." He generalizes it, "as you progress and your souls expand in Love these things of the mind will have less importance and relevance. The things of your souls will be what comes forth and has power." And he leaves a test anyone can use: "When you come across a new concept, an idea, ask yourselves, how does this increase the love in my life... because all else, in many respects, is irrelevant." He is careful not to condemn his old habit, which the chain should keep: "I do not blame myself for immersing my mind in all of these aspects... It is the nature of your world to inform the mind."
- `divine-love-mediumship` **[Elaboration]** : a fact about how communication works, given as an aside by the communicator himself. The instrument can lower a threshold, and that determines who is able to speak: "I hope that I will be able to speak again through this instrument for he has let down a barrier allowing those of us who are not yet a Celestial Angel but are well on our way." He also reports the experience from his side, "How different it is to speak on this side of the voice." The chain's founding message, 2016-03-27-af-seretta-kem, is about how faithfully a truth survives the passage into a mortal mind. This is about who is admitted in the first place, and on whose decision. Worth reading beside 2016-12-06-af-seretta-kem, which tests a message by its content: this message passes that test while its author is explicitly not a Celestial angel, so the rule sorts content rather than rank.
- NOTE (witness, `simplicity-of-the-path`): complication located in the seeker's own accumulated spiritual information, judged from the far side of death. "It is all very simple and it is based on the foundation of love"; "You need to shake off the clutter and the error and the irrelevant details and focus, focus, my friends, on love"; "Prayer, faith, trust in God, an eagerness for love; this is simple." The chain has held complication in how a person lives and in how a servant speaks. This would add a third, complication as knowledge-hunger.
- NOTE (witness, `death-transition`): a first-hand report of the continuing life, "I am very happy in the realms of spirit. I dwell within the 5th sphere and am finding much to do here and am learning much as I progress through this beautiful sphere," with a commitment to keep working alongside those still here and an invitation, "you will join me in this beautiful sphere of light when it is your time to pass." It illustrates the chain's claim that death is a transition rather than arguing it. 2019-04-08-mc-mary is the nearest, a soul newly passed speaking back to those who loved her.
- NOTE (sighting, `conduit-not-scripture`): "that which is the fabrication of the mind, part of the intellectual ruminations of imagination and interpretation of truth will fall away." Close to that chain's claim that words are never the sacred thing, but aimed at the seeker's own reading rather than at the standing of a recorded message.
- NOTE (build, `two-paths`): James Reid is a Divine Love seeker who says he dwells in the fifth sphere and is "not yet a Celestial Angel but well on our way", and he invites the circle to join him there. The `two-paths` chain and the "Soul Spheres & Progression" subject both describe the numbered spheres as the natural love path's progression. Not a contradiction on its face, since souls receiving Divine Love pass through the spheres, but it is a plain case of a Divine Love communicator placing himself by sphere number.
- NOTE (witness, `knowing-your-own-soul`): titles the thread's claim and adds the shared frame, that knowing your souls is what a circle gathers to do and helps one another do: "This is your focus; this is why you come together. To help one another in this great effort, to rise above the conditions of this world, the conditions of your mind and truly come to know your souls."

### 2016-11-27-af-james-reid-2 | Many Souls on Our Side Are Part of This Effort* | 2016-11-27 [not publicly posted]
- NOTE (witness, `prayer-changes-world-conditions`): the same claim seen from the other end. The circle's prayers reach a company on the spirit side far larger than the room: "so we gather many souls on our side of life, vastly more than are in the flesh... there are many involved in these efforts and are touched by your gatherings and prayers together. Much is accomplished." The chain's founding message describes prayer travelling outward to distant souls; this describes spirits gathered around the praying circle itself, which is nearer to the lattice material.
- NOTE (sighting, `the-whole-requirement`): 2016-11-27-af-confucius and this message, through different spirits, state the human contribution as a closed list and refuse the question of how the work will get done. Confucius: "All that we require of you is for you to come together in harmony and prayer." James Reid here: "What is required of you is that faith that you are indeed answering God's call and doing your part in this effort." `provision-for-service` holds the other half of that bargain, what God supplies; this candidate holds the requirement side, stated as a complete minimum.

### 2016-11-27-af-jesus | Jesus Brings His Blessing to the Group | 2016-11-27 [not publicly posted]
- NOTE (sighting, `bell-toll-instrumentality`): "as you journey upon this road many will follow you," with the passing on named, "You will share with others this great, wondrous truth and enfold them in it as God's instruments of Love." A blessing of a hundred and twenty-eight words, with nothing said about how it works.

### 2016-11-27-af-mary | Mary Brings Her Blessings to the Group | 2016-11-27 [not publicly posted]
- NOTE (context): forty words promising Mary's presence to those ministering to a praying gathering. Recorded so the message is not searched twice.

### 2016-11-28-af-goldie | Why Complicate Life? | 2016-11-28 [not publicly posted]
- NOTE (witness, `simplicity-of-the-path`): the chain's claim in its lightest register, "Oh, how you wish to complicate life when it is so simple and joyful. Go with God and go with joy and life will be simpler, easier, lighter." What is new is that joy is the thing doing the simplifying, and that the complication is located in the seeker's own heaviness rather than in doctrine, in speech, or in a way of living. Two sentences in a short message.
- NOTE (considered and set aside, `affect-as-soul-marker`): joy runs through this message, "in the laughter and in the joy, in the openness... you bring a great light" and "I will help you to know joy," but never as a sign to be read about the state of one's soul. The same thing happens at 2016-11-27-af-confucius. With both in view, the candidate would be clearer for a line saying that joy prescribed or promised does not count, only joy read as a sign.
- NOTE (sighting, `earth-conditions-and-prayer`): the world's conditions described as felt weather, "this world so full of stress and pressure and tightness," with a remedy that is not prayer, "This is a tonic, a blessing that many need." 2016-08-27-af-brother-mandus similarly widens the remedy, to love in ordinary contact.
- NOTE (sighting, `bell-toll-instrumentality`): teaching by flavour rather than by argument, "these people are used to sugar and you brought honey... continue to feed them the honey of God's Love, to show them, to surround them, and envelop them."

### 2016-11-28-af-luke | Luke Welcomes Those Who Are a Part of the Punalu'u Gathering | 2016-11-28 [not publicly posted]
- `spiritual-fellowship` **[Elaboration]** : Luke states the exchange as an obligation running both ways, at the moment a circle is being formed: "each of you has something to give to your brothers and sisters here and each of you has something to learn from your brothers and sisters in this circle." He gives the instruction that follows from it, "Continue to observe and be together as you form your bonds, and form this Circle of Light," and then the ground of the whole thing: "each of you comes from a different life experience, a different perspective, yet, each one of you is touched by God's Love and this Love is what binds you together and brings you to a place of common understanding and experience." The founding message, 2015-03-28-af-augustine, says that sharing between souls opens awarenesses none of them would reach alone. Luke says what makes that sharing possible across real difference, which is the Love they have each received rather than any shared background or belief, and he makes the exchange a duty rather than a benefit.
- NOTE (sighting, `prayer-changes-world-conditions`): the outward claim in the closing lines, "All those you pray for will be touched and comforted, healing will spread out to many. This is part of your effort and dedications together." Stated without mechanism.

### 2016-11-28-af-moses | Unite in the Name of Love | 2016-11-28 [not publicly posted]
- `spiritual-fellowship` **[Elaboration]** : Moses names what stops a fellowship forming at all, and it is the mirror of the failure this chain already records. "when you are bogged down in judgement, in fear, in assumptions, this cannot happen. You will have pockets, several here, several there but not unity, not true brotherhood and sisterhood." He states what is required, "you are required to give each a choice in a loving way, to open the door in a loving way and say 'you are welcome'," and he accepts that some will not come without forcing them, "Invariably there will be some who cannot and will not release those conditions... I am not suggesting that you accept those in a forcible way." He offers his own commission as evidence that it can be done: "I was charged by God to bring together the Jews, my people, and there were many such conditions and strife within that family; much larger than your own, and yet we did bond together, we did unite, we did put aside our differences," closing "no challenge is too great when God has his hand in your life." In 2015-06-15-af-joseph-smith the failure is belonging bought at the cost of truth. Here it is belonging that never forms, because judgment and old slights keep people apart, and the cost is named for the work rather than for the individual. This and 2016-11-28-af-luke were given the same day to the same circle and divide the subject cleanly, Luke on what fellowship gives and Moses on what prevents it; weigh them together. Testimony is a defensible alternative reading for this message, on the strength of the commission paragraph.
- NOTE (witness, `judgment-to-love`): the cost of judgment stated as a loss rather than a diagnosis, "When you push your brothers and sisters away in judgement or fear, you push God away, you push love away," with the positive form following, "When you envelop and accept, when you make an effort to go beyond your own barriers and reach out to others in sincerity, in humility, you bring the light to you." 2017-06-15-af-augustine holds that judgment is incompatible with Love; this says the brother and God go out the same door together.
- NOTE (sighting, `earth-changes`): "the changes that are coming in this world" in a subordinate clause, and "as time grows short, it is time for action."
- NOTE (considered and set aside, `comfort-dulls-longing`): "You are richly blessed. You are given a feast for the soul. God nurtures you at every turn." That candidate holds that met needs produce too little longing. Moses uses the same abundance as the ground of an obligation to give more, which is the opposite move.

### 2016-12-06-af-seretta-kem | Talk on Mediumship: The Laws of Communication and Rapport | 2016-12-06
- `divine-love-mediumship` **[Elaboration]** : Develops the conditions and laws of communication that the Foundation (2016-03-27) opens. The circle's prime objective must be to seek the Divine Love, not a message; harmonious conditions built by prayer and desire draw the angels close, and the Laws of Communication and Rapport come into effect from those conditions. Carries the thread's discernment principle: if a message has no mention of God's Love and is not about the opening and flowering of the soul, it is not from an angel, and each must discern through their own soul what is true. Elaborates the mechanism into a practical account of what makes reception clear and how to test it.
- NOTE (sighting, `mind-vs-soul`): the soul's wordless truth narrowed and distorted as it passes into the material mind, so no medium is perfect; shared with the 2016-03-27-af-seretta-kem entry rather than a separate contribution here.

### 2016-12-07-af-andrew | Be Calm in the Coming Storms | 2016-12-07
- `preparation-to-be-a-rock` **[Elaboration]** : the rock argument stated plainly: "For in any storm the soul that is calm, the soul that steadies those around it is the one that will be looked to," with the prepared soul's task named, "you, my beloveds, must be bold, declare the truth, bring a stability, strength, calm and peace and truth to all in the flow of God's Love," and the source of the steadiness, "The most precious thing that you possess, my beloveds, is a soul enlivened by God's Love." Compare 2017-09-13-af-keea-atta-kem.
- NOTE (witness, `earth-changes`): the world and the soul changing together, "The world is changing and you are changing," with the Earth to be restored, "this precious earth must be healed and brought back into balance," in the prophecy register.

### 2017-01-16-af-keea-atta-kem | Accept Every Unique Soul: Their Differences Will Not Harm You Spiritually | 2017-01-16
- `sensitive-souls-redemptive` **[Objection-removed]** : clears the fear that blocks the thread's next step, that growing sensitivity leaves a soul exposed. Sensitivity comes with growth, "the more sensitive you are as you open and progress in the Divine Love... you obtain certain sensitivities," and what is felt passes, "there may be some peripheral effect upon your being, but nothing that is permanent, nothing that is damaging," because the Love within is the protection, "With the blessing of God's Love, you have a great deal of immunity and protections from these conditions," so "do not fear or worry."
- NOTE (witness, `opposition-to-the-light-worker`): the cause located in the seeker's own thoughts, "Thoughts do indeed attract and bring like energies close. This is a Law and this Law comes into effect whether you are aware of it or not," with responsibility kept, "you are responsible for your own thoughts, my beloveds... this does not mean that you are a victim of these things."
- NOTE (witness, `judgment-to-love`): "You are learning to not judge those who you find different in this world... to allow the individual soul to walk their individual paths in love," each soul's path its own.

### 2017-01-25-af-alec-gaunt | Building a Foundation of Spiritual Growth and Development | 2017-01-25
- NOTE (considered and set aside, `challenges-as-blessings`): God's presence carries a soul through every challenge, "whether it be joyful or sad, difficult, or fulfilling, God will be there," but nothing here says hardship strengthens the soul.
- NOTE (sighting, `prayer-soul-longing`): "Reach out to God in this simple way with a longing and a desire to receive this essence, this blessing and to be with God."
- NOTE (context): the message gives a short prayer as words to say, introduced as "that step in faith that says" and later called "this simple prayer": "I am with you. I love you. Dear God be with me that I might feel your love...", preserved as a standalone practice file (content/practices/prayer-2017-01-25-af-alec-gaunt.md).

### 2017-03-27-af-james | Release Self-Will and Embrace the Perfect Will of God | 2017-03-27
- NOTE (sighting, `challenges-as-blessings`): struggle as part of the passage from self-will to God's Will, "It does not come easily. It does not come without a struggle," with progress marked by meeting it, "upon each success and shift and healing within, you will know joy and a true awakening." The struggle belongs to the transition rather than strengthening the soul as such.
- NOTE (sighting, `free-will-and-gods-plan`): self-will released into God's Will at the scale of one life, "What holds you back, beloveds, is not so much those old conditions within you as it is your willful desire to plant your feet, one within the world of man and one within the world of God. At some point you must choose."

### 2017-05-11-af-jesus | Jesus Speaks of the Pentecost | 2017-05-11
- `pentecost` **[Foundation]** : Jesus explains why the Pentecost came to the disciples, then asks the circle in front of him whether the same conditions are present in them. The conditions first: the crucifixion left them shattered, "they were distressed and traumatized by this great loss... They felt that their future had been dashed upon the rocks"; his appearances restored their attention, "this time they heard my words, they were eager to know"; his final departure confused them again, since they had expected him to rule, "to be the Prince of their country, to be the Messiah of their world"; and out of that confusion came the prayer that was answered, "in their earnestness, that deep earnestness rising from their souls, each one with that single voice, 'Father touch me, open me to your Love, show me the way, Light my path', came a great rush in response to their earnestness." Then the two modes, stated as two forms of one awakening rather than as success and failure: "This awakening is not necessarily done through the Pentecostal experience, for in many cases it is a gradual experience. Bit by bit, you continue to inch your way forward," while "those who are blessed with the Pentecost will find great, great benefits and will come to know the Will of God." And the claim that governs which mode arrives: it is never withheld, "when you pray for this and it does not come, it is not that God does not wish for you to have this experience, it is that you are not ready to receive in this way." Pairs with the Testimony (2019-02-15-af-matthew): Matthew supplies the experience from inside it, Jesus supplies the conditions that produced it.
- `prayer-soul-longing` **[Elaboration]** : Reception is proportional to the depth of the asking, and Jesus measures the circle against that standard to their face: "if it is your sincere desire to receive the Pentecost, the depth of your longing, the breath of your desire, the capacity of your souls to open and to receive this gift... must be far greater than any of you here are feeling and desiring to receive." He also names what the prayer must be allowed to contain: not only joy but the unwelcome material, "To cry out not in joy, but in fear. To cry out in confusion, to cry out tinged with error." And he prescribes escalation: "with each day may the intensity of your desire for this... be doubled and redoubled until you come to that point where the Pentecost is assured."
- `who-jesus-was` **[Elaboration]** : First-person account of the post-crucifixion appearances, given as mechanism rather than miracle: "I transformed my spirit body into flesh and walked upon the earth for some time and spoke with them and told them of the Truth once more." He also names what the disciples wrongly expected of him, placing that specific misunderstanding at the source rather than in later tradition. Sits with the 2018-11-26 statement that he will never return in the flesh: the appearances were temporary and purposeful, not a precedent.
- `free-will-and-self-responsibility` **[Elaboration]** : The thread's claim stated sharply, and the message's door: "The only thing that holds you back from the awakening of your soul is you. Your inability to have that faith, that trust, that awareness, that all you essentially need is God." Stated immediately after the assurance that the help is already in place, "we, the angels of Heaven, will surround you and support you and inspire you and protect you," so responsibility is placed without abandonment. Closes: "This is between you and God."
- NOTE (witness, `incremental-journey`): the gradual road named and dignified, "in many cases it is a gradual experience. Bit by bit, you continue to inch your way forward." -- fenced: the modes framing is logged under `pentecost` above, per the registry's scoping. This thread's own question, why growth comes in increments, is not addressed here.
- NOTE (witness, `fear-of-full-immersion`): vulnerability named as the cost of the full opening, "In this opening comes a great vulnerability and an acknowledgment of those conditions within your souls that are not in harmony with God." -- the Foundation (2016-08-15-af-alec-gaunt) treats vulnerability as an imagined loss; Jesus treats it as a real requirement. Promote if a later message develops the difference.
- NOTE (witness, `soul-faculties`): the awakened soul described by what it can do, "a soul awakened knows far greater than the greatest minds on your planet... their mere embrace upon you will give healing and upliftment. The words they speak, will speak the simple Truth, their presence will be known."
- NOTE (witness, `divine-love-without-knowing`): the thirst that does not know itself, "they walk in a land without the waters of life, dry and parched. They don't even know their own thirst, they don't know where to turn."
- NOTE (sighting, `bell-toll-instrumentality`): presence doing the work without speech, in the same passage as the `soul-faculties` note above; recorded once.
- NOTE (sighting, `vanguards-of-change`): "To have those individuals in this world who are awakened in this way, will be a great boon to mankind, will help lead mankind from the darkness and the error."
- NOTE (sighting, `comfort-dulls-longing`): comfort as an obstacle to depth. Jesus tells a circle gathered somewhere pleasant that their circumstances work against them: "As you come together in this place of luxury, of abundance, of joyful interaction and Love together, in many ways your needs are met. You do not feel in crisis, you do not feel in crisis of your soul or your faith." The disciples were driven that deep by trauma and confusion; this circle has nothing driving them. He refuses to condemn the comfort, "This is not wrong, God wants you to have joy," which sharpens the claim rather than softening it: the good life is not sinful, it is merely insufficient to produce the necessary desperation. Distinct from `challenges-as-blessings`, which says hardship produces growth; this says ease produces shallowness, and those are not the same claim. -- worth noticing in other messages given at retreats and in comfortable settings.
- NOTE (build, `pentecost`): the transcription ends mid-sentence with no closing blessing, so the final turn of the teaching is lost. Relevant because this message is the thread's anchor.

### 2017-06-15-af-augustine | Lesson on Judgement | 2017-06-15
- `judgment-to-love` **[Foundation]** : a talk on judgment given at the circle's request, opening on the thread's premise: "The desire to judge, those feelings that you are uncomfortable with, how another is in the world, express to you things that reflect back to you your own soul condition and personality... In this you more readily judge yourself than anyone else." Perception is not the fault, "It is your reaction to that perception which is a good indicator of your own soul progression," and the rest of the argument is built on that ground: the judged side, "When someone is judging you, my beloveds, say a prayer for them"; love as what makes judging impossible, "When you send love how can judgement survive the infusion of love?"; conscious choice as the method, with a first-person template, "I will let go of my need and my habit to judge... Then when I am faced with the dilemmas of judgement, I will choose Love"; and no self-condemnation when one fails, "even when you fall into judgement, do not condemn yourself." Anchor.
- NOTE (witness, `self-love-foundation`): "For a soul that truly loves itself and is embedded within this pure Love of God there can be no judgment, only acceptance and perception," with self-condemnation refused, "There is no need to say, 'I am a failure. I am unworthy.'"

### 2017-08-21-af-matthew | Only the Soul Can Know God's Love | 2017-08-21
- `mind-vs-soul` **[Elaboration]** : Matthew corrects a specific misconception about the two faculties, which is that feeling belongs to the emotions and thinking to the mind, with the soul left out of the account. "The minds of men find it difficult to speak of feelings, suggesting that a mind cannot feel, rather it perceives a Truth as thoughts. This is untrue when it comes to the understandings of the soul, for a soul can feel its understanding, its comprehension of a Truth. For when a soul understands a Truth, all aspects of that soul are engaged." So soul-knowing is not a thought and not an emotion; it is a comprehension that arrives as feeling because the whole soul is engaged in it. The limit of the intellect follows: "Can the intellectual describe Love? No, one must feel Love to understand it," and the message's door, "A thousand words, a million books will never truly bring this Truth as it is experienced by that deep Touch from God." Notably he does not discard the mind. The prayer engages everything, "engaging your emotional self, engaging your material mind and engaging the perceptions of your soul," which puts this closer to the integration messages (2018-10-09-af-seretta-kem) than to the messages that set mind against soul.
- `mind-vs-soul` **[Objection-removed]** : Removes the objection the thread's own doctrine invites, that if the soul knows in a particular way, there must be a particular experience to check yourself against. Matthew forbids exactly that: "One cannot say 'oh, you have received the Father's Love because you have experienced this or that.' No, my beloveds, the experience is unique for each soul." He names the failure mode as dogma forming out of shared testimony, which is how it would actually happen in a circle like his: "do not get caught up in a form of dogma that says 'oh if you experienced this, then you have received the Blessing of the Father's Love.'" And he closes the door on standardisation, "There is no formula for this. There is no standard." He will say what is common, "often in this Touch comes joy, comes upliftment, comes an opening and expansion within," but immediately denies it the status of a test. -- this is the message's real contribution. Logged as a second line rather than merged, since it answers a different question from the Elaboration above: that one says how the soul knows, this one says why no one else's account of it can be used to measure your own.
- `spiritual-fellowship` **[Elaboration]** : The thread's argument in its practical form, and it develops the Augustine claim rather than repeating it. Augustine says other people's journeys reveal what your own cannot; Matthew says what the circle must do to make that possible: share the experience, "It is important to teach this my beloveds, to share in this experience together in prayer, to speak of your feelings, to speak of your perceptions," and then receive it without measuring, "Be open to the experiences of others. Accept their words even if they do not match your own." The failure mode is the same one he names above: a fellowship that shares experience will tend to standardise it, and that tendency has to be actively resisted for the sharing to be worth anything.
- NOTE (considered and set aside, `divine-love-without-knowing`): the message does not support an Elaboration for this thread. Matthew is addressing people who are praying for the Love and receiving it, and his subject is how the reception is known and misdescribed, not whether it can arrive without knowledge of the doctrine. The nearest ground is "belief is of the mind", which this message does not enter. -- the thread's claim is made elsewhere.
- NOTE (witness, `prayer-soul-longing`): prayer as the means of experiencing rather than of asking, "this, my beloveds, is why you continue to pray and long for this Love, to experience it on all these different levels of awareness."
- NOTE (witness, `soul-faculties`): feeling named as a faculty of the soul rather than of the emotions, in the opening passage above; recorded once.
- NOTE (sighting, `bell-toll-instrumentality`): "May this precious group continue to bring the Light of God's Love in its midst and be a channel of Love for this world."

### 2017-09-13-af-keea-atta-kem | Make Love Your Guiding Truth Through Earth Changes | 2017-09-13
- `preparation-to-be-a-rock` **[Elaboration]** : God orchestrates the readying: "this is God orchestrating your life, preparing you for what is to come," so that those who receive His guidance become rocks amid the storms, "You will be a rock in a world disrupted by conditions of change," and are guided to scientists, leaders and seekers who will take notice as their gifts and Love grow. Develops the Foundation from preparing refuges toward God preparing each person to be the steady presence others are drawn to.
- `earth-changes` **[Elaboration]** : what the changes are and what causes them: "the magnetic polarities are shifting, the energetic compass of this earth is shifting," animals drawn near homes "because they are confused," and the cause in the Laws of Creation, "when humanity ignores the Laws of Creation, there is a response. For every act, there is a reaction," on a planet that "is a closed system" and "cannot be put under stress ad infinitum without some response." This is the message's primary home; the `preparation-to-be-a-rock` line above is the be-a-rock and guided-to-leaders facet.

### 2018-03-02-af-andrew | Allow the Soul to Have Its Say | 2018-03-02
- `redemption-universal` **[Testimony]** : Andrew's own account of going from the Celestial Heavens to the Hells to help his brother Judas: "I came to him very often and brought my love for him... And in time, he found his way. In time, he became a Celestial Angel." The rescue seen from the rescuer's side, complementing Judas's own Testimony (2014-10-11-af-judas) from the inside: Love reaching even the Hells, enacted, and shortening the road, "my dear brother Judas found his way to Heaven more quickly than if he was left to his own devices."
- `mind-vs-soul` **[Elaboration]** : guidance is coloured by the condition of the one receiving it, "any guidance or communication from the angels that you may receive, all that that bubbles up from your soul into your mind, is not entirely pure," so the seeker must ask "how much of this is truly in accord with Love and with God's Will and how much reflects my own condition." The mind "greatly desires to dissect and delineate and make things either black or white," and the two ways of knowing stay divided "Until the condition of your mind and the condition of your soul is merged into Light and Love." (Also cited for the thread in the 2019-02-18-af-augustine NOTE.)
- NOTE (witness, `judgment-to-love`): non-judgment given as his own example, "Now, I could judge my brother for what he did. I could say, he deserved his fate but where would that thought originate? Not of the soul," with the instruction that follows, "Do not judge your brothers, beloved souls... But consider how you may uplift them."

### 2018-04-03-af-luke | Sins of the Parents | 2018-04-03
- `soul-expiation` **[Elaboration]** : develops the inherited strand alone, an answer to the question of the sins of the parents. Conditions pass from mother to child before birth, "A new soul is very sensitive indeed and absorbs much of these energies, these emotions, these conditions which the mother is expressing, thinking, feeling... So, it goes back generations," and through upbringing "by a sort of osmosis." The remedy is the Love, "It is the power of Divine Love that is the great healer, washing through the individual, cleansing the soul," and the seeker who receives it ends the line, "You will break the chain, beloveds... You do this not only for yourself, but for all humanity and all humanity to come."

### 2018-05-12-af-luke | Law of Compensation: Natural Compared with the Divine Path | 2018-05-12
- `two-paths` **[Elaboration]** : A teaching given in response to discussion among the circle's friends about the Law of Compensation, and the machinery beneath the Andrew Capstone (2016-05-02), which states the contrast in summary. The accumulation premise: "Every intentional deed, thought, condition that you experience or bring into yourselves has an effect upon your countenance... the memories of these things reside within your soul. They accumulate day by day." Natural path, conscious and one by one: "Those who walk the path of the natural love must make great effort to come into awareness these memories and conditions within the soul and address each and every one of these things... So they must comb through all these experiences in life, often when they are in the world of spirit, and find some resolution, some way of transforming them from negative to positive." Divine path, reflexive and by forgetfulness: "Rather than a state of memory and reliving these conditions that remain active within the soul, they are neutralized and there is a condition of forgetfulness within the soul... There is no sense of pain, only healing and self compassion," done "not necessarily done in a conscious way but done as a reflexive response of the Touch of Divine Love within your souls." Two facets the Capstone does not carry. First, at-onement named as the gate: "at-onement cannot come without the healing of the soul. At-onement does not come without the infusion of Divine Love to such a degree that there is a resonance between the mortal soul that becomes a Divine Soul and God's Soul," where the Capstone's contrast ends at unlimited progression without naming what the natural path cannot reach. Second, the sixth sphere described from the inside rather than named as a ceiling (see the rehabilitation note below).
- `soul-expiation` **[Elaboration]** : Names the Law and states its insistence in full: "The Laws of Compensation which insist that every part of your soul that is contaminated with darkness, memories and pain, be reconciled and brought to Light, bit by bit." Adds the positive ledger: "Those things, actions that are positive and of Light, help to strengthen that individual in their pursuit of cleansing the soul," so the accounting runs both directions rather than tallying injury alone. Adds the assisted-cleansing provision on the natural road: "In the world of spirit there are many who will assist those who are struggling... This God has provided in the world of spirit through His Compassion, through the compassion of those who are further ahead in their spiritual growth." And states the outcome as neutralization rather than erasure, with the mind's memory left intact: "Although the mind may retain these memories, the active energies, the negative energies that are associated with these sins within are indeed neutralized." Closest members: 2016-05-02-af-andrew (the deferred-cost argument, which this develops into the named Law) and 2018-07-14-af-seretta-kem (Reframe). Luke supplies the Law by name, its mechanism on both roads, and the positive ledger.
- NOTE (sighting, `two-paths`): a full and generous account of the natural destination. Sixth-sphere souls are described from the inside and at length, "Many souls are content to reside within this sphere, to continue their explorations of God's Creation, His Universe, to accumulate knowledge, to refine their gifts... Their interactions with one another are harmonious and joyful. They have a great deal of love within them for one another and for themselves," with the concession that the difference is presently indiscernible from where the hearer stands: "At the present state of your progression it would be difficult to truly discern between the joy of the perfect heaven and the joy of the Celestial Heavens." Distinct from 2016-08-27-af-brother-mandus and 2016-09-02-af-augustine, which decline to call natural love inferior; Luke instead honours the natural destination while keeping the ceiling explicit, "There is a state of harmony but it can go no further than this," and keeping the difference in kind, "you do however feel a difference in the quality of the Love of the angels and the quality of the love of those spirits who are advanced in natural love." Counted as an instance for the natural-love rehabilitation watch. Its bearing on the qualifying clauses of the `two-paths` spine and the `earth-conditions-and-prayer` remedy wording is the curator's decision (see Open questions in chains-threads).
- NOTE (build, `two-paths`): the thread's close is now split across two candidates. 2016-05-02-af-andrew holds Capstone and states the fork as a choice, closing on the open invitation. This message states the at-onement gate, the facet that names what the natural path cannot reach, which is arguably the truer close of the argument. The working reading that keeps Andrew: Andrew addresses a hearer who has not yet chosen, Luke expounds for a circle that already has, so Andrew closes the argument and Luke completes the doctrine. Decide at build; both belong in the Capstone section either way.
- NOTE (witness, `mind-vs-soul`): the counterfeit facet running the other direction. Andrew 2016-05-02 has the mind settled while the soul is not; Luke has the soul healed while the mind still remembers, "Although the mind may retain these memories, the active energies... are indeed neutralized." The pair together give the full asymmetry: mental resolution does not reach the soul, and soul healing does not erase the mind's record. Asserted in one sentence here rather than developed, so witness, but flag for promotion if a message builds the asymmetry; the two read together would make a strong section.
- NOTE (witness, `soul-faculties`): the natural path's ceiling stated in faculty terms, the same point 2019-01-21-af-augustine makes as a `two-paths` Elaboration. Sixth-sphere souls "refine their gifts in as far as their gifts are possibly recognized and expressed," a bounded development, against the Divine road where "The cleansing of your soul is just one step of many more openings and developments of the capacities of the soul." The earlier statement of the only-Divine-Love-opens-the-deeper-faculties facet; place beneath the Augustine entry at build.
- NOTE (witness, `death-transition`): the same condition-determines-your-place facet carried by 2016-05-11-af-james-padgett and 2019-02-10-af-augustine, here as advance preparation: "advancing the condition of your souls in both the natural and the Divine Love so that you will progress into higher spheres when that time comes of your passing to spirit." No new facet.
- NOTE (sighting, `incremental-journey`): the reconciliation itself incremental, "be reconciled and brought to Light, bit by bit." Asserted in a clause.
- NOTE (sighting, `challenges-as-blessings`): "though at times this journey may have its struggles and difficulties and challenges, the rewards that you reap... are immense, are immeasurable, are wondrous indeed." The thread's claim in its closing-reassurance form; asserted.
- NOTE (considered and set aside, `divine-love-healing`): "The power of the Divine Love is a great healing force, purifying the soul" is a strong sufficiency claim, but there is no comparison with other healing energies and no primacy argument, so it sits below the roster's explicit-claim bar. Same call as 2015-03-27-af-andrew. Recorded so the checkpoint is not re-litigated.
