- NOTE (pattern, described and not penned): possibly the pattern described at 2016-08-26-af-augustine (security in God), "do you not feel that you are secured with God and that within your souls comes a strength, a faith, a joy, a sense of Love?" Weaker than that message: security located in the relationship with God, but with no substitution of the world's securities, which was the distinguishing move. A partial instance, for the curator's judgment.

### 2018-05-17-af-augustine | 60/40 Soul Mind Guidance | 2018-05-17
- `mind-vs-soul` **[Reframe]** : clarifies the circle's reading of an earlier guidance as a 60/40 split between soul and mind: "This is not the goal. The goal is to have the mind dominated by the soul at all times," while a mind "completely subjugated by the soul is an unrealistic expectation." The image is mixing paint, the soul yellow and the mind red making "a beautiful orange, a sunset orange," because "It is not one or the other... it is not soul or mind." The corrective the thread's integration end state needs cleared before it.
- NOTE (witness, `soul-faculties`): "as you progress in the soul's awakening and attuning yourself to the faculties of the soul, other things come. You will be able to see others for who they are."

### 2018-06-18-af-matthew | The Reality of the Soul Can Be Merged with the Mind | 2018-06-18
- `mind-vs-soul` **[Chrysalis]** : Matthew reframes the thread's whole subject. The two minds are not a conflict to be won but two realities to be merged: "You have within you two minds my beloveds, the material mind and the soul mind. Each may entertain different realities at once, causing a great deal of confusion for those who are aware of both to try and reconcile these two realities." He then takes the seeker's disorientation, which the thread elsewhere treats as a problem, and recasts it as the beginning of an expedition: "You are the vanguards seeking to move forward. Scientists of the soul, discovering its mysteries and coming to a different Truth than what has been placed within your mind." The vista is stated three ways. It is unending, "It does not end. The awakening continues, the perceptions come one after the other, like waves on the ocean." It is permanent, which is the turn that makes this a Chrysalis rather than another Elaboration: "This reality is the reality that you will keep and will sustain you as you continue to grow within your souls," against the earlier warning that without Divine Love the shift "would become nothing more than a curiosity, a fleeting experience, an opening of perceptions of the soul that are not sustained." And it resolves rather than replaces: the merged state is "harmonizing the two and bringing a new perspective," with the mind fed by the soul rather than silenced, "the mind, that so wishes to be in charge of Truth and yet is wholly incapable of knowing the Truth of the soul without the soul awakened and feeding the mind with its perceptions." Closes on the promise, "eventually the veil is lifted and the true reality begins to take form within your consciousness." -- Chrysalis section member beneath the 2018-10-09-af-seretta-kem anchor. Matthew turns the conflict into an expedition and promises the new perception will hold; Seretta Kem describes the conflict ceasing to exist. Chronological order in the section puts Matthew first, which reads well: expedition, then arrival.
- `mind-vs-soul` **[Objection-removed]** : Answers the fear that this shift is a kind of madness, which the thread invites by claiming an alternative perception of reality. Matthew names it directly rather than avoiding it: "The thought of shifting from that comfortable place of understanding the world can be intimidating and can bring fear. At times, those who have shifted a great deal to other realities are considered insane and require intervention." His answer is carefully qualified rather than dismissive, and he does not claim all such perception is sound: "Of course there are times when this is appropriate, but often the reality that those who are within it is valid but not common." He then gives the test that distinguishes the two, and it is Love: without it "there is no true purpose of shifting one's reality," and the perception is not sustained. -- notable for conceding the other side of the case; worth keeping at build, since a thread arguing for a second way of perceiving reality needs an answer to this, and this message gives one.
- `soul-faculties` **[Elaboration]** : The faculties are not merely opened by Divine Love but held open by it, which is the facet the thread's other members leave implicit. "It is only the Divine Love of God that not only awakens this new way, but sustains and develops and brings it into fruition," against the alternative, perceptions "that are not sustained." The mechanism is stated as ignition: "the strength of the soul that has absorbed within it the power of the Divine Essence, igniting certain faculties which create a different perspective, a different Truth."
- NOTE (witness, `earth-conditions-and-prayer`): the world's thought conditions as an active force shaping what people take to be real, "the thought conditions of this world are powerful indeed and fixate certain realities, understandings, within most of you who dwell upon this earth."
- NOTE (witness, `vanguards-of-change`): the message's door, "You are the vanguards seeking to move forward... into a new dawn of Love and Truth that God intends for this world," with the circle cast as explorers rather than as recipients.
- NOTE (witness, `prayer-soul-longing`): the Law of Attraction applied to the communication itself, "Where there is that longing, that desire, we are able to come and be with you because you invoke the Law of Attraction and you invoke the Laws of Love."
- NOTE (sighting, `incremental-journey`): "this process takes time. There are shifts and adjustments, lessons and deep, deep perceptions."

### 2018-06-24-af-luke | Seek Humility and Compassion for Self and Others | 2018-06-24
- `judgment-to-love` **[Elaboration]** : the impulse to correct named as the mind's bid for superiority, "Can you truly call yourselves a channel of Love when you judge, pull back, desire to correct and to give advice as if you are superior and incapable of error yourselves?" The test is the seeker's own reaction, "What is your true and honest feeling when you perceive error in another? If it is compassion, you are responding with your soul's light," and the reaction is a mirror, "When you have an intense reaction of another's behaviour, realize that within you lies the same condition." New facet: what judging costs the work. Others are watching, their souls drawn while their minds look for reasons to resist, so "when you are amongst others and express judgment... you empower the minds of others to reject you and to reject the message that you give." Closest member 2017-06-15-af-augustine (the reaction as a measure of one's own soul).
- NOTE (witness, `self-love-foundation`): the seeker's own imperfection met with compassion, "you cannot be perfect... So, you must have compassion for yourselves, beloveds," and "please do not reject yourselves."

### 2018-06-26-af-matthew | Release Judgment, Embrace Love | 2018-06-26
- `judgment-to-love` **[Elaboration]** : what lies beneath judgment: the soul desires not to judge, the mind answers with anger and resentment, and "Beyond these feelings often lies a very deep feeling of being unloved and unrecognized in the world," from times of feeling "rejected and unloved." The test is daily and has a recognizable pass mark: facing someone who has stirred the old feelings over and over, "rather than the old patterns expressing themselves in your consciousness, there is love, compassion, and forgiveness, then you have passed the test... And so each day is a gift, each day a test, each day an opportunity to change, to choose, to grow in love."
- NOTE (witness, `self-love-foundation`): "Stop judging, my beloveds. Stop judging yourselves. Stop condemning yourselves and condemning others," with compassion for one's own patterns asked in place of self-condemnation.
- NOTE (witness, `mind-vs-soul`): the soul healed while the mind keeps the old conditions running, "God will indeed heal you of these conditions, these hurts within the soul. But because of the well-worn patterns of the mind, these conditions continue to be active within the thought patterns of the material mind," so "The mind and the soul must progress in tandem." The same asymmetry as 2018-05-12-af-luke.

### 2018-07-14-af-seretta-kem | Soul Expiation Through Pursuit of Truth, Release of Error | 2018-07-14
- `soul-expiation` **[Reframe]** : recasts the hard times of expiation as the times of greatest progress: "When you look back upon your lives beloveds, you will feel as much gratitude for these difficult times as you will for those times of joy. For, it is in these difficult times, the greatest progress is made, the most Love is infilled into your soul." The release is a birth with the angels attending, "It is like a birth within you, this release... Like midwives, we help you along," and its pain gives way to a joy "that is not reliant upon some condition from without you but a condition within that is steady and sure."
- NOTE (witness, `incremental-journey`): "this is a slow process, incremental in nature. It does not come all at once but in bits and pieces which you are able to deal with in a conscious way."
- NOTE (witness, `challenges-as-blessings`): the struggles as what strengthens, "the struggles that you have had, the tests that have been difficult. Yet, these things have carried you forward, strengthened you, built wisdom within you and this deep compassion for yourself and for others."
- NOTE (considered and set aside, `divine-love-healing`): the healing here is the Love clearing soul conditions, with no comparison to other kinds of healing, which that thread requires.

### 2018-07-16-af-andrew | Forgiveness | 2018-07-16
- `judgment-to-love` **[Capstone]** : a talk on forgiveness that states the thread's close: forgiveness is "a measure of your own soul's progression, as God infills your soul with His Love. So the urge to forgive, to let go, comes with this blessing," until "the love within your soul is so powerful and so present within all of you, that you forgive reflexively, no matter the intention of others." Its weight is put plainly, "Even a measure of this, no matter how small, is greater than all good deeds done in this world," and the failure state named, "When you judge and condemn another or even yourself and lack forgiveness and compassion, you hold your soul's progression from moving forward." Anchor.
- NOTE (build, `judgment-to-love`): the message holds forgiveness as both a decision and a reflex, "you must be strong in your decision to seek forgiveness always" alongside "you forgive reflexively," which answers the ordering question raised by 2016-09-01-af-jesus, where forgiveness opens the door: it begins as an act of will and matures into a reflex as the Love fills the soul.

### 2018-07-19-af-matthew | Death Can Be a Joyful Transitioning to Spirit | 2018-07-19
- `death-transition` **[Foundation]** : death as a change of conditions rather than an end: "the transition to the world of spirit will be celebrated, a changing of the conditions in which a soul lives and operates, which is the truth. It is nothing more than this." The fear is the mind's, "much of this comes from the mind, beloved souls, the mind that is so reliant upon the five senses of the body," and the self continues whole, "All that you are beloved souls, without your physical bodies, is the same as you are now. It does not diminish or disappear," within a progression that for the soul seeking God's Love has no limit. Anchor.

### 2018-07-30-af-keea-atta-kem | Prayer | 2018-07-30
- `prayer-soul-longing` **[Capstone]** : Keea Atta Kem gives a full treatment: prayer is the language of the soul and an act of faith opening a thread of light to God; names the Law of Activation as the mechanism ("It is prayer that brings the response from God enacting His Law of Activation"); repetition without feeling yields no real response. Ties the soul-longing argument to a named spiritual law.

### 2018-08-13-af-seretta-kem | Growing the Light Within Your Souls Is the Greatest Service to God | 2018-08-13
- `bell-toll-instrumentality` **[Elaboration]** : the light built within is itself the greatest service, whatever one's situation: "no matter what situation you are in in your life, it is your efforts in prayer, your desire to serve, your continued awakening that brings change to this world... it is the light that you carry and the light that builds within you that will have the greatest effect," and "Though your walk may be gentle and quiet, it is the light that you carry that pushes back the darkness." The Augustine Capstone, 2018-09-01-af-augustine, states it in full.
- `earth-changes` **[Elaboration]** : the Earth "imbalanced and needs to come back into balance," with conditions that "will unfortunately intensify, as so many blindly ignore the warnings," and the solution placed inside people rather than in their works: "It is not more inventions, more ideas, more efforts to build a great structure that magnifies the conditions of mankind," but each soul being honest about what is within and seeking God.
- NOTE (witness, `prayer-changes-world-conditions`): intercession asked for the whole suffering world, "You must pray for these lost souls. You must pray for the leaders of your world. You must pray for those who are suffering, confused and in darkness. For without the upliftment that must come for all the children of this world, there is no hope."

### 2018-08-29-af-seretta-kem | What Brings Divine Love Mediumship | 2018-08-29
- `divine-love-mediumship` **[Elaboration]** : The pivotal Elaboration on why Divine Love changes reception. The basic laws of communication are the same on the natural love and Divine Love paths, but Divine Love in the soul transforms the quality, clarity, and depth of what comes through: as the soul awakens, God's Love reshapes the mind's gifts and faculties drop by drop, the mind acquiesces, and rapport with the Celestials deepens until clear communication (in some, trance mediumship) emerges. Adds the universality claim the thread relies on: "each mortal in your world has some gift that will open and bring clear communication with the Celestials, provided their souls are enlivened with the Divine Love." Distinguishes the mechanism from the mind's role (the mind is a factor, not the predominant one; it is the Divine Love and the soul).
- NOTE (sighting, `mind-vs-soul`): the integration of mind and soul as the gift matures.

### 2018-09-01-af-augustine | The Awakening of Your Souls Brings the Message of Truth to Humanity | 2018-09-01
- `bell-toll-instrumentality` **[Capstone]** : Augustine states the thread whole, in its own image: "As you walk in the world a bell tolls with your presence," and the signal does not depend on doing anything, "Even when you are sitting in your home doing idle things, you still send out this signal of light into the world. It is with you. It is always with you for it is truly a part of you." It grows with prayer, "So, the light grows stronger. The tone and strength of this bell sweeter and reverberates out into the world, calling your brothers and sisters to prayer, stimulating that part of their souls that longs for God," and its pull is named as God's, "This magnetism is the Touch of God upon you."
- NOTE (witness, `opposition-to-the-light-worker`): the same light draws opposition as well as interest, "Some are intrigued, while others respond with suspicion, some with anger because you challenge the world, beloveds."
- NOTE (sighting, `currency-of-love`): "that harmony may come to this world, peace may come to this world, love may be the currency of this world."

### 2018-09-01-af-seretta-kem | You Are Ready to Step Forward | 2018-09-01
- `preparation-to-be-a-rock` **[Elaboration]** : Marks the readiness turn: years of preparation are near fruition ("for many of you, we have spent many years preparing you... a time to come that is now very close"), and the readying is completed through mind-soul alignment - "strong with your feet firmly planted upon the ground and your soul connected with Heaven." Adds that the work begins small and humble and grows through persistence, and that each finds a unique role. Moves the argument from "change is coming, prepare" to "you are now ready."
- NOTE (sighting, `soul-purpose-emergence`): each finding a unique role.

### 2018-09-29-af-goldie | Celebrating Vicky's Passing Into the World of Spirit | 2018-09-29
- NOTE (witness, `death-transition`): the doctrine applied at a memorial, a passing called a birth, "celebrating the birth of Vicky into the world of spirit," with grief turned, "If only more knew how beautiful and wonderful is God's creation, all that worry and concern would melt into joy and laughter." Lived rather than developed.

### 2018-10-09-af-seretta-kem | The Integration of the Material Mind with the Soul Mind | 2018-10-09
- `mind-vs-soul` **[Chrysalis]** : Seretta Kem describes the two minds becoming one, and the thread's whole conflict dissolving in the process. The transformation begins in the soul but does not stay there: "touching every cell in your body, every system of your spirit body, every part of your physical mind and the mind of your consciousness is infused with the consciousness of the soul." The integration is then given as a sequence rather than an event. First a passage opens, "The connections are forged so that there is a clear passage of consciousness between the two and this is the first step." Then it is worn deeper by use, "wearing the passage more permanently within your consciousness." Then the two meet as equals, each supplying what the other lacks: the conscious mind gives "the strength that you carry in a conscious way, the love that you carry, the wisdom and knowledge that you carry," and "in return the soul gives deep and varied perceptions, profound wisdom, joy, and love." He is precise about what the awakening soul is like at this stage, and it is not what the thread's rhetoric would predict: "it is like a birthing, this awakening of the soul, this consciousness so tender and vulnerable, soft, and in need of the strength" the mind carries. Then the end state: "these parts of you begin to merge and blend until there comes a time, given enough of this gift of Divine Love within your soul, that all will merge into oneness. There will be no sense of separation. There will only be the singular consciousness of the soul mind merging with the material mind." And the vista beyond it, so that oneness is not mistaken for an ending: "even this is not the end but merely the beginning. Soul progression, soul consciousness does not end. It expands and grows with inflow of this Love, the eternal awakening towards at-onement with God." -- the Chrysalis anchor, over 2018-06-18-af-matthew. Matthew turns the conflict into an expedition and promises the new perception will last; Seretta Kem describes the conflict ceasing to exist, which is the further turn and the one the thread has been building toward. Matthew stands beneath it in the same section.
- `mind-vs-soul` **[Elaboration]** : The reversal of the thread's usual picture, worth logging separately because it is easy to lose. Elsewhere the mind is the obstacle and the soul the authority. Here the newly awakened soul is the weaker party, tender and vulnerable, and the mind's accumulated strength, love, wisdom and knowledge are what it needs in order to express itself at all. The mind is still described as resisting, "The resistance of your mind is beginning to be assuaged by the influence of love," but it is a partner rather than an adversary. -- fence at build against the messages that set mind against soul; the thread should carry both pictures rather than choosing.
- `soul-faculties` **[Elaboration]** : The faculties come online as part of the same integration, "enlivening all the chambers and facets of the soul, bringing the faculties of the soul into alignment with your consciousness as it awakens and becomes active," with the result stated as capacity rather than knowledge, "Your capacity to see the world, to love the world, increases exponentially with this gift of Love."
- `soul-expiation` **[Elaboration]** : Names why the cleansing has to happen for the merging to proceed, which the thread's other members do not connect: honest recognition comes first, "to come to recognize in an honest way, those things within your mind that are not in harmony with your soul. In this deeper recognition, the peeling away of these layers of error, of thought, of fear, of confusion, being released, being healed, so that the soul is more readily able to merge with the mind." So expiation is not only purification but the removal of what stands between the two minds.
- NOTE (witness, `incremental-journey`): the beginning stated as almost imperceptible, "In the beginning of this journey, the effects are very subtle indeed," and the process denied any shortcut, "This process does not happen in an instant."
- NOTE (witness, `divine-love-healing`): the reach claim in the same terms as this spirit's own Foundation (2015-03-29), body, spirit body, and mind all touched, "It brings changes that are permanent."
- NOTE (sighting, `prayer-soul-longing`): the thirst that grows by being met, "the soul stirs and awakens. Emanating from the soul is a deep desire for more of this sustenance, these living waters, to refresh the parched soul."

### 2018-10-16-af-seretta-kem | A Kaleidoscope of Light | 2018-10-16
- `soul-faculties` **[Chrysalis]** : the soul's gifts as seeds in dry chambers, "A soul that is unawakened by God's Divine Love has many chambers within it that are dry and inactive... Within each of these chambers are seeds, God's gifts to your soul," germinating as the Love's waters reach them, each soul unique, "Think of how every snowflake is different," a diamond whose facets are its own. The vista is the kaleidoscope, a soul "ever evolving" whose "progression... is infinite," so that no description of it can hold from one moment to the next. Opens the view of what the awakened faculties become.
- NOTE (witness, `incremental-journey`): "This does not happen all at once, the chambers are in-filled gradually and awakened in this way."

### 2018-10-24-af-seretta-kem | Seretta Kem Talks of the Service that May Come | 2018-10-24
- `preparation-to-be-a-rock` **[Elaboration]** : Widens the readying to its collective architecture: the prepared souls are "one component of a vast network that is being built" and coordinated by God and angels, becoming known in surprising places as materialism loses its hold and humanity's hunger for truth intensifies. Adds that the preparation cannot be delivered as fixed dates or specifics - "the future is not written in stone" - so readiness is developed day by day through the soul faculties. Anticipates the objection Augustine 2019-01-28 answers head-on.

### 2018-11-05-af-john-wesley | Speak Up for Truth and Change | 2018-11-05
- `religious-error` **[Testimony]** : the founder of Methodism on the limits of his own ministry: "I was unable to clearly understand the truths that were attempted to be given to me by the angels in heaven. My mind would not accept that which was not in orthodoxy presented within the Bible," so the best he could do was "reorganize and rearrange the information within that noble book." From the Celestial Kingdom he judges his church and others in need of reform, "my church and I would venture to guess all churches in your world, are in great need of reformation," with the cause named, "truth has been perverted by this ambition to have control, wealth, power." Stands with the other reformers testifying against their own traditions.

### 2018-11-21-af-james | On the Value of Building Portals of Light | 2018-11-21
- `portals` **[Elaboration]** : the mechanics and the human share of the work: portals "act in some ways like a machine that functions to bring and focus energy," enabling spirit "to make connection and rapport more readily to the material world"; the bridge built from both ends, "You are one side and we are the other and we work together to weave this connection"; the anchoring account, sustained presence in a place dedicated to prayer is what lets the conduit be created ("when you establish yourself in a certain place for some time dedicated to prayer, then you facilitate... the possibility of working with us"); and the purpose, connection to "the great lattice of light" and stability "given the unstable conditions of your world." Closest member the Foundation below, 2019-01-26-af-seretta-kem; the collaborative language ("help us build") resolves against the Foundation's "You cannot build" as a division of labour, humans anchoring what spirits formulate. Alternative shape recorded in the roster: this as Foundation, a dedicated full teaching, with the message below as Capstone.
- NOTE (pattern, described and not penned): the lattice of Light named as the structure portals connect to and feed; the lattice material remains its own layer, recorded so the join is findable.

### 2018-11-22-af-yogananda | Yogananda Describes His Journey Through the Spheres Towards the Celestial Heavens | 2018-11-22
- `reincarnation-progression` **[Testimony]** : First-person account of progressing 4th sphere -> Celestial Kingdom after releasing Eastern beliefs; demonstrates the progression Aman describes, from the inside.
- `two-paths` **[Testimony]** : Lived witness that the natural love spheres are real but not the summit; only Divine Love crosses into the Celestial.

### 2018-11-26-af-jesus | Clarification on the Existence of Jesus | 2018-11-26
- `who-jesus-was` **[Foundation]** : a record-correction statement beside the complete-soul Foundation, 2019-01-27-af-jesus. He affirms his own existence and place, "I am a celestial spirit who has lived a life on earth and come to a place that is close to God," names himself the first to enter the Celestial Kingdom, "the celestial kingdom, of which I was the first to enter and reside within the highest realms," and ends the expectation of a return in the flesh, "I will never return to the flesh beloveds though this myth has persisted for many, many years. It cannot be and contravenes the laws of creation." Both stand in the Foundation section; the anchor is chosen at build.
- `divine-love-mediumship` **[Objection-removed]** : clears the belief that messages in Jesus' name are from Jesus: "Many have tried to impersonate me in your world. Some do so overtly, but many do so because they do not understand the power of the influence of spirit upon them," mediums "influenced by spirits who are eager for acknowledgement and a platform from which to speak," the rapport sometimes "so close that they truly believe that they are that individual." Only the awakened soul can tell the difference, "When you have the eyes of your soul awakened and opened you will see." Read with 2019-01-30-af-jesus, where Jesus grounds the discernment teaching.
- NOTE (witness, `religious-error`): how error enters through the well-meaning, "There are many in your world and there have been many in your world, that have brought distorted truth through and yet they strive to bring light and harmony and truth to the world," which has "brought forth many errors within the Bible, many errors in doctrines of religion... through the best of intentions."

### 2018-11-28-af-augustine | All Truth Comes from the Soul | 2018-11-28
- `mind-vs-soul` **[Elaboration]** : how the soul knows, set against how the mind builds: "The mind of the soul absorbs truth. It does not work diligently to construct complex concepts and ideas of what it considers reality. This is how the mind operates." It gives a test for what others say, "take it to your soul. Come to know that familiar feeling and knowing from within that will affirm a truth that the mind may be speculating upon," and carries the mind's power past death: spirits too "have found a truth that they have created of themselves and made a world real and substantial through their own creation." Closes on the two minds collaborating, "so that the material mind and the mind of the soul may collaborate and come to an understanding, a harmony, an integration."
- NOTE (witness, `simplicity-of-the-path`): "Yes, it is a simple path, is it not? It is not adorned with many complex ideas and this is for a reason," the reason being that "until all is predicated upon love, awakened in love, and reflection of love, you cannot venture into these more complex arenas with confidence."
- NOTE (build, `mind-vs-soul`): Part 1 of a two-part teaching with 2018-12-02-af-augustine; read the two together at build.

### 2018-11-29-af-abdullah-latif | The Power of Prayer | 2018-11-29
- `knowing-your-own-soul` **[Elaboration]** : Extends the Foundation in two directions it does not reach. Makes the knowing a prerequisite for action rather than a route to God only: "you cannot know what it is to pray until you know your own heart and know your own soul in relationship with your Creator", with the failure state named, "otherwise, you just cast your intentions to the wind, neither heard by God nor will it bring any satisfaction to that deep part of yourselves." The phrase "in relationship with your Creator" supplies the bridge the Foundation asserts without building: the soul is not known first and God second, because the soul is known as a thing already in relation to God. Extends it outward as well: "to be an agent of change in the world one must change within to carry a deep love and compassion for humanity. In this way, you will understand the Will of God."
- `prayer-changes-world-conditions` **[Elaboration]** : Puts a condition on the thread's central claim: intercession works only in proportion to what is behind it. "If you pray in anger and fear, concern and indignation your prayers only go as high as your breath." Also gives the thread its account of how the effect travels and why it is not observed: "It adds a stream of possibility into the mix and currents of the human condition. The stream may be nurtured by God as you continue in your prayers", and "one cannot expect an outcome that is obvious to those who pray... most often wheels are set in motion that may not come to their endpoint quickly, even within a lifetime."
- NOTE (witness, `free-will-and-gods-plan`): the limit on intercession stated from the law rather than from God's reluctance, "God cannot contravene the gift of free will and thus you cannot expect God to orchestrate every part of your life... This contravenes the laws that God has set into motion from the very beginning of time."

### 2018-12-02-af-augustine | All Truth Comes from the Soul: Part 2 | 2018-12-02
- `mind-vs-soul` **[Elaboration]** : reality as the mind builds it: "Brick by brick, reality is formed. Yet this reality is more reliant upon the perceptions of the material mind than the absolute truth," so "humanity lives in a fog," and a reality made this way "is not stable nor is not absolute. It can be changed," open to manipulation. With practical counsel: "we discourage you somewhat to feed your mind, your material mind extensively. This does not help you to engage your soul."
- `soul-faculties` **[Elaboration]** : the new faculties are "so tender, sensitive, and new. You must nurture these parts, protect them, love them," and their blending with the mind "will bring authority to your perceptions and what you speak of and share with others, and this will intrigue many."
- NOTE (build, `mind-vs-soul`): Part 2 of a two-part teaching with 2018-11-28-af-augustine ("All Truth Comes from the Soul"); read the two together at build. Read in full, Part 2 runs as one argument and serves two threads on different passages.

### 2018-12-17-af-mary | Many Believe That Their Mind Brings Soul Consciousness | 2018-12-17
- `mind-vs-soul` **[Objection-removed]** : clears the belief that the mind's inner awareness already is the soul's knowing: "the vast majority of souls in this world are enmeshed within the condition of the mind and believe this is the great reality of soul. It is not, my beloveds. It is a reality, one that is created by the mind but it is not the reality of the soul." The belief blocks the thread's integration argument, since a seeker who takes the mind's awareness for the soul's has no reason to look further.
- NOTE (witness, `soul-faculties`): "The soul has its own faculties and abilities to perceive and reason and know truth."

### 2019-01-21-af-augustine | Divine Love Path and Natural Love Path | 2019-01-21
- `two-paths` **[Elaboration]** : Free will governs the fork, God blesses both roads, and what the natural road forgoes is a faculty rather than a blessing: "not everyone will ascribe to this journey, for God gives everyone free will... they may choose another road, the road of the natural man. In this, God will indeed bless them and guide them but the depth and acuity of the knowings of the soul will not be part of this reality." New facet: the contrast set at the scale of humanity's need rather than the soul's destination, "This is the next step for humanity... In order to curtail all the disruptions and in-harmonies and pain that man has created."
- `soul-faculties` **[Elaboration]** : States the exclusivity the Josephus Foundation (2019-02-01) does not carry, the faculties gated to one road: "a place of deep joy and recognition that can only come through the gifts and faculties of your soul," against the natural road's missing "depth and acuity of the knowings of the soul." Names the faculty as wisdom, not intellect: "This awakening of deeper wisdom and potentials must come to the fore." Shares its passage with the two-paths line above; demote to witness at build if double-counting is unwanted.
- NOTE (witness, `mind-vs-soul`): "the opportunity to emerge from the realities of the mind to the truth of the soul," the succession asserted as humanity's next step; same claim as 2016-08-31-af-martin-luther.
- NOTE (witness, `bell-toll-instrumentality`): "Through you God will stir and rouse the sleeping souls"; the Foundation's mechanism restated.
- NOTE (witness, `sensitive-souls-redemptive`): "do you not feel saddened that your brothers and sisters cannot experience this Holy Blessing"; the sorrow facet, pastoral.
- NOTE (sighting, `prayer-soul-longing`): the gift attributed to soul yearning over mental grasp, "it is the strength and urgency within your souls that brings you to this gift."
- NOTE (sighting, `preparation-to-be-a-rock`): "there is a great work ahead... but first you must be awakened"; readying named without content.
- NOTE (sighting, `unlimited-reception`): "you will never receive all that can be, for God's blessings are infinite." Read with 2016-05-02-af-andrew and 2016-09-01-af-seretta-kem.

### 2019-01-26-af-andrew | All Have Some Ability to Communicate with Angels | 2019-01-26
- `divine-love-mediumship` **[Elaboration]** : Widens the thread from designated mediums to everyone. Through the Laws of Attraction the angels are drawn close by the soul's desire for God, so "each one of you possesses many gifts and many abilities which we may use to establish a deep rapport with you." What inhibits awareness is not lack of gift but the dense reality the material mind and humanity have conferred on the world; as the soul awakens in Divine Love the perception clears "like a flower whose petals unfold." Elaborates the universality strand (every soul has some avenue) that Seretta Kem stated 2018-08-29, framed now through the mind's constructed reality as the obstacle.
- NOTE (sighting, `mind-vs-soul`): the duality of reality between soul and mind.

### 2019-01-26-af-seretta-kem | Establishing Portals | 2019-01-26
- `portals` **[Foundation]** : the doctrine complete. Definition: "Portals of Light are a mechanism for communication and bring the flow of blessings from God in a concentrated form." The conferral law: "You cannot build a Portal of Light, beloved souls. You have not the capacity or the ability to do so, but you may pray for this blessing and this blessing is conferred upon those who have made a commitment"; "we do not act unless this blessing is conferred by God's Will." Construction and composition: a team of spirits formulates the gift, which "is made of energy and it has substance." Maintenance: "The energy within a portal is sustained with prayer and desire." Weight: "These portals are not conferred lightly." Ignition: "the beginnings of each of these portals were ignited by the Master Jesus as a gift to you all." Proliferation: "It is God's will that this particular and unique blessing may proliferate in your world helping to elevate the conditions." And the temporary clause that retroactively explains the 2016-07-25 sighting: "At times these portals may be temporarily established... not as strong or wide in scope or ability, but indeed they do help to sustain the light within a Circle of Light." Self-described as a summary of prior teachings ("I have spoken on several occasions... I will summarize now"), the ground of the alternative Capstone shape recorded in the roster.
- NOTE (sighting, `divine-love-mediumship`): the opening section teaches the Law of Communication and Rapport as the circle's own responsibility, which bears on the conditions of clear reception; no role proposed.
- NOTE (sighting, `conduit-not-scripture`): the same teaching on the Law of Communication and Rapport bears on rapport as the sacred element; no role proposed.

### 2019-01-27-af-jesus | Jesus Incarnated as a Complete Soul and Has No Other Half or Soulmate | 2019-01-27
- `who-jesus-was` **[Foundation]** : Jesus states he incarnated as a complete, singular soul by prior agreement with God, with no soulmate. Sets the premise the thread depends on.

### 2019-01-28-af-augustine | Questions Regarding Earth Changes | 2019-01-28
- `preparation-to-be-a-rock` **[Objection-removed]** : Answers the demand the readying keeps provoking: give us specific predictions. Augustine removes it honestly - the world's conditions are erratic, the veil distorts what is conveyed, and spirit experiences time differently, so specific prophecy cannot be relied upon ("we cannot steer the ship for you... you have within you a compass"). The preparation is therefore inner, not informational: trust the compass, make plans when guidance is clear and no apprehension warns, and be assured God's plan for the salvation of mankind will not fail. Clears the false expectation that being a rock depends on knowing the future, and affirms the rock role directly - "you will be the rock, beloved souls, that others may lean upon."
- NOTE (sighting, `earth-changes`): also an `earth-changes` adjacency (the erratic world conditions and the limits of prophecy about them); the spine here remains the preparation thread.

### 2019-01-28-af-matthew-2 | Releasing the Last Dark Vestiges in the Soul | 2019-01-28
- `incremental-journey` **[Elaboration]** : develops the why of the increments from the human side: the most tenacious soul conditions "stick to you with such tenacity" and require equal force of yearning to break; the seeker therefore chooses between the purposeful, intense inner gaze into the dark chambers now, or the drop-by-drop accumulation of Divine Love over many years. "This healing will take place whether it be soon or at some future date... it is your choice." The two-roads teaching gives the thread its choice mechanism.
- NOTE (witness, `pentecost`): refers back to the Pentecost teaching, 2017-05-11-af-jesus, and the conditions Jesus described for the dramatic inflowing ("conditions that none in the room or very few in this world are experiencing at this time"), framing the sudden-versus-gradual question as a matter of yearning intensity. States the registry spine's step that the depth of yearning decides which mode arrives, without developing it.

### 2019-01-29-af-augustine | Does Every Soul Have a Purpose? | 2019-01-29
- `soul-purpose-emergence` **[Foundation]** : Augustine gives the thread its doctrinal root: every soul is entrusted by God with a unique purpose and the gifts to fulfil it, offered as an invitation that free will may or may not heed, so many never fulfil it. Crucially, the purpose is not grasped by the mind or revealed all at once - "you continue upon your path of life and are drawn and choose certain expressions... which essentially is a reflection of your gifts," the soul "naturally gravitating to that place when it is ready," drawn by "the gravity of delight within your soul." Anchors the emergence argument; pairs with the free-will premise of `free-will-and-gods-plan` without being reducible to it (this thread is about how the unique purpose surfaces, not whether free will and God's plan conflict).
- `free-will-and-gods-plan` **[Elaboration]** : Restates the free-will premise in the register of personal calling: the invitation is "deep and well-embedded" but "this does not mean that the soul will listen," and God "has seeded many souls with this invitation to fulfil their purpose" within the plan for the salvation of mankind. Confirms the thread rather than founding it.

### 2019-01-29-af-mary-magdalene | Mary Magdalene Is Not Jesus' Soulmate | 2019-01-29
- `who-jesus-was` **[Objection-removed]** : Mary Magdalene directly confirms she was never his soulmate, wife, or lover, removing the most common popular counter-claim. Follows the Jesus message chronologically and logically.

### 2019-01-30-af-jesus | How Can We Discern Truth When Messages from Different Mediums Contradict Each Other? | 2019-01-30
- `divine-love-mediumship` **[Testimony]** : First-person witness and authority grounding the whole thread. Jesus confirms the governing principle experientially: no medium is one hundred percent accurate; the harmonisation of mind and soul and the soul's development in Divine Love bear directly on the quality of a celestial message; excessive mental expectation draws not Celestials but eager higher spirits mistaken for them. Supplies key testimony on the instrument in question, "this instrument has a higher soul development than James Padgett had", and on how the Celestials designate a channel only once a certain soul progression is attained, not by the mind's ambition. Also carries the discernment teaching: test the truth that comes, take responsibility through the spirit of truth within the soul rather than putting the onus on the messenger. Grounds the doctrine the Seretta Kem and Andrew Elaborations describe in Jesus' own endorsement and correction.
- NOTE (sighting, `religious-error`): the James Padgett record and the correction of mediumship expectations; the message's spine is mediumship and discernment.

### 2019-01-30-af-seretta-kem | Jesus Assigns Spirit Guides | 2019-01-30
- `divine-love-mediumship` **[Objection-removed]** : clears the belief that Jesus can be summoned on demand or claimed as any one person's guide: "There are those in the world who believe that Jesus will come and speak through them at their beck and call... They do not realize that he is at God's beck and call," and "Jesus does not guide any individual at this moment, merely Jesus comes to many individuals in response to prayer." Guides are assigned, "Jesus assigns these guides in his wisdom," and the condition of genuine communication is humility rather than expectation, "It is best that you humbly ask to be used as an instrument, keeping your thoughts and inclinations out of this picture." (Also cited in the 2019-02-18-af-augustine NOTE.)
- NOTE (witness, `soul-purpose-emergence`): the purpose as a commitment made before birth that the soul still knows, "You ask whether your commitment in this regard was made before you came to Earth as a soul willing to serve God... I say, in many cases, this is true and that your soul knows," so "you must go deep, beloved souls, and seek to know your purpose."
- NOTE (sighting, `mind-vs-soul`): the end state seen from the Celestial side, "the material mind, does not exist in our realm, within our beings. It has been completely absorbed and transformed by the power of God's Love so that we possess a mind, but the mind of the soul."

### 2019-01-31-af-augustine | How Can a Person Receive Divine Love Without Knowing About It? | 2019-01-31
- `divine-love-without-knowing` **[Foundation]** : Establishes the core claim: the mind need not understand the mechanics for the soul to long for God and receive the gift; souls of all beliefs already receive it.
- `mind-vs-soul` **[Elaboration]** : The mind can restrict and constrict the inflowing of Love; the soul receives on its own accord.

### 2019-01-31-af-yogananda | Reincarnation and the Perception of It Proliferated by Many Yogis and Gurus of the East | 2019-01-31
- `reincarnation-progression` **[Objection-removed]** : Explains WHY people believe in reincarnation (Law of Attraction drawing like-believing spirits into close rapport), clearing the experiential evidence that seems to support it. Pairs with Aman's foundation.
- `mind-vs-soul` **[Elaboration]** : Reinforces that the soul knows truth where the mind perpetuates error.
- `religious-error` **[Testimony]** : Yogananda on his own tradition's perpetuated error: taught reincarnation from a young age as part of his culture, believed and reinforced it through his spiritual pursuits, and corrects it now from spirit. Extends the thread's reformer arc beyond Christianity for the first time.

### 2019-02-01-af-confucius | Come Together Again With Your Questions | 2019-02-01
- NOTE (witness, `portals`): closes with "This portal opens wide. Many gifts are coming." The word is used here in the full sense that 2019-01-26-af-seretta-kem teaches, unlike the loose use at 2016-03-25-af-augustine. -- Otherwise a light message: mostly personal reassurance to the circle host and a call to gather again. Roster usage witness for `portals`.

### 2019-02-01-af-josephus | Faculties of the Soul | 2019-02-01
- `soul-faculties` **[Foundation]** : Josephus catalogues the soul's faculties (awareness of God, knowing Truth, perceiving spirits, sensing God's Will, healing, creativity, original thought, moral compass, higher love) and teaches that Divine Love opens the soul's chambers and refines each. Anchors the new `soul-faculties` thread.
- `mind-vs-soul` **[Elaboration]** : Frames the dual consciousness (material mind vs. soul mind) and insists the soul's faculties must be experienced, not merely understood by the mind. Deepens the mind-vs-soul argument rather than founding or capping it.

### 2019-02-03-af-andrew | The Door of Truth Is Opened With God's Love | 2019-02-03
- `soul-purpose-emergence` **[Elaboration]** : Companion to the Augustine Foundation (2019-01-29), restated days later: true self and true purpose come not as a sudden revelation "but comes with the slow awakening and expression of your soul within your lives until you find yourself walking upon a path that is truly in harmony with God's Will." Adds the framing of gifts "yearning to be awakened" for service and counsels "humility and patience" while the purpose surfaces. Confirms the emergence argument rather than advancing it.

### 2019-02-04-af-jesus | The Light Must Overcome the Darkness | 2019-02-04
- `bell-toll-instrumentality` **[Elaboration]** : The subtle-spreading mechanism in macro form: "God establishes beachheads of Light in a darkened world," and as the seeker empowers them, "these nodes of Light expand and grow. It is subtle... and it touches many, many more than you are aware of," for "God uses you more than you know." Same radiating instrumentality as the bell toll, scaled up to the collective contest against darkness. Strong witness; pairs with the John 2016-03-12 ripples and Augustine 2016-03-25 subtle-instrumentality entries.
- `mind-vs-soul` **[Elaboration]** : States the hierarchy directly as a truth to be taught: "You must teach that the Truth of the soul is greater than the truth of the mind, that the power of Love is greater than the power of the will of humanity," and frames transformation as the soul finding "predominance over your minds." Adds the teaching-imperative dimension - the mind/soul distinction as something the seeker must actively convey to others.
- NOTE (sighting, `two-currents-cosmology`): opens with a distinct macro framing - "two powerful currents in this world, one that flows towards darkness and one that flows towards light," with darkness presently the stronger, fuelled by greed and the misplaced search for safety in material things, and the seekers carrying "a powerful antidote to the darkness." -- Related to but distinct from `earth-conditions-and-prayer` (which is the darkness-field buffeting the individual soul); this is the macro contest of currents and the collective movement that overcomes it. Related statements: 2018-05-28-af-jesus, "This great association of souls of Light and Truth that is coming together and coalescing into a beautiful movement that shall indeed push back the darkness," and 2018-10-24-af-seretta-kem, "those nodes of deep darkness that exist in the world will be surrounded and neutralized." The candidate is minted when a message develops the framing.

### 2019-02-05-af-keea-atta-kem | Feeling the Love | 2019-02-05
- `divine-love-without-knowing` **[Elaboration]** : Adds the felt-experience dimension to the thread the Augustine Foundation (2019-01-31) frames in terms of understanding: one may receive the gift without consciously *feeling* it, not only without comprehending it. "For those who do not feel the inflowing of the Divine Love within their souls, this does not mean that they are not receiving this Love, for indeed they are." Names the mechanism - the soul's sincere yearning brings the Holy Spirit's response, "the law in action, the Law of Love" - and promises that those who cannot yet feel it will in time be given "some powerful experience, some form of awakening" that confirms the Truth. Distinct contribution: shifts the thread from "received without knowing" to "received without feeling," and supplies the reassurance pastorally.
- `mind-vs-soul` **[Elaboration]** : Years of living through "the faculties of your material mind" dull sensitivity to a gift that is known "in a way of knowing that goes beyond the mind"; the soul receives regardless of the mind's recognition. Companion to the Augustine 2019-01-31 mind-soul note.

### 2019-02-06-af-francis-of-assisi | Your Prayers May Change the Conditions of the World | 2019-02-06
- NOTE (witness, `prayer-changes-world-conditions`): central claim is intercessory - "Prayer is powerful... Prayer can change conditions. Prayer can change the behavior of those who violate and are in ignorance and error," so that "the minds of those in darkness may be changed" and "blessings will pour upon this world." Distinct from `prayer-soul-longing` (the nature of true prayer) and from `earth-conditions-and-prayer` (prayer defending the individual soul against the world's darkness-field): this is prayer reaching outward to change the world and other people. -- a strong statement of the argument that mostly asserts rather than develops it, which is why it sits at witness.

### 2019-02-10-af-augustine | Choosing Light Over Darkness | 2019-02-10
- `death-transition` **[Elaboration]** : Adds the condition-determines-your-starting-plane mechanism the Matthew Foundation (2018-07-19) leaves implicit: "By the Laws of Creation, you are destined to enter some place in this world of spirit that is determined by the condition of your soul," and that plane "will reinforce your own condition, your own level of light." Death is still seamless transition, but Augustine specifies that what you carry across determines where you begin and what you face. Pairs with Matthew's foundation.
- `earth-conditions-and-prayer` **[Elaboration]** : Light touch: "all of humanity carries a certain amount of darkness within them because the dark conditions of this Earth impinge upon the soul," and prayer for Divine Love "heals the damage of your life" and the places "that are not of light." Same conditions-impinge / heal-with-Love mechanism, though the message's spine is the afterlife-condition teaching rather than the daily-prayer-as-defense argument.
- NOTE (sighting, `short-life-long-road`): where the soul begins in spirit is set by its condition at death, "you are destined to enter some place in this world of spirit that is determined by the condition of your soul," and that place "will reinforce your own condition, your own level of light," which is why "It is easier to grow in this world." Read with 2019-01-31-af-yogananda, who says progression in spirit "can be swift indeed once there is a desire and the knowledge to do so": the two agree, since the road in spirit is swift for the soul that knows the way and long for the soul that arrives without it.

### 2019-02-12-af-osho-bhagwan | God's Love Is the True Way to Liberation | 2019-02-12
- `two-paths` **[Testimony]** : A second testimony to complement Yogananda's, from the opposite vantage. Where Yogananda testifies from the Celestial Kingdom looking back (2018-11-22), Osho testifies from inside the climb: a guru who taught the natural road - liberation of the passions, awakening of human potential - who has "not entered into the Celestial Spheres" but whose "soul yearns for the gift of Love that comes from the Creator." He names the natural path's real but partial reach ("liberation of these things is only one part of that journey"; "my mission, though partially successful, was never complete") and confirms the higher road from below: "I now recognize the power of love that is the true passion of the soul." Strengthens the thread by witnessing the natural-love path as genuinely traversed yet incomplete, in real time, rather than from the summit. Distinct value: shows a soul still on the natural road actively turning toward Divine Love.
- NOTE (sighting, `divine-love-mediumship`): the instrument's "mind was not so happy" to channel this spirit "but his soul allowed this," a datapoint on the instrument's own mind-soul dynamic, echoing the rapport material at 2016-03-27-af-seretta-kem.

### 2019-02-15-af-matthew | The Experience of Pentecost | 2019-02-15
- `pentecost` **[Testimony]** : Eyewitness account supplying the experiential side of the thread, whose Foundation, 2017-05-11-af-jesus, frames it as teaching. Asked to describe the Pentecost, Matthew, one of those present, says "it came suddenly... in great light and heat and we thought that the building in which we resided was afire with this great gift and energy, although it was a fire that did not burn. It was a fire that healed and changed us." Then draws the teaching point: seekers "yearn for this instantaneous healing and awakening, instead of the incremental and sometimes difficult journey of cleansing" they have chosen. Pairs with 2017-05-11-af-jesus; together they give the two modes of awakening, sudden vs gradual, with both witness and doctrine.
- `soul-expiation` **[Elaboration]** : Frames the seeker's chosen road as the incremental cleansing the expiation thread describes: "you continue to slough off these burdens," climbing "at a steady pace upward," and notes the burdens shed now are ones that would otherwise have been carried into spirit ("Into spirit, you would have gone, carrying these terrible loads of pain"). Adds the contrast that the gradual cleansing road is itself a mercy, sparing the soul a heavier load later.
- NOTE (sighting, `prayer-soul-longing`): "listen to the beating of your souls... this is the soul yearning for God" restates prayer as soul-longing as counsel rather than developed argument.

### 2019-02-16-mc-seretta-kem | Process of Expiation | 2019-02-16
- `soul-expiation` **[Testimony]** : Witnesses a single expiation from the inside, start to finish, through one soul's recent experience (related with her consent, third-person and unnamed): the inflowing Love brings warmth and joy, then loosens a condition ready to heal, surfacing buried emotion and pain; God and the angels feel most absent at exactly the moment they are closest ("it is the time when we observe you and we are very close... yet it may be a difficult sensation to feel us"); the soul withdraws to isolate; the angels call her into prayer with two others, and "using the power of the three souls together yearning for God" the release comes, felt "as though an ice block was being extracted from her solar plexus," followed by a flood of tears and "an overwhelming sense of peace," leaving a soul-chamber that "now holds love, joy and peace... for eternity." Confirms the thread's whole arc experientially and adds the angels-closest-when-felt-absent paradox and the power of communal prayer to precipitate release. Delivered through Maureen Cardoso, it corroborates through a second medium the same teaching Seretta Kem gives through Al Fike at 2018-07-14-af-seretta-kem.
- NOTE (context): the published text is bracketed by the medium's own notes stressing that the tangible "ice block" experience is an example, not a standard; expiation is unique to each soul and moved through intuitively. Worth preserving when `soul-expiation` is built, so the Testimony is not read as a prescriptive template.

### 2019-02-18-af-augustine | Be Receptive to God and the Celestial Angels | 2019-02-18
- `mind-vs-soul` **[Elaboration]** : Applies the distinction to perception of the spirit world: to know the Celestial angels assigned to each earnest soul, one must "put aside your preconceptions, your mental expectations and barriers, and allow our presence to be felt and known by the senses that are not of the mind and the physical but of the soul and the heart." Insights then come "not from the mental faculties but from the faculties of the soul." Adds the soul-as-organ-of-perception facet specifically to sensing spiritual presence; the same soul-over-mind perception the thread develops elsewhere (cf. Andrew 2018-03-02, Seretta Kem 2019-01-30), here turned toward receptivity to the angels rather than toward judgment or knowing truth.
- NOTE (sighting, `angelic-accompaniment`): the Celestial angels assigned to each earnest soul, with the uniqueness of each soul's path, "you cannot say to another, 'this is how it is'." The accompaniment is standing rather than sent on a request, but conditioned on earnestness, which places it between the default form the candidate is built on and the request-and-dispatch form at 2016-09-10-af-andrew. Echoes 2019-01-30-af-seretta-kem on Jesus assigning guides.

### 2019-02-21-af-elijah | It Is Your Destiny to Bring Your Gifts Forward for Mankind | 2019-02-21
- `soul-purpose-emergence` **[Elaboration]** : Elijah adds the designation-and-unfolding facet: the gifted souls before him "have been designated this role as prophet," a purpose already assigned that comes to expression as the soul is developed and its faculties opened ("with your dedication to receive this gift of God's Love, it is opening up many possibilities and faculties of your souls"). The purpose is fulfilled not by grasping it whole but by daily listening and acting on guidance until "you fulfill your destinies as channels of love and truth and light." Confirms and extends the Augustine Foundation (2019-01-29) and the Andrew companion (2019-02-03): purpose surfaces through the soul's awakening rather than arriving as a single revelation. Distinct contribution: frames the purpose as a role already designated by God, opened out through the reception of Divine Love, rather than only gravitated toward.
- NOTE (sighting, `two-currents-cosmology`): Elijah gives a strong statement of the gifted souls as anchors in the coming upheaval - "this will be an anchor in the changes and the storms with your relationship with God. As you hold on to one another as the winds blow, so you will support and save many from the coming changes." Same anchor-in-the-storms role the Keea atta Kem earth-changes teaching develops (2017-09-13, gifted souls as rocks of stability); pairs there rather than in `two-currents-cosmology` proper, which is the macro contest of currents. Logged as a sighting for whichever thread carries the "steady souls others lean on through the coming changes" argument when building; not minting a new thread on this message alone.
- NOTE (context): Elijah names the network-of-light bonding, "the gifted souls are drawn together as the Laws of Attraction and the power of God's Will continue to cement this great network of light... bonded for all eternity," a close echo of the "bonded for eternity" affirmation at 2015-08-17-af-isaiah. A Spiritual Community & Fellowship connection, and the basis of the related_messages link to Isaiah, rather than a thread contribution.

### 2019-02-22-af-augustine | The Power to Change Needs Your Conscious Understanding of the Power of Free Will | 2019-02-22
- `free-will-and-self-responsibility` **[Foundation]** : (presumptive section anchor) A full, developed statement of the thread's argument: a person's own free will is the turning point of their healing. God honours free will absolutely and "will not override" it, even to relieve pain a person has created for themselves. Prayers for relief go unanswered when "within your mind you continue to persist" in feeding the very condition, and real change is "not just something that is a correction that comes from your mind, but a deep desire for change from your soul." Augustine states the Law of Compensation without naming it: "There is a law that says you must be responsible for yourself." Distinct from `free-will-and-gods-plan`, which traces how God carries out His plan across generations through willing souls. Shares ground with `soul-expiation` (change that comes from the soul; compare 2023-02-10-af-augustine, "you need not cleanse everything first") and with `mind-vs-soul` (the mind's correction set against the soul's desire), but neither of those threads makes this argument.

### 2019-02-22-af-keea-atta-kem | Life Challenges Strengthen the Soul, Creating More Powerful Channels of Love | 2019-02-22
- `challenges-as-blessings` **[Elaboration]** : a warm, clear statement of the thread's claim ("every challenge is a blessing bringing you to greater light, to deeper joy") rather than a developed argument for it.
- NOTE (build, `challenges-as-blessings`): Keea Atta Kem states the teaching warmly and clearly, "all conspire to bring many insights and lessons, strengthening your soul... Every struggle, every opportunity, every challenge is a blessing bringing you to greater light, to deeper joy", as encouragement rather than as an argument that develops it. The same principle is stated at 2015-11-02-af-augustine ("lessons to be learned... this is the challenge of life"), 2016-02-29-af-confucius and 2018-08-06-af-solomon; 2016-08-11-af-alec-gaunt is the message that builds it into an argument. The wording is also close to the channel-of-love and planting-seeds language of 2016-01-09-af-augustine (the gardeners), though it adds nothing to that thread.

### 2019-02-24-af-andrew | Each Uses Their Gifts to Help Each Other Heal | 2019-02-24
- `soul-expiation` **[Elaboration]** : Andrew, who gives the Foundation (2015-03-27-af-andrew, the universality of soul injury), adds a facet: expiation as a communal process of complementary perception. The familiar mechanism is present - old wounds walled off behind "a protective wall around those places so tender," "the encrustations of life" loosened by "the power of God's Love" - but the distinctive contribution is that God deliberately brings differing souls together so that "presumptions and insights that are obscured by one, may come clear to another," giving "a clear perception of the soul condition." Rather than the individual's cleansing alone, this one shows the cleansing arranged in community: differing gifts reveal to a soul what it cannot see in itself, and shared prayer readies each to "cross the chasm and be free." Extends the Foundation rather than merely restating it.
- NOTE (sighting, `soul-faculties`): the differing "gifts of perception" among souls; the message's spine is expiation in community.
- NOTE (sighting, `bell-toll-instrumentality`): each soul serving another's awakening; the message's spine is expiation in community.

### 2019-02-27-af-andrew | Mediumship of Al and Ability to Communicate for All | 2019-02-27
- `divine-love-mediumship` **[Elaboration]** : Adds two things to the thread. First, the mechanics: the instrument as "a finely tuned mechanism developed over many years," the radio-frequency image of a clear channel, and the governing principle that reception quality depends on "development of the soul in Divine Love" rather than "the satisfaction of the mind and the ego" (the same principle as 2019-01-30-af-jesus, developed at 2016-12-06-af-seretta-kem and 2018-08-29-af-seretta-kem). Second, the widening: "every soul who desires to be guided is blessed and accompanied by an angel or many angels," each with "many possibilities for your angel friends to connect," so "do not underestimate your own ability to communicate with us... you are never alone" (the same argument as 2019-01-26-af-andrew). Elaborates the Foundation, 2016-03-27-af-seretta-kem, on both fronts: the instrument's preparation and the universality of the gift.
- NOTE (context): "This precious son has mentioned that he has monitored the progress of the messages" refers to a spirit not identified in the text; per never-infer, not added to spirits[] and not named here.

### 2019-03-03-mc-keea-atta-kem | Seek the Counsel of Your Soul | 2019-03-03
- `mind-vs-soul` **[Elaboration]** : Adds a practice. Rather than framing the mind as obstacle or describing the integrated end-state, Keea Atta Kem gives a deliberate method for cultivating mind-soul harmony: "do you spend time communing with your soul? Do you allow your conscious mind to befriend your soul? Do you allow the language of love to be shared between mind and soul?" She casts this as a regular discipline - creating "a loving environment within you, where there is a resonance and a language of love" between the two - and reframes the mind not only as the barrier but as a potential ally: "use your mind in a most loving way and invite your soul to share its wisdom." Also supplies a specific application: physical pain and mental turmoil sit atop "a truth that you carry in your soul, a part of your soul that desires healing," so the courage to hear the soul's counsel is what releases it. Elaborates the thread toward befriending rather than merely subduing the mind.
- NOTE (sighting, `soul-expiation`): the pain-carries-a-truth-to-be-released mechanism, as at 2018-07-14-af-seretta-kem; the message's spine is the mind-soul relationship and its cultivation.

### 2019-03-08-af-andrew | The Highest Gift of All Is the Blessing of God's Love | 2019-03-08
- `earth-conditions-and-prayer` **[Elaboration]** : Adds the resilience facet to the thread whose Foundation is 2016-02-29-af-luke. Andrew opens with the thread's signature image - "the cold winds of earth buffet every soul, making for challenging conditions and life experiences that cause much pain, confusion and difficulties" - and gives the grounded-soul response: those who seek God and know the power of prayer stand firm, "all of these winds that blow and buffet you cannot topple you for you are firmly grounded in light. You have God's Hand upon you." Where Luke supplies the mechanism (prayer neutralizes the darkness-field) and Augustine 2023-02-10 supplies the re-sensitization (come awake to the conditions), this adds the note that the soul anchored in Divine Love is not toppled by the winds at all. Complements rather than merely restates.
- NOTE (sighting, `two-paths`): God guiding every soul to light "in one way or another" set against the higher, irrevocable transformation of Divine Love, stated as the familiar choice rather than developed with a new facet.
- NOTE (sighting, `mind-vs-soul`): the choice to follow the soul's desire or let the material mind keep precedence, stated as the familiar choice rather than developed with a new facet.

### 2019-03-13-af-stephen | There Is a Power to This Gift of Insight | 2019-03-13
- `preparation-to-be-a-rock` **[Elaboration]** : Supplies the mechanism of the readying the thread otherwise only names. Dreams, portents, insights, and visions are God communicating through the soul as advance preparation - "do not dismiss this... there is a kernel of truth" - so "that when that time to act comes, all is not confusing and foreign and misunderstood." Joins this to the strengthening-through-cleansing ("all is in the cleansing and strengthening of your soul... it is a healing of the deep parts within you") and the rock role ("able to be a rock for those who are lost... this stabilizing force"). Adds the how of God's preparation to a thread whose other witnesses establish the that.

### 2019-03-14-af-jesus | Love Is My Message | 2019-03-14
- `religious-error` **[Elaboration]** : Jesus himself extends the thread's "no written word is final, truth comes through the soul" argument, applied directly to the Bible: "there are many things there that I have said and done and there are many things that I have not said nor done," so "when you read that book... bring it to your heart and listen to what your heart has to say." Adds the reform vision from the source it most needs - the churches should "simplify the doctrines, wipe away the error," their edifices become "sanctuaries of light." Where Augustine (testimony), Joseph Smith (institutional ambition), and Moses (Vicarious Atonement, finality of written word) build the case from various witnesses, here the founder of the tradition confirms it: read scripture through the soul's feeling for truth, not as final authority.
- `who-jesus-was` **[Elaboration]** : Adds the "not God" facet to the record-correction thread. "Those who pray within these churches may not pray to me for I am your brother, but I am not God. God is separate from me as He is from you... Yet I am closest to God." Distinct from the Foundation (complete soul, 2019-01-27) and the Mary Magdalene Objection-removed (2019-01-29): this corrects the specific doctrine of Jesus' divinity and redirects prayer to God, while affirming his unique nearness to the Creator.
- NOTE (sighting, `prayer-soul-longing`): "the power of prayer... sincere and longing from your soul", with a channel-of-love adjacency alongside; the message's chain-bearing contributions are to `religious-error` and `who-jesus-was`.

### 2019-03-29-af-mark | Divine Love Brings Your Soul Nourishment Needed to Awaken | 2019-03-29
- NOTE (sighting, `soul-faculties`): Mark gives an invitational overview of the soul as "full of facets and chambers and aspects" that "remain unawakened" until "this gift of Divine Love brings to your soul the nourishment it requires to bring it to life." Restates the `soul-faculties` premise (the chambers awakened by Divine Love) at a high level rather than developing the faculties argument with a new facet the way Josephus 2019-02-01 (the chambers/faculties enumerated) and Seretta Kem 2018-10-16 (seeds germinating, the kaleidoscope) do, so logged as a search-level sighting rather than a member. Also a light `preparation-to-be-a-rock` echo ("those who have a strong faith... will become the leaders of this world when these changes come"), asserted in passing rather than developed.

### 2019-04-01-jw-yogananda | We Are Not So Different | 2019-04-01
- `religious-error` **[Testimony]** : Yogananda's reincarnation correction in full, in the first person, and, notably, its origin story: he taught reincarnation as his guru taught it, then entered spirit and found that among all his loved ones and the ancient teachers, "NONE of them had ever had any experience of reincarnation on earth," and so dropped the belief. Complements his 2019-01-31 correction (through Al Fike) with the personal narrative of how the error fell away. The thread's Eastern voice deepens: confession, repair (the Yogananda book), and now the moment of seeing.
- `two-paths` **[Testimony]** : a compressed sphere-progression witness: praying for God's Love "became my principal practice," and reunion with Sri Yukteswar "helped me progress quickly through the various spheres" toward at-onement. Lived testimony of the Divine Love path from a natural-love-sphere arrival.
- `divine-love-mediumship` **[Elaboration]** : the rapport's origin, from the spirit's side: Yogananda names why he comes to this particular medium (the parallel lives, the shared path) and asks permission to deliver "the broader teachings" through "our conversations in spirit." A Celestial establishing a working rapport on the record, companion to the Al Fike invitation (2019-04-06).
- NOTE (context): a message from medium Rev. Jimbeau Walsh, with rich medium biography: the two religious mothers, priest-versus-guru training, 1960s counterculture and esoteric study, the soulmate "Care," thirty years with Padgett's writings.
- NOTE (sighting, `divine-love-without-knowing`): Yogananda "knew of this Love too but within the confines... of Hinduism."
- NOTE (sighting, `who-jesus-was`): Jesus "the highest soul in God's kingdom."

### 2019-04-03-af-joseph | The Gift of Divine Love Is the Greatest Truth You May Know | 2019-04-03
- `who-jesus-was` **[Testimony]** : Joseph, the earthly father, testifies that during his Earth life he did not understand his son's mission or the power of the Love, and proclaims Jesus as the Messiah "not just for the Jews but for all of humanity," who "opened the doors to this gift." Read beside 2016-03-31-af-judas, a disciple who could not work him out at the time, and 1963-12-26-ds-mary, a mother correcting the record of her own son, the three carry the thread's why-the-record-went-wrong step: the people closest to him did not understand him while he lived.
- NOTE (sighting, `soul-faculties`): Joseph proclaims the supremacy of the Divine Love gift - "no matter how long is your journey to God, this gift is the greatest truth you may know," and in receiving it "all other blessings come, all doors are opened." Pairs this with the dormant-soul-awakened image ("your soul for the most part lies dormant without this gift... this gift to flow and open the many chambers of your souls"), a `soul-faculties` touch at a search level. The two-paths and divine-love-supremacy themes are asserted rather than built, so they stay at sighting. The Jesus-record content is not: it is carried as Testimony above.

### 2019-04-04-af-matthew | Awaken! | 2019-04-04
- `mind-vs-soul` **[Elaboration]** : adds the resistance-and-retreat dynamic: the seeker mistrusts the soul's "newfound freedom," tests the higher perspectives "constantly" against the old earthly conditions, fears the shifts underway, and hurries "to the comfort of your old ways, clinging, reinforcing, embracing what is old and familiar." Names the contest directly - the mind's stubbornness, fear, and anger are "not a part of love but a part of fear" - and the treasures of the soul surface only "if the tyranny of the mind does not overpower the gentleness yet persistence of the soul." Closes on the integration end-state ("the mind and the soul are one and in harmony together"), restating the vista the thread's Chrysalis entries open; the distinct contribution is why integration is slow: the mind's fear-driven retreat.
- `incremental-journey` **[Elaboration]** : the development that carried the thread out of the holding pen: "you take two steps forward and one back. You try something new and then retreat to the old. But each time you make that effort, beloved souls, you reinforce truth." Supplies the mechanism of the oscillation (fear-driven retreat to the familiar) and the claim that each forward effort reinforces truth.
- NOTE (sighting, `soul-faculties`): "treasures, gifts, perceptions that will come to your consciousness", and the opening "faculties of the soul bring depth and truth and perspective", asserted rather than developed.
- NOTE (sighting, `bell-toll-instrumentality`): "how else may change come into this world but with the change of each soul awakened in love", asserted rather than developed.

### 2019-04-05-af-augustine | Mediumship, Gifts, Being a Channel of Love | 2019-04-05
- `divine-love-mediumship` **[Elaboration]** : criterializes the discernment strand into a teachable test, the mark of Truth: "If a message from a medium is not of love and does not include the Divine Love as the source of Truth, then this is not of a Celestial spirit." Adds the kernel doctrine (a Celestial Angel cannot speak untruth; distortion may blur but never empty a genuine message) and locates verification in the soul, which "may always go to the soul, to that source of knowing and receive a signal from that place that affirms its truthfulness and its source." Restates and sharpens the discernment Jesus grounded in Testimony (2019-01-30) and answers the impersonation problem (2018-11-26); also reaffirms this instrument's protection and rapport.
- `incremental-journey` **[Elaboration]** : extends the thread to the apprehension of truth itself, in the thread's own word: "It is a long journey, beloveds, to the Truth. It is incremental. It is something that builds in understanding." God's Truth simple in essence yet long to implement; each day and each prayer clears conditions and deepens understanding a little more; patience with self and others while the foundation of truth is built in the soul.
- `judgment-to-love` **[Elaboration]** : the facet within the community itself: schisms among those living the Divine Love come from differing approaches, not error ("this does not mean that your brothers and sisters are wrong or misdirected"); the stronger in love must be the example; rejection is met with greater love because it "is a cry for love"; release the judgments and stress the commonalities. Applies the thread's argument inside the fellowship rather than toward the world.
- `self-love-foundation` **[Elaboration]** : the mechanism stated as logic: "Come to understand yourself above all, to accept yourself above all... In this way, how can you judge another when you are being of love and acceptance, joy and light?" Self-acceptance rendering judgment of others impossible; arguably a developed statement, though it stands as one movement within a larger message. Possible section anchor; decide at build.
- NOTE (context): the closing turns to the expression of unique soul gifts, a Unique Soul Gifts connection rather than a thread contribution.

### 2019-04-06-af-yogananda | Your Gifts Are Opening | 2019-04-06
- `religious-error` **[Testimony]** : the correction as an ongoing project rather than a retrospective confession: "there are many things missing within this prescribed way to God that I have put forth on paper. I desire to rectify the error... and to bring the Truth of Divine Love in these teachings." Yogananda plans to correct his own published books through a new writing rapport, for the many who follow his path "yet they miss the greatest Truth." With his 2019-01-31 reincarnation correction, the thread's Eastern voice now holds both the confession and the repair.
