- `divine-love-mediumship` **[Elaboration]** : the development-protocol facet: the steps of establishing rapport (dedicated time, prayer, thinking of the spirit, openness, practice: "like any venture in the beginning, it may not be perfect... but with practice and effort we will have a good working relationship"); the tools (pen and paper or a recording device); and the invitation principle ("There is no pressure to begin. There is merely an invitation... I do not wish to impinge upon my brother's free will"). A Celestial recruiting a new instrument on the record, beside Jesus designating channels (2019-01-30).
- NOTE (build, `portals`): a sitter asks whether the guest house needs a portal; "There is no need for a portal beloved soul... There is a great energy here." Portals are an assumed fixture in the circle's vocabulary here, and the reply gives the thread a boundary: rapport and communication do not require one.
- `portals` **[Objection-removed]** : clears the belief that communication requires a portal, voiced in the room by a sitter ("is the portal there as well as here?") and removed in direct answer: "There is no need for a portal beloved soul. But any place within these beautiful grounds are adequate." The thread's boundary: portals concentrate blessing; they do not gate contact. One exchange, but the objection was explicitly raised and completely cleared, which is the role's essence.
- NOTE (sighting, `incremental-journey`): "Every great thing that is of light and beauty takes time, takes effort, persistence and love", the incremental principle applied to the mission and its fruits rather than the soul's growth; witness-level.
- NOTE (build, `religious-error`): the writing collaboration this message initiates did materialize and later produced a book of Yogananda's messages. Strengthens the `religious-error` Testimony above: the announced repair project was carried out.

### 2019-04-07-af-augustine | Walking God's Path of Divine Love Will Bring You Upon A Different Road | 2019-04-07
- `mind-vs-soul` **[Elaboration]** : two facets. First, the control-precondition: the material mind "desires to know and to have all the information that it is able to receive before it allows such things to manifest through your being," and thereby blocks awareness of the soul's own gifts, which flow "not from the preparation of the mind, but the knowing of the soul." Second, the social dimension of the mind's resistance: the inclination "to walk in the world as others walk in the world... to not feel different," conformity as the mind's ally against the soul's awakening that sets a person apart.
- NOTE (witness, `soul-purpose-emergence`): the gifts hidden within, blocked from awareness by the mind, "will become self-evident and will grow and grow" as the soul awakens, which is the registry's own word for the step.
- NOTE (sighting, `incremental-journey`): "Day by day, moment by moment, these changes take place, these awakenings, so subtle yet so powerful." Witness-level.
- NOTE (sighting, `divine-love-mediumship`): what empowers Celestial contact with mortals "is the power of God's Love within your souls," the mechanism restated in a sentence.
- NOTE (sighting, `judgment-to-love`): "There is much judgment within the human condition that is directed towards those who are different."

### 2019-04-08-jw-yogananda | Realms of Light | 2019-04-08
- `death-transition` **[Elaboration]** : a plain statement of the reversal at the heart of the archive's view of death: "it is we who grieve. It is our loss but their gain." The departed live "in realms of light so far above this earth plane," progressing toward the Celestial Heavens; grief belongs to the living. Adds the continuing-bond teaching (they come when called and when thought of) tied to the scriptural "ask and you shall receive." Delivered through Rev. Jimbeau Walsh in the circle where Maureen Cardoso was simultaneously receiving the companion message.
- NOTE (context): a message from medium Rev. Jimbeau Walsh, received alongside 2019-04-08-mc-mary: the Yogananda message reflects on grief and the realms of light while, in the same room, Mary delivers the personal message from the departed. The two are reciprocally linked; see also 2019-04-01-jw-yogananda.

### 2019-04-08-mc-mary | A Message from Jeanne's Loving Mother upon Passing into the Spirit World | 2019-04-08
- `death-transition` **[Testimony]** : a lived enactment of the thread's doctrine rather than its teaching: three days after her passing, Kay Dunbar sends word back through Mary, full of humour and fire, grateful to her caregivers by name, tender to her daughters, already "in a beautiful place." Where the companion message, 2019-04-08-jw-yogananda, teaches that the departed progress in realms of light, this shows one doing exactly that, personality intact, the continuing bond made concrete ("Only think of me and I am there"; "when you miss her... blow a kiss").
- NOTE (context): Mary delivers on behalf of the newly passed Kay Dunbar, mother of Jeanne Fike (wife of medium Al Fike) and Judy Dunbar. Paired with 2019-04-08-jw-yogananda. Kay's "her dear Albert" refers to Al Fike, her son-in-law.

### 2023-02-10-af-augustine | The Impediments of Soul Awakening | 2023-02-10 [not publicly posted]
- `soul-expiation` **[Objection-removed]** : Augustine corrects a key misconception: "It is not as others have said that they must first cleanse all of these conditions before they are able to know their soul or receive the great gift of God's Love. Rather, the contrary is true." Prayer and longing draw in the Essence first, which then strengthens the soul to clear its conditions. Removes the worthiness/purify-first barrier that blocks the expiation process.
- `earth-conditions-and-prayer` **[Elaboration]** : Develops the thread whose Foundation is 2016-02-29-af-luke: humanity is desensitized from childhood and absorbs the world's conditions; one must come awake to these rather than absorb them, neutralize them with light, and the Earth itself is changing to force humanity awake. Shifts the emphasis from Luke's "weather-field that buffets" to the awakening/re-sensitization needed to meet it.
- `earth-changes` **[Elaboration]** : carries the thread's purpose claim, that the Earth itself is changing to force humanity awake. Moves the thread from what the changes are to what they are for.
- `mind-vs-soul` **[Elaboration]** : The surface/material mind is a barrier of control over the underlying feelings and the soul; awakening means penetrating that barrier to "clear the channel between the mind and the soul." Adds the desensitization/re-sensitization dimension to the mind-soul argument.
